            if not ST.knife_predict then return base end
            local tr=trackers[part]
            local vel=tr and tr.vel or part.AssemblyLinearVelocity
            if not validVector(vel) then return base end
            -- A standing target needs no lead; leave the toggle enabled.
            local live=part.AssemblyLinearVelocity
            if validVector(live) and Vector3.new(live.X,0,live.Z).Magnitude<0.75
                and math.abs(live.Y)<1 then return base end
            local latency=ping_sec()
            latency=finite(latency) and math.clamp(latency,0,1) or 0
            local scale=number(ST.knife_lead,100,0,300)/100
            local speed=number(ST.knife_speed,96,40,400)
            local air=number(ST.knife_air,35,0,120)/100
            local extra=number(ST.knife_offset,0,-80,220)/1000
            local stable=tr and tr.stable or 1
            local hum=part.Parent and part.Parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local airborne=hum and hum.FloorMaterial==Enum.Material.Air
            local point,travel=base,0
            for _=1,3 do
                local t=math.clamp((latency+travel)*scale+extra,0,1)
                local y=0
                if airborne and math.abs(vel.Y)<80 then
                    local tv=t*air
                    y=math.clamp(vel.Y*tv-0.5*workspace.Gravity*tv*tv,-1.8,1.8)
                end
                local offset=limit(Vector3.new(vel.X*t*stable,y,vel.Z*t*stable),60)
                point=base+offset
                travel=math.clamp((point-origin).Magnitude/speed,0,0.7)
            end
            return validVector(point) and point or base
        end
        SHF.resolveKnifeAim=function(origin)
            if espLifecycle.stopped or not ST.auto_knife_throw or getgenv().AUTOFARM_HOLD then return nil end
            local knife,root=localKnife()
            if not knife then return nil end
            local part=nearest(root)
            if not part then return nil end
            if not validVector(origin) then
                local hand=LP.Character:FindFirstChild("RightHand") or LP.Character:FindFirstChild("Right Arm")
                origin=hand and hand:IsA("BasePart") and hand.Position or root.Position
            end
            return CFrame.new(aimPoint(part,origin))
        end
        local function throwRemote()
            local knife=localKnife()
            local events=knife and knife:FindFirstChild("Events")
            local remote=events and events:FindFirstChild("KnifeThrown")
            if remote and remote:IsA("RemoteEvent") then return remote end
        end
        local function isThrow(remote)
            -- Object identity, not name matching: Shoot and all other remotes pass untouched.
            return remote~=nil and remote==throwRemote()
        end
        local function installThrowHook()
            if hook_state then return true end
            if type(hookfunction)~="function" then
                stats.status="hookfunction unavailable"
                return false
            end
            local state={active=true,busy=setmetatable({}, {__mode="k"})}
            local probe=Instance.new("RemoteEvent")
            local fire=probe.FireServer
            probe:Destroy()
            local function dispatch(previous,remote,...)
                local thread=coroutine.running()
                if state.busy[thread] or not state.active or espLifecycle.stopped
                    or not ST.auto_knife_throw or getgenv().AUTOFARM_HOLD then
                    return previous(remote,...)
                end
                local args=table.pack(...)
                local ok,target=pcall(function()
                    if not isThrow(remote) or typeof(args[1])~="CFrame"
                        or typeof(args[2])~="CFrame" then return nil end
                    return SHF.resolveKnifeAim(args[1].Position)
                end)
                if not ok then
                    stats.errors=stats.errors+1; stats.status=tostring(target)
                    return previous(remote,...)
                end
                if not target then return previous(remote,...) end
                -- Consume the original request. Send exactly one replacement through
                -- the unhooked function; preserve A, trailing arguments and nils.
                args[2]=target
                if pending_input then pending_input.seen=true end
                state.busy[thread]=true
                local result=table.pack(pcall(state.fireOriginal,remote,table.unpack(args,1,args.n)))
                state.busy[thread]=nil
                if not result[1] then
                    stats.errors=stats.errors+1; stats.status=tostring(result[2])
                    -- Never retry here: a retry could duplicate an already sent event.
                    error(result[2],0)
                end
                last_sent=os.clock()
                stats.redirected=stats.redirected+1
                stats.sent=stats.sent+1; stats.remote="KnifeThrown"; stats.status="redirected"
                return table.unpack(result,2,result.n)
            end
            local function fireWrapper(remote,...)
                return dispatch(state.fireOriginal,remote,...)
            end
            local wrap=type(newcclosure)=="function" and newcclosure(fireWrapper) or fireWrapper
            local ok,previous=pcall(hookfunction,fire,wrap)
            if not ok or type(previous)~="function" then
                state.active=false; stats.status="FireServer hook failed: "..tostring(previous)
                return false
            end
            state.fireOriginal=previous
            state.fireWrapper=wrap
            hook_state=state
            -- Some executors route colon calls through __namecall without visiting
            -- the hooked function. Both entry points use the same guarded sender.
            if type(hookmetamethod)=="function" and type(getnamecallmethod)=="function" then
                local function namecallWrapper(remote,...)
                    if getnamecallmethod()~="FireServer" then return state.nameOriginal(remote,...) end
                    return dispatch(state.nameOriginal,remote,...)
                end
                local nw=type(newcclosure)=="function" and newcclosure(namecallWrapper) or namecallWrapper
                local nok,np=pcall(hookmetamethod,game,"__namecall",nw)
                if nok and type(np)=="function" then state.nameOriginal=np; state.nameWrapper=nw end
            end
            stats.status="hook ready"
            return true
        end
        local function sendInput(down,mode,backend)
            if mode=="E" then
                if backend=="executor" then
                    if down then keypress(0x45) else keyrelease(0x45) end
                else vim:SendKeyEvent(down,Enum.KeyCode.E,false,game) end
            else
                if backend=="executor" then
                    if down then mouse2press() else mouse2release() end
                else
                    local mouse=UIS:GetMouseLocation()
                    vim:SendMouseButtonEvent(mouse.X,mouse.Y,1,down,game,0)
                end
            end
        end
        local function releaseInput()
            local held=held_input
            held_input=nil
            if held then pcall(sendInput,false,held.mode,held.backend) end
        end
        local function cancelInput()
            input_token=input_token+1
            releaseInput(); pending_input=nil
        end
        local function pressInput()
            local mode=ST.knife_input=="Right Mouse" and "Right Mouse" or "E"
            local native=mode=="E" and type(keypress)=="function" and type(keyrelease)=="function"
                or mode=="Right Mouse" and type(mouse2press)=="function" and type(mouse2release)=="function"
            local backend=native and "executor" or (vim and "vim" or nil)
            if not backend then stats.status="input unavailable; select Remote mode"; return false end
            local ok,err=pcall(sendInput,true,mode,backend)
            if not ok then
                pcall(sendInput,false,mode,backend)
                stats.errors=stats.errors+1; stats.status=tostring(err); return false
            end
            held_input={mode=mode,backend=backend}
            input_token=input_token+1
            local token=input_token
            task.delay(number(ST.knife_hold,0.15,0.05,0.5),function()
                if token==input_token then releaseInput() end
            end)
            return true
        end
        SHF.setKnifeThrowing=function(enabled)
            if not enabled then
                ST.auto_knife_throw=false; stats.status="off"
                cancelInput(); table.clear(trackers)
                return true
            end
            if espLifecycle.stopped or not installThrowHook() then
                ST.auto_knife_throw=false
                if notify then notify("Knife",stats.status) end
                return false
            end
            ST.auto_knife_throw=true; stats.status="waiting for equipped knife"
            last_attempt=-math.huge
            return true
        end
        SHF.knifeDiagnostics=function()
            if notify then
                notify("Knife",string.format("function hook: %s | namecall: %s | replaced: %d | sent: %d | errors: %d | %s",
                    hook_state and "on" or "off",hook_state and hook_state.nameOriginal and "on" or "off",
                    stats.redirected,stats.sent,stats.errors,stats.status))
            end
        end
        local function step()
            if espLifecycle.stopped or not ST.auto_knife_throw then
                if held_input or pending_input then cancelInput() end
                return
            end
            local now=os.clock()
            if now>=next_roles then
                next_roles=now+0.5
                local modules=ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Modules")
                local module=modules and modules:FindFirstChild("CurrentRoundClient")
                if not role_data and module then
                    local ok,data=pcall(require,module)
                    if ok and type(data)=="table" then role_data=data end
                end
            end
            if not installThrowHook() then return end
            local knife,root=localKnife()
            if getgenv().AUTOFARM_HOLD or not knife then
                cancelInput(); stats.status="waiting for equipped knife"; return
            end
            updateTrackers(now)
            if pending_input and now>=pending_input.deadline then
                if not pending_input.seen then stats.status="no game throw event; try Remote mode" end
                pending_input=nil
            end
            if ST.knife_auto_mode~="Game Input" and (held_input or pending_input) then cancelInput() end
            if pending_input or held_input or UIS:GetFocusedTextBox() then return end
            if now-math.max(last_attempt,last_sent)<number(ST.knife_interval,1,0.25,3) then return end
            local remote=throwRemote()
            if not remote then stats.status="Knife.Events.KnifeThrown not found"; return end
            local char=LP.Character
            local hand=char:FindFirstChild("RightHand") or char:FindFirstChild("Right Arm")
            local origin=hand and hand:IsA("BasePart") and hand.CFrame or root.CFrame
            local target=SHF.resolveKnifeAim(origin.Position)
            if not target then stats.status="no live target in range"; return end
            last_attempt=now; stats.attempts=stats.attempts+1
            if ST.knife_auto_mode=="Game Input" then
                pending_input={seen=false,deadline=now+math.max(0.8,number(ST.knife_hold,0.15,0.05,0.5)+0.5)}
                if not pressInput() then pending_input=nil end
            else
                -- Direct automatic throw, independent of input and gun hooks.
                -- Native game input remains available above for local tool animations.
                local thread=coroutine.running()
                hook_state.busy[thread]=true
                local ok,err=pcall(hook_state.fireOriginal,remote,origin,target)
                hook_state.busy[thread]=nil
                if ok then
                    last_sent=now; stats.sent=stats.sent+1
                    stats.remote="KnifeThrown"; stats.status="auto remote sent (server acceptance unverified)"
                else stats.errors=stats.errors+1; stats.status=tostring(err) end
            end
        end
        -- Dedicated heartbeat: errors in the old combat loop cannot stop throwing.
        table.insert(espLifecycle.connections,RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            local ok,err=pcall(step)
            if not ok then
                cancelInput(); stats.errors=stats.errors+1; stats.status=tostring(err)
            end
        end))
        knife_silent_step=nil
        table.insert(espLifecycle.connections,LP.CharacterRemoving:Connect(function()
            cancelInput(); table.clear(trackers)
        end))
        table.insert(espLifecycle.connections,LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
            cancelInput(); table.clear(trackers); last_attempt=-math.huge
        end))
        table.insert(espLifecycle.cleanups,function()
            SHF.setKnifeThrowing(false)
            -- Leave inert pass-throughs rather than overwrite a later hook chain.
            if hook_state then hook_state.active=false end
        end)

    end
end
initKnifeThrow()

-- ============================================================
-- INDEPENDENT AIM LOCK + IMAGE ROLE ARROWS
-- ============================================================
local function initAimLockAndArrows()
    local roundData=nil
    local roster={}
    local nextRefresh=0
    local arrows={}
    local arrowsGui=nil
    local arrowImage="rbxassetid://118079146241188"
    local cameraBinding="DaDa_AimLock_"..tostring(LP.UserId)
    local white=Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
    local red=Color3.fromRGB(255,60,60)
    local blue=Color3.fromRGB(0,153,255)
    local function refreshRoster()
        if not roundData then
            local modules=ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Modules")
            local module=modules and modules:FindFirstChild("CurrentRoundClient")
            if module then
                local ok,data=pcall(require,module)
                if ok and type(data)=="table" then roundData=data end
            end
        end
        local fresh={}
        local data=roundData and roundData.PlayerData
        for _,player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if player~=LP then
                local info=type(data)=="table" and data[player.Name]
                local role=type(info)=="table" and info.Role or "Innocent"
                local char=player.Character
                local bag=player:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
                if holds(char,"Knife") or holds(bag,"Knife") then role="Murderer"
                elseif role~="Murderer" and (holds(char,"Gun") or holds(bag,"Gun")) then role="Sheriff" end
                fresh[#fresh+1]={player=player,role=role,dead=type(info)=="table" and info.Dead==true}
            end
        end
        roster=fresh
    end
    local function alive(entry)
        if entry.dead or entry.player.Parent~=Players then return nil end
        local char=entry.player.Character
        local hum=char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local part=char and (char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or char:FindFirstChild("Torso"))
        if hum and hum.Health>0 and part and part:IsA("BasePart") then return part end
    end
    local function clearPlayer(player)
        local image=arrows[player]
        if image then image:Destroy(); arrows[player]=nil end
    end
    local function clearAll()
        local keys={}
        for player in pairs(arrows) do keys[#keys+1]=player end
        for _,player in ipairs(keys) do clearPlayer(player) end
        if arrowsGui then arrowsGui.Enabled=false end
    end
    local function ensureGui()
        if arrowsGui and arrowsGui.Parent then return arrowsGui end
        clearAll()
        if arrowsGui then arrowsGui:Destroy(); arrowsGui=nil end
        local screen=Instance.new("ScreenGui")
        screen.Name="DaDa_RoleArrows"
        screen.IgnoreGuiInset=true
        screen.ResetOnSpawn=false
        screen.DisplayOrder=80
        screen.ZIndexBehavior=Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
        local parents={}
        if type(gethui)=="function" then
            local ok,parent=pcall(gethui)
            if ok and parent then parents[#parents+1]=parent end
        end
        if CoreGui then parents[#parents+1]=CoreGui end
        local playerGui=LP:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
        if playerGui then parents[#parents+1]=playerGui end
        local attached=false
        for _,parent in ipairs(parents) do
            local ok=pcall(function() screen.Parent=parent end)
            if ok and screen.Parent then attached=true; break end
        end
        if not attached then screen:Destroy(); error("Arrows GUI parent unavailable") end
        arrowsGui=screen
        return screen
    end
    local function makeArrow(player,parent)
        local image=arrows[player]
        if image and image.Parent==parent then return image end
        if image then image:Destroy() end
        image=Instance.new("ImageLabel")
        image.Name="Arrow_"..tostring(player.UserId)
        image.BackgroundTransparency=1
        image.AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5,0.5)
        image.Image=arrowImage
        image.ImageTransparency=0
        image.ScaleType=Enum.ScaleType.Fit
        image.Active=false
        image.ZIndex=1
        image.Visible=false
        image.Parent=parent
        arrows[player]=image
        return image
    end
    local function arrowsStep(camera,dt)
        if not ST.arrows then
            if next(arrows) then clearAll() end
            if arrowsGui then arrowsGui.Enabled=false end
            return
        end
        local viewport=camera.ViewportSize
        if viewport.X<2 or viewport.Y<2 then
            if arrowsGui then arrowsGui.Enabled=false end
            return
        end
        local parent=ensureGui()
        parent.Enabled=true
        local center=viewport*0.5
        local size=math.clamp(tonumber(ST.arrows_size) or 26,12,64)
        -- Reserve half the image diagonal so rotation stays inside the viewport.
        local maxRadius=math.max(0,math.min(viewport.X,viewport.Y)*0.5-size*0.7072-6)
        local radius=math.clamp(tonumber(ST.arrows_radius) or 160,0,maxRadius)
        local hideOnScreen=ST.arrows_mode=="Hide On Screen"
        local fade=1-math.exp(-18*math.clamp(tonumber(dt) or 1/60,0,0.1))
        local used={}
        for _,entry in ipairs(roster) do
            local part=alive(entry)
            if part then
                local relative=camera.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(part.Position)
                -- Camera forward is -Z; screen up is -Y. Do not negate Z:
                -- doing so swaps front/back and mirrors the arrow vertically.
                local bearing=Vector2.new(relative.X,relative.Z)
                local dir=bearing.Magnitude>0.001 and bearing.Unit or Vector2.new(0,-1)
                local anchor=center+dir*radius
                local color=entry.role=="Murderer" and red
                    or ((entry.role=="Sheriff" or entry.role=="Hero") and blue or white)
                local image=makeArrow(entry.player,parent)
                image.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(size,size)
                image.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(anchor.X,anchor.Y)
                -- The source arrow is treated as pointing up at zero rotation.
                image.Rotation=math.deg(math.atan2(dir.Y,dir.X))+90
                image.ImageColor3=color
                if hideOnScreen then
                    local screenPoint,onScreen=camera:WorldToViewportPoint(part.Position)
                    local targetTransparency=(onScreen and screenPoint.Z>0) and 1 or 0
                    local transparency=image.ImageTransparency
                    transparency=transparency+(targetTransparency-transparency)*fade
                    if math.abs(transparency-targetTransparency)<0.005 then
                        transparency=targetTransparency
                    end
                    image.ImageTransparency=math.clamp(transparency,0,1)
                else
                    -- Switching back to Always must also restore faded arrows.
                    image.ImageTransparency=0
                end
                image.Visible=image.ImageTransparency<1
                used[entry.player]=true
            end
        end
        local stale={}
        for player in pairs(arrows) do if not used[player] then stale[#stale+1]=player end end
        for _,player in ipairs(stale) do clearPlayer(player) end
    end
    local function lockStep(camera)
        if not ST.aim_lock or getgenv().AUTOFARM_HOLD or UIS:GetFocusedTextBox() then return end
        local char=LP.Character
        local hum=char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local root=char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not root or not hum or hum.Health<=0 then return end
        local selected=nil
        local distance=math.huge
        for _,entry in ipairs(roster) do
            local role=entry.role
            local filter=ST.aim_lock_target
            if filter=="Nearest" or (filter=="Sheriff" and (role=="Sheriff" or role=="Hero"))
                or (filter=="Murder" and role=="Murderer") then
                local part=alive(entry)
                if part then
                    local d=(part.Position-root.Position).Magnitude
                    if d<distance then distance=d; selected=part end
                end
            end
        end
        if not selected then return end
        local point=selected.Position
        if ST.aim_lock_predict then
            local vel=selected.AssemblyLinearVelocity
            local horizontal=Vector3.new(vel.X,0,vel.Z)
            if horizontal.Magnitude>=0.75 then
                if horizontal.Magnitude>170 then horizontal=horizontal.Unit*170 end
                point=point+horizontal*math.clamp(tonumber(ST.aim_lock_lead) or 0.12,0,0.5)
            end
        end
        local origin=camera.CFrame.Position
        if (point-origin).Magnitude>0.01 then camera.CFrame=CFrame.lookAt(origin,point) end
    end
    table.insert(espLifecycle.connections,RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if espLifecycle.stopped or not (ST.aim_lock or ST.arrows) then return end
        local now=os.clock()
        if now>=nextRefresh then
            nextRefresh=now+0.2
            local ok,err=pcall(refreshRoster)
            if not ok then SHF.aim_arrows_error=tostring(err) end
        end
    end))
    -- Runs after the default camera controller so it cannot overwrite the lock.
    RunService:BindToRenderStep(cameraBinding,Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value+1,function(dt)
        if espLifecycle.stopped then return end
        local camera=workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not camera then return end
        local ok,err=pcall(lockStep,camera)
        if not ok then SHF.aim_lock_error=tostring(err) end
        ok,err=pcall(arrowsStep,camera,dt)
        if not ok then
            SHF.arrows_error=tostring(err); ST.arrows=false; clearAll()
            if notify then notify("Arrows",tostring(err)) end
        end
    end)
    table.insert(espLifecycle.connections,Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(clearPlayer))
    table.insert(espLifecycle.cleanups,function()
        ST.aim_lock=false; ST.arrows=false
        RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(cameraBinding)
        clearAll(); table.clear(roster)
        if arrowsGui then arrowsGui:Destroy(); arrowsGui=nil end
    end)
end
initAimLockAndArrows()


-- ============================================================
-- INIT 9: FARM + FLING + ANTIFLING + KILL AURA
-- ============================================================
local function initFarmFling()
    local was_down=false; local down_ref_y=nil; local last_touch=0
    local farm_target=nil; local collected_ids={}; local collected_count=0
    local coins_done=false; local saw_coins=false
    getgenv().AUTOFARM_HOLD=false
    farm_hrp=function() local c=LP.Character; return c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") end
    local function get_round_data() local m=get_round(); return m and m.PlayerData end
    can_farm=function()
        local char=LP.Character; local hum=char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hum or hum.Health<=0 then return false end
        local d=get_round_data()
        if type(d)=="table" then local me=d[LP.Name]; if not me or not me.Role or me.Dead then return false end end
        return true
    end
    coin_bags_full=function()
        local pg=LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui"); local main=pg and pg:FindFirstChild("MainGUI"); local gg=main and main:FindFirstChild("Game")
        local bags=gg and gg:FindFirstChild("CoinBags"); local container=bags and bags:FindFirstChild("Container")
        if not container then return false end
        local any=false
        for _,v in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
            if v:IsA("Frame") and v.Visible then
                any=true
                local full=v:FindFirstChild("Full")
                if not (full and full.Visible) then return false end
            end
        end
        return any
    end
    local function coin_available(v) return v and v.Parent and v:IsA("BasePart") and not v:GetAttribute("Collected") and not v:GetAttribute("Delete") end
    local function nearest_coin(pos,list)
        local best,bd=nil,math.huge
        for _,v in ipairs(list) do
            local d=(v.Position-pos).Magnitude
            if d<bd then bd=d; best=v end
        end
        return best
    end
    local function set_farm_noclip(on)
        local char=LP.Character; local hum=char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if on then
            if not char then return end
            if hum then pcall(function() hum.PlatformStand=true end) end
            for _,p in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
                if p:IsA("BasePart") and p.CanCollide then
                    if nc_cache[p]==nil then nc_cache[p]=p.CanCollide end
                    p.CanCollide=false
                end
            end
        else
            if hum then pcall(function() hum.PlatformStand=false end) end
            for p,v in pairs(nc_cache) do if p and p.Parent then pcall(function() p.CanCollide=v end) end end
            nc_cache={}
        end
    end
    local up_params=RaycastParams.new()
    up_params.FilterType=Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
    up_params.IgnoreWater=true
    local function return_to_surface()
        local hrp=farm_hrp(); if not hrp then return end
        local origin=hrp.Position
        up_params.FilterDescendantsInstances={LP.Character}
        local res=workspace:Raycast(origin,Vector3.new(0,400,0),up_params)
        local y=res and (res.Position.Y+5) or (down_ref_y and down_ref_y+5 or nil)
        if not y then return end
        pcall(function()
            hrp.CFrame=CFrame.new(origin.X,y,origin.Z)
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity=Vector3.zero
            hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity=Vector3.zero
        end)
    end
    farm_release=function() if was_down then was_down=false; return_to_surface() end; set_farm_noclip(false) end
    local murder_hrp_cache,murder_hrp_t=nil,0
    local function murderer_hrp()
        local now=os.clock()
        if now-murder_hrp_t<0.25 then return murder_hrp_cache end
        murder_hrp_t=now; murder_hrp_cache=nil
        local d=get_round_data()
        if type(d)=="table" then
            local me=d[LP.Name]
            if me and me.Role=="Murderer" then return nil end
            for name,info in pairs(d) do
                if type(info)=="table" and info.Role=="Murderer" and not info.Dead and name~=LP.Name then
                    local pl=Players:FindFirstChild(name)
                    local char=pl and pl.Character
                    local h=char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    local hum=char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    if h and (not hum or hum.Health>0) then murder_hrp_cache=h end
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        return murder_hrp_cache
    end
    local function flat_dist(a,b) return Vector3.new(a.X-b.X,0,a.Z-b.Z).Magnitude end
    local function pick_coin(pos,list,mpos)
        if not (farm_cfg.avoid_murder and mpos) then return nearest_coin(pos,list) end
        local safe,sd=nil,math.huge
        local far,fd=nil,-1
        for _,v in ipairs(list) do
            local md=flat_dist(v.Position,mpos)
            if md>fd then fd=md; far=v end
            if md>=farm_cfg.avoid_dist then
                local d=(v.Position-pos).Magnitude
                if d<sd then sd=d; safe=v end
            end
        end
        if safe then return safe end
        if far and fd>=farm_cfg.avoid_dist*0.6 then return far end
        return nil
    end
    local function coin_ok_now(v,mpos)
        if not coin_available(v) then return false end
        if farm_cfg.avoid_murder and mpos and flat_dist(v.Position,mpos)<farm_cfg.avoid_dist*0.6 then return false end
        return true
    end
    local function avoid_steer(cur,dest,mpos)
        if not (farm_cfg.avoid_murder and mpos) then return dest end
        local dm=flat_dist(cur,mpos)
        if dm>=farm_cfg.avoid_dist then return dest end
        local away=Vector3.new(cur.X-mpos.X,0,cur.Z-mpos.Z)
        if away.Magnitude<0.1 then away=Vector3.new(1,0,0) end
        away=away.Unit
        local want=Vector3.new(dest.X-cur.X,0,dest.Z-cur.Z)
        local mag=want.Magnitude
        if mag<0.1 then return dest end
        local weight=1+(1-dm/farm_cfg.avoid_dist)*2
        local blend=(want.Unit+away*weight)
        if blend.Magnitude<0.1 then blend=away else blend=blend.Unit end
        local np=cur+blend*mag
        return Vector3.new(np.X,dest.Y,np.Z)
    end
    local function touch_targets(coin)
        local list,seen={},{}
        local function add(p)
            if p and not seen[p] and p:IsA("BasePart") and p:FindFirstChildOfClass("TouchTransmitter") then
                seen[p]=true; list[#list+1]=p
            end
        end
        add(coin)
        for _,v in ipairs(coin:GetChildren()) do add(v) end
        local par=coin.Parent
        if par then
            if par:IsA("BasePart") then add(par) end
            for _,v in ipairs(par:GetChildren()) do add(v) end
        end
        if #list==0 then list[1]=coin end
        return list
    end
    local function fire_touch(coin)
        if type(firetouchinterest)~="function" then return end
        if not coin or not coin.Parent then return end
        local now=os.clock()
        if now-last_touch<0.05 then return end
        last_touch=now
        local hrp=farm_hrp(); if not hrp then return end
        for _,p in ipairs(touch_targets(coin)) do
            pcall(firetouchinterest,hrp,p,0)
            pcall(firetouchinterest,hrp,p,1)
        end
    end
    local function farm_move(hrp,dest,dt)
        local dir=dest-hrp.Position
        local dist=dir.Magnitude
        local np=dest
        if dist>0.1 then np=hrp.Position+dir.Unit*math.min(farm_cfg.speed*dt,dist) end
        local cf=CFrame.new(np)
        if farm_cfg.mode=="Down" then cf=cf*CFrame.Angles(-math.pi*0.5,0,0); was_down=true end
        pcall(function()
            hrp.CFrame=cf
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity=Vector3.zero
            hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity=Vector3.zero
        end)
    end
    farm_step=function(_,dt)
        if not farm_cfg.on then return end
        if not can_farm() then
            farm_target=nil
            was_down=false
            set_farm_noclip(false)
            getgenv().AUTOFARM_HOLD=false
            return
        end
        local hrp=farm_hrp(); if not hrp then return end
        local list={}
        for _,v in ipairs(CollectionService:GetTagged("CoinVisual")) do
            if v and v.Parent and v:IsA("BasePart") then
                if v:GetAttribute("Collected") then
                    local id=v:GetAttribute("CoinID")
                    if id and not collected_ids[id] then collected_ids[id]=true; collected_count=collected_count+1 end
                elseif not v:GetAttribute("Delete") then
                    list[#list+1]=v
                end
            end
        end
        local function finish()
            farm_target=nil; farm_release()
            if not coins_done then
                coins_done=true
                getgenv().AUTOFARM_HOLD=false
                if farm_cfg.auto_reset then
                    local hum=LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    if hum then pcall(function() hum.Health=0 end) end
                end
            end
        end
        if saw_coins and coin_bags_full() then finish() return end
        if #list>0 then
            saw_coins=true
            if coins_done then coins_done=false end
            local mhrp=farm_cfg.avoid_murder and murderer_hrp() or nil
            local mpos=mhrp and mhrp.Position or nil
            if not coin_ok_now(farm_target,mpos) then farm_target=pick_coin(hrp.Position,list,mpos) end
            if farm_target then
                set_farm_noclip(true)
                local cpos=farm_target.Position
                down_ref_y=cpos.Y
                local dest=cpos
                if farm_cfg.mode=="Down" then
                    local xz=flat_dist(hrp.Position,cpos)
                    local safe_rise=(not mpos) or flat_dist(hrp.Position,mpos)>farm_cfg.rise_safe_dist
                    if xz<=4 and safe_rise then dest=cpos; fire_touch(farm_target)
                    else dest=Vector3.new(cpos.X,cpos.Y-farm_cfg.down_depth,cpos.Z) end
                elseif (cpos-hrp.Position).Magnitude<=6 then
                    fire_touch(farm_target)
                end
                dest=avoid_steer(hrp.Position,dest,mpos)
                farm_move(hrp,dest,dt)
            elseif mpos then
                set_farm_noclip(true)
                local away=Vector3.new(hrp.Position.X-mpos.X,0,hrp.Position.Z-mpos.Z)
                if away.Magnitude<0.1 then away=Vector3.new(1,0,0) end
                away=away.Unit
                local y=hrp.Position.Y
                if farm_cfg.mode=="Down" and down_ref_y then y=down_ref_y-farm_cfg.down_depth end
                farm_move(hrp,hrp.Position+away*40+Vector3.new(0,y-hrp.Position.Y,0),dt)
            end
        else
            -- No visible coin targets remain. Some rounds remove CoinVisual instances
            -- before setting the Collected attribute, so do not require collected_count>0.
            farm_target=nil
            farm_release()
            if saw_coins and not coins_done then
                finish()
            end
        end
    end
    RunService.Stepped:Connect(farm_step)

    do
    -- Anti-fling.
    -- Collision is disabled in Stepped (right before the physics step). The old
    -- Heartbeat-only version ran after physics, so a freshly spawned/rebuilt
    -- character or a re-enabled part could collide for one step and fling you.
    local FLING_MAX_VEL=400; local FLING_MAX_ANG=40; local FLING_HOLD=0.35; local FLING_SAFE_VEL=120
    local OTHER_MAX_VEL=120; local OTHER_MAX_ANG=20
    local fling_safe_cf=nil; local fling_hold_until=0
    local af_chars={}   -- character -> {parts={}, conns={}}
    local af_applied=false
    local function af_unwatch(char)
        local st=af_chars[char]; if not st then return end
        for _,c in ipairs(st.conns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
        af_chars[char]=nil
    end
    local function af_add(st,part)
        if not part:IsA("BasePart") then return end
        if af_cache[part]==nil then af_cache[part]=part.CanCollide end
        st.parts[part]=true
        if part.CanCollide then part.CanCollide=false end
    end
    local function af_watch(char)
        local st=af_chars[char]; if st then return st end
        st={parts={},conns={}}; af_chars[char]=st
        for _,d in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do af_add(st,d) end
        st.conns[#st.conns+1]=char.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
            if ST.antifling and af_applied then af_add(st,d) end
        end)
        st.conns[#st.conns+1]=char.DescendantRemoving:Connect(function(d) st.parts[d]=nil end)
        return st
    end
    local function af_restore()
        for char in pairs(af_chars) do af_unwatch(char) end
        for p,v in pairs(af_cache) do if p.Parent then pcall(function() p.CanCollide=v end) end end
        af_cache={}; af_applied=false
    end
    local function af_self_check()
        local hrp=get_hrp()
        if not hrp or not hrp.Parent then fling_safe_cf=nil; return end
        local lin=hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        local ang=hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity
        local maxLin=ST.fly and math.huge or FLING_MAX_VEL
        local spike=lin.Magnitude>maxLin or ang.Magnitude>FLING_MAX_ANG
        local now=os.clock()
        if spike then fling_hold_until=now+FLING_HOLD end
        if spike or now<fling_hold_until then
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity=Vector3.zero
            hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity=Vector3.zero
            if fling_safe_cf and (hrp.Position-fling_safe_cf.Position).Magnitude>6 then
                hrp.CFrame=fling_safe_cf
            end
        elseif lin.Magnitude<FLING_SAFE_VEL and ang.Magnitude<FLING_MAX_ANG*0.5 then
            fling_safe_cf=hrp.CFrame
        end
    end
    local function af_pre_physics()
        if not ST.antifling then if af_applied or next(af_cache) then af_restore() end; return end
        -- Own fling needs collisions with the target; step aside while it runs.
        if (getgenv().FLING_ACTIVE or 0)>0 then if af_applied then af_restore() end; return end
        af_applied=true
        for _,p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            local char=p~=LP and p.Character
            if char and char.Parent then
                local st=af_watch(char)
                for part in pairs(st.parts) do
                    if part.Parent then
                        if part.CanCollide then part.CanCollide=false end
                        -- Kill replicated spin/velocity locally so a flinger's
                        -- parts are not extrapolated into us between packets.
                        if part.AssemblyAngularVelocity.Magnitude>OTHER_MAX_ANG then
                            part.AssemblyAngularVelocity=Vector3.zero
                        end
                        if part.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude>OTHER_MAX_VEL then
                            part.AssemblyLinearVelocity=Vector3.zero
                        end
                    else
                        st.parts[part]=nil
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        for char in pairs(af_chars) do
            if not char.Parent then af_unwatch(char) end
        end
        af_self_check()
    end
    antifling_conn=RunService.Stepped:Connect(function() pcall(af_pre_physics) end)
    -- Post-physics pass (called from the Heartbeat main loop): catch whatever
    -- still got through before the frame is rendered.
    antifling_step=function()
        if not ST.antifling or (getgenv().FLING_ACTIVE or 0)>0 then return end
        af_self_check()
    end
    end

    -- Shared SHubFling movement from Murder Mystery 2, used by both fling entry points.
    -- Keep one owner of the character and always restore it, including on cancellation.
    SHF.runFling=function(tp,options)
        options=options or {}
        if espLifecycle.stopped or SHF._flingRun or not tp or tp==LP then return false end
        local char=LP.Character
        local hrp=char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local hum=char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local tc=tp.Character
        local th=tc and tc:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local accessory=tc and tc:FindFirstChildOfClass("Accessory")
        local handle=accessory and accessory:FindFirstChild("Handle")
        local head=tc and tc:FindFirstChild("Head")
        local targetPart=(th and th.RootPart) or head or handle
        if not hrp or not hum or hum.Health<=0 or not th or th.Health<=0 or th.Sit then return false end
        if not targetPart or not targetPart:IsA("BasePart") then return false end

        local oldPos=hrp.CFrame
        local rootToPivot=oldPos:ToObjectSpace(char:GetPivot())
        local seatedEnabled=hum:GetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated)
        local camera=workspace.CurrentCamera
        local oldSubject=camera and camera.CameraSubject
        local cameraTarget=head or handle or th
        local bodyVelocity
        local run={source=options.source,cancelled=false,finished=false}
        SHF._flingRun=run
        ST.fling_active=true
        getgenv().FLING_ACTIVE=(getgenv().FLING_ACTIVE or 0)+1

        local function restorePosition()
            if LP.Character~=char or not hrp.Parent or not char.Parent then return end
            char:PivotTo(oldPos*rootToPivot)
            for _,part in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
                if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                    part.AssemblyLinearVelocity=Vector3.zero
                    part.AssemblyAngularVelocity=Vector3.zero
                end
            end
            if hum.Parent and hum.Health>0 then hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp) end
        end
        run.finish=function()
            if run.finished then return end
            run.finished=true
            -- Each restore is isolated: one destroyed instance cannot skip the others.
            if bodyVelocity then pcall(function() bodyVelocity:Destroy() end) end
            pcall(function() hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated,seatedEnabled) end)
            pcall(restorePosition)
            pcall(function()
                if camera and workspace.CurrentCamera==camera then
                    local currentHum=LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    camera.CameraSubject=(oldSubject and oldSubject.Parent and oldSubject) or currentHum
                end
            end)
            getgenv().FLING_ACTIVE=math.max(0,(getgenv().FLING_ACTIVE or 1)-1)
            if SHF._flingRun==run then
                SHF._flingRun=nil
                ST.fling_active=false
            end
        end
        local function canContinue()
            return not run.cancelled and not run.finished and not espLifecycle.stopped
                and LP.Character==char and char.Parent and hrp.Parent and hum.Parent and hum.Health>0
                and tp.Parent==Players and tp.Character==tc and tc.Parent
                and th.Parent and th.Health>0 and not th.Sit and targetPart.Parent
                and (not options.isEnabled or options.isEnabled())
        end
        local ok,err=pcall(function()
            if camera then camera.CameraSubject=cameraTarget end
            bodyVelocity=Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
            bodyVelocity.Name="DaDaSHubFlingVelocity"
            bodyVelocity.Velocity=Vector3.new(9e8,9e8,9e8)
            bodyVelocity.MaxForce=Vector3.new(math.huge,math.huge,math.huge)
            bodyVelocity.Parent=hrp
            hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated,false)

            local duration=math.clamp(tonumber(options.duration) or 2.5,0.1,10)
            local deadline=os.clock()+duration
            local angle=0
            local offsets={
                CFrame.new(0,1.5,0),
                CFrame.new(0,-1.5,0),
                CFrame.new(2.25,1.5,-2.25),
                CFrame.new(-2.25,-1.5,2.25),
            }
            while canContinue() and os.clock()<deadline do
                if not options.bypassVelocity and targetPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude>500 then break end
                angle=angle+100
                for _,offset in ipairs(offsets) do
                    if not canContinue() or os.clock()>=deadline then break end
                    if not options.bypassVelocity and targetPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude>500 then break end
                    local cf=CFrame.new(targetPart.Position)*(offset+th.MoveDirection)*CFrame.Angles(math.rad(angle),0,0)
                    char:PivotTo(cf*rootToPivot)
                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity=Vector3.new(9e7,9e8,9e7)
                    hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity=Vector3.new(9e8,9e8,9e8)
                    task.wait()
                end
            end
            -- Remove force first, then settle at the saved position before releasing ownership.
            if bodyVelocity then bodyVelocity:Destroy(); bodyVelocity=nil end
            for _=1,3 do
                if run.finished or espLifecycle.stopped or LP.Character~=char or not hrp.Parent then break end
                if options.isEnabled and not options.isEnabled() then break end
                restorePosition()
                task.wait()
            end
        end)
        run.finish()
        if not ok then warn("[Fling] Error:",err) end
        return ok
    end
    SHF.cancelFling=function(source)
        local run=SHF._flingRun
        if run and (not source or run.source==source) then
            run.cancelled=true
            run.finish()
        end
    end
    table.insert(espLifecycle.cleanups,function() SHF.cancelFling() end)
    table.insert(espLifecycle.connections,LP.CharacterRemoving:Connect(function() SHF.cancelFling() end))

    local function get_knife()
        local char=LP.Character
        if char then local k=char:FindFirstChild("Knife") if k then return k,true end end
        local bp=LP:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
        if bp then local k=bp:FindFirstChild("Knife") if k then return k,false end end
        return nil,false
    end
    local function ensure_knife()
        local k,eq=get_knife(); if not k then return nil end
        if not eq then
            local hum=LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if hum then pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(k) end) end
            return nil
        end
        return k
    end
    local function knife_stab(k)
        local ev=k:FindFirstChild("Events")
        local s=ev and ev:FindFirstChild("KnifeStabbed")
        if s then pcall(function() s:FireServer() end) end
    end
    local function knife_touch(k,part)
        local ev=k:FindFirstChild("Events")
        local t=ev and ev:FindFirstChild("HandleTouched")
        if t then pcall(function() t:FireServer(part) end) end
    end
    local last_ka=0
    killaura_step=function()
        if not (ST.killaura or ST.autoknife) then return end
        if my_role()~="Murderer" then return end
        if os.clock()-last_ka<0.05 then return end
        local k=ensure_knife(); if not k then return end
        local my_hrp=get_hrp(); if not my_hrp then return end
        local victims={}
        for _,p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if p~=LP and p.Character then
                local hum=p.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                local part=p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or p.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
                if hum and hum.Health>0 and part then
                    if ST.autoknife then victims[#victims+1]=part
                    elseif ST.killaura and (part.Position-my_hrp.Position).Magnitude<=ST.killaura_dist then
                        victims[#victims+1]=part
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        if #victims>0 then
            knife_stab(k)
            for _,part in ipairs(victims) do knife_touch(k,part) end
            last_ka=os.clock()
        end
    end
end
initFarmFling()

-- ============================================================
-- INIT 9.5: FLING (fixed)
-- ============================================================
local function initFlingMini()
    ST.fling_mini = ST.fling_mini or {
        on = false,
        mode = "murderer",
        target = nil,
        bypass_vel = false,
        delay = 0.15,
        duration = 2.5,
    }
    ST.fling_active = false
    ST.fling_killer_name = ST.fling_killer_name or nil

    -- === ХЕЛПЕРЫ ===
    local function hasKnife(plr)
        local ch = plr and plr.Character
        local bp = plr and plr:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
        if ch and ch:FindFirstChild("Knife") then return true end
        if bp and bp:FindFirstChild("Knife") then return true end
        return false
    end
    local function hasGun(plr)
        local ch = plr and plr.Character
        local bp = plr and plr:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
        if ch and ch:FindFirstChild("Gun") then return true end
        if bp and bp:FindFirstChild("Gun") then return true end
        return false
    end

    -- === ПОИСК ЦЕЛИ ===
    local function findTarget()
        local mode = ST.fling_mini.mode
        if mode == "target" and ST.fling_mini.target then
            local p = Players:FindFirstChild(ST.fling_mini.target)
            if p and p ~= LP and p.Character then return p end
            return nil
        end
        if mode == "killer" and ST.fling_killer_name then
            local p = Players:FindFirstChild(ST.fling_killer_name)
            if p and p ~= LP and p.Character then return p end
            return nil
        end
        local best, bestScore = nil, math.huge
        local myRoot = get_hrp()
        local origin = myRoot and myRoot.Position or Vector3.zero
        for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if plr ~= LP and plr.Character then
                local hum = plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                local root = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if hum and hum.Health > 0 and root then
                    local ok = false
                    if mode == "murderer" then ok = hasKnife(plr)
                    elseif mode == "sheriff" then ok = hasGun(plr) and not hasKnife(plr)
                    elseif mode == "all" then ok = true end
                    if ok then
                        local d = (root.Position - origin).Magnitude
                        local score = d
                        if mode == "murderer" and hasKnife(plr) then score = score - 1000 end
                        if score < bestScore then
                            bestScore = score
                            best = plr
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return best
    end

    -- One activation, one shared Duration budget (including target reacquisition).
    local activeSession=nil
    local function durationSeconds()
        local value=tonumber(ST.fling_mini.duration) or 2.5
        if value~=value then value=2.5 end
        return math.clamp(value,1,5)
    end
    local function sessionRemaining(session)
        return math.max(0,session.startedAt+durationSeconds()-os.clock())
    end
    local function sessionEnabled(session)
        return activeSession==session and ST.fling_mini.on and not espLifecycle.stopped
            and sessionRemaining(session)>0
    end
    local function mini_fling(plr,session)
        if session and not sessionEnabled(session) then return false end
        return SHF.runFling(plr,{
            source="mini",
            duration=session and sessionRemaining(session) or durationSeconds(),
            bypassVelocity=ST.fling_mini.bypass_vel,
            isEnabled=function()
                if session then return sessionEnabled(session) end
                return ST.fling_mini.on and not espLifecycle.stopped
            end,
        })
    end
    local function stopSession()
        activeSession=nil
        ST.fling_mini.on=false
        SHF.cancelFling("mini")
    end
    local function startSession()
        if espLifecycle.stopped then ST.fling_mini.on=false; return end
        -- UI/config synchronization must not silently reset the running timer.
        if activeSession and ST.fling_mini.on then return end
        local session={startedAt=os.clock()}
        activeSession=session
        ST.fling_mini.on=true
        task.spawn(function()
            local ok,err=pcall(function()
                while sessionEnabled(session) do
                    if not ST.fling_active then
                        local target=findTarget()
                        if target then mini_fling(target,session) end
                    end
                    if not sessionEnabled(session) then break end
                    local pause=ST.fling_active and 0.05 or (tonumber(ST.fling_mini.delay) or 0.15)
                    task.wait(math.min(math.max(0.01,pause),sessionRemaining(session)))
                end
            end)
            -- A cancelled old worker must never switch off a newer activation.
            if activeSession==session then stopSession() end
            if not ok then warn("[Fling Session] Error:",err) end
        end)
    end

    -- === ЗАХВАТ УБИЙЦЫ ===
    local function bind_killer(char)
        if not char then return end
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hum then return end
        table.insert(espLifecycle.connections,hum.Died:Connect(function()
            local k = hum:FindFirstChild("creator") or hum:FindFirstChild("Killer")
            if k and k.Value then ST.fling_killer_name = k.Value.Name end
        end))
    end
    bind_killer(LP.Character)
    table.insert(espLifecycle.connections,LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(c)
        task.wait(1)
        if not espLifecycle.stopped and LP.Character==c then bind_killer(c) end
    end))

    table.insert(espLifecycle.cleanups,stopSession)
    table.insert(espLifecycle.connections,LP.CharacterRemoving:Connect(stopSession))

    -- === API ===
    SHF.fling_mini = {
        set_on = function(v)
            if v==true then startSession() else stopSession() end
        end,
        set_mode     = function(m) ST.fling_mini.mode = m end,
        set_target   = function(n) ST.fling_mini.target = n end,
        set_bypass   = function(v) ST.fling_mini.bypass_vel = v end,
        set_delay    = function(v) ST.fling_mini.delay = v end,
        set_duration = function(v)
            local value=tonumber(v)
            if not value or value~=value then return end
            ST.fling_mini.duration=math.clamp(value,1,5)
            if activeSession and sessionRemaining(activeSession)<=0 then stopSession() end
        end,
        get_killer   = function() return ST.fling_killer_name end,
        state        = ST.fling_mini,
        fling_now    = mini_fling,
    }
end
initFlingMini()

-- ============================================================
-- INIT 10: MOVEMENT (FIXED InfJump)
-- ============================================================
local function initMovement()
    -- Fly v2: camera-relative PlayerModule controls (keyboard, touch, gamepad).
    -- Constraints are recreated for each character and removed on disable/unload.
    do
        local controls, lastControlsTry = nil, -math.huge
        local root, humanoid, attachment, velocity, orientation
        local savedAutoRotate, savedPlatformStand
        local function stopFly()
            for _, object in pairs({velocity, orientation, attachment}) do
                if object then object:Destroy() end
            end
            velocity, orientation, attachment = nil, nil, nil
            if root and root.Parent then
                root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            end
            if humanoid and humanoid.Parent then
                humanoid.AutoRotate = savedAutoRotate
                humanoid.PlatformStand = savedPlatformStand
                if humanoid.Health > 0 and not savedPlatformStand then
                    humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
                end
            end
            root, humanoid = nil, nil
        end

        local function loadControls()
            if controls or os.clock() - lastControlsTry < 2 then return end
            lastControlsTry = os.clock()
            -- Never yield inside the physics callback.
            local scripts = LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerScripts")
            local module = scripts and scripts:FindFirstChild("PlayerModule")
            if not module then return end
            task.spawn(function()
                local ok, result = pcall(function()
                    return require(module):GetControls()
                end)
                if ok and not espLifecycle.stopped then controls = result end
            end)
        end

        local function moveVector(hum, camera)
            if UIS:GetFocusedTextBox() then return Vector3.zero end
            if controls then
                local ok, result = pcall(function() return controls:GetMoveVector() end)
                if ok and typeof(result) == "Vector3" then
                    -- Standard Roblox controls return camera-local X/Z, including analog strength.
                    local world = camera.CFrame.RightVector * result.X
                        + camera.CFrame.LookVector * -result.Z
                    return world.Magnitude > 1 and world.Unit or world
                end
            end
            -- Fallback for games with no standard PlayerModule.
            local x = (UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) and 1 or 0)
                - (UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) and 1 or 0)
            local z = (UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) and 1 or 0)
                - (UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) and 1 or 0)
            if x ~= 0 or z ~= 0 then
                local world = camera.CFrame.RightVector * x + camera.CFrame.LookVector * z
                return world.Magnitude > 1 and world.Unit or world
            end
            local md = hum.MoveDirection
            local look = camera.CFrame.LookVector
            local flat = Vector3.new(look.X, 0, look.Z)
            if flat.Magnitude > 0.001 then
                local forward = flat.Unit
                local right = Vector3.new(-forward.Z, 0, forward.X)
                return camera.CFrame.RightVector * md:Dot(right) + look * md:Dot(forward)
            end
            return md
        end

        local function startFly(hrp, hum)
            root, humanoid = hrp, hum
            savedAutoRotate, savedPlatformStand = hum.AutoRotate, hum.PlatformStand
            hum.Sit = false
            hum.AutoRotate = false
            hum.PlatformStand = false
            hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics)
            attachment = Instance.new("Attachment")
            attachment.Name = "DaDaFlyAttachment"
            attachment.Parent = hrp
            velocity = Instance.new("LinearVelocity")
            velocity.Name = "DaDaFlyVelocity"
            velocity.Attachment0 = attachment
            velocity.RelativeTo = Enum.ActuatorRelativeTo.World
            velocity.VelocityConstraintMode = Enum.VelocityConstraintMode.Vector
            velocity.ForceLimitsEnabled = false
            velocity.VectorVelocity = Vector3.zero
            velocity.Parent = hrp
            orientation = Instance.new("AlignOrientation")
            orientation.Name = "DaDaFlyOrientation"
            orientation.Attachment0 = attachment
            orientation.Mode = Enum.OrientationAlignmentMode.OneAttachment
            orientation.RigidityEnabled = false
            orientation.MaxTorque = math.huge
            orientation.MaxAngularVelocity = math.huge
            orientation.Responsiveness = 35
            orientation.CFrame = hrp.CFrame.Rotation
            orientation.Parent = hrp
        end

        local function flyStep()
            if espLifecycle.stopped then return end
            local char = LP.Character
            local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not ST.fly or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 or not hrp or hrp.Anchored then
                if root then stopFly() end
                return
            end
            if root ~= hrp or humanoid ~= hum or not velocity or velocity.Parent ~= hrp
                or not orientation or orientation.Parent ~= hrp
                or not attachment or attachment.Parent ~= hrp then
                stopFly()
                startFly(hrp, hum)
            end
            loadControls()
            local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
            if not camera then velocity.VectorVelocity = Vector3.zero; return end
            local mv = moveVector(hum, camera)
            -- Altitude follows the camera pitch; no extra buttons or height keys.
            if mv.Magnitude > 1 then mv = mv.Unit end
            velocity.VectorVelocity = mv * math.clamp(tonumber(ST.fly_speed) or 80, 0, 300)
            local look = camera.CFrame.LookVector
            local flat = Vector3.new(look.X, 0, look.Z)
            if flat.Magnitude > 0.001 then
                orientation.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(Vector3.zero, flat.Unit)
            end
            hum.AutoRotate = false
            hum.PlatformStand = false
            if hum:GetState() ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics then
                hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics)
            end
        end
        loadControls()
        table.insert(espLifecycle.connections, RunService.PreSimulation:Connect(flyStep))
        table.insert(espLifecycle.connections, LP.CharacterRemoving:Connect(stopFly))
        table.insert(espLifecycle.cleanups, stopFly)
    end
    local nocache={}
    RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
        if not ST.noclip then
            for p,v in pairs(nocache) do if p.Parent then p.CanCollide=v end end
            nocache={}
            return
        end
        local char=LP.Character; if not char then return end
        for _,p in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
            if p:IsA("BasePart") then
                if nocache[p]==nil then nocache[p]=p.CanCollide end
                if p.CanCollide then p.CanCollide=false end
            end
        end
    end)
    local infJumpConn=nil
    RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if ST.inf_jump and not infJumpConn then
            infJumpConn=UIS.JumpRequest:Connect(function()
                if not ST.inf_jump or ST.fly then return end
                local hum=LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if hum then hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping) end
            end)
        elseif not ST.inf_jump and infJumpConn then
            pcall(function() infJumpConn:Disconnect() end)
            infJumpConn=nil
        end
    end)
    -- Save the actual speed of each controlled Humanoid, not a hard-coded 16.
    local speedHumanoid, savedWalkSpeed
    local function restoreWalkSpeed()
        local hum, original = speedHumanoid, savedWalkSpeed
        speedHumanoid, savedWalkSpeed = nil, nil
        if hum and original ~= nil then
            pcall(function() hum.WalkSpeed = original end)
        end
    end
    local function speedStep()
        local hum = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if espLifecycle.stopped or not ST.speed_hack or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then
            restoreWalkSpeed()
            return
        end
        if hum ~= speedHumanoid then
            restoreWalkSpeed()
            speedHumanoid, savedWalkSpeed = hum, hum.WalkSpeed
        end
        if hum.WalkSpeed ~= ST.speed_val then hum.WalkSpeed = ST.speed_val end
    end
    SHF.setSpeedHack = function(enabled)
        ST.speed_hack = enabled
        speedStep()
    end
    table.insert(espLifecycle.connections, RunService.Stepped:Connect(speedStep))
    table.insert(espLifecycle.connections, LP.CharacterRemoving:Connect(restoreWalkSpeed))
    table.insert(espLifecycle.cleanups, restoreWalkSpeed)
    local jumpHumanoid, savedJump
    local function restoreJump()
        local hum, original=jumpHumanoid,savedJump
        jumpHumanoid,savedJump=nil,nil
        if hum and original then
            pcall(function()
                hum.JumpPower=original.JumpPower
                hum.JumpHeight=original.JumpHeight
                hum.UseJumpPower=original.UseJumpPower
            end)
        end
    end
    local function jumpStep()
        local hum=LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if espLifecycle.stopped or not ST.jump_power or not hum or hum.Health<=0 then
            restoreJump(); return
        end
        if hum~=jumpHumanoid then
            restoreJump()
            jumpHumanoid=hum
            savedJump={JumpPower=hum.JumpPower,JumpHeight=hum.JumpHeight,UseJumpPower=hum.UseJumpPower}
        end
        hum.UseJumpPower=true
        if hum.JumpPower~=ST.jump_val then hum.JumpPower=ST.jump_val end
    end
    SHF.setJumpPower=function(enabled) ST.jump_power=enabled; jumpStep() end
    table.insert(espLifecycle.connections,RunService.Stepped:Connect(jumpStep))
    table.insert(espLifecycle.connections,LP.CharacterRemoving:Connect(restoreJump))
    table.insert(espLifecycle.cleanups,restoreJump)
    local bhop_conn=nil; local bhop_speed=0; local bhop_last_jump=0; local bhop_last_cam_yaw=nil
    local function bhop_cleanup()
        if bhop_conn then pcall(function() bhop_conn:Disconnect() end) bhop_conn=nil end
        bhop_speed=0; bhop_last_jump=0; bhop_last_cam_yaw=nil
    end
    local function bhop_start()
        bhop_cleanup()
        bhop_conn=RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not ST.bhop or ST.fly then return end
            local hum=LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local hrp=get_hrp()
            if not hum or not hrp then return end
            local state=hum:GetState()
            local jumping=state==Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping
            local freefall=state==Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall
            local airborne=jumping or freefall
            local now=tick()
            if jumping and now-bhop_last_jump>0.03 then
                bhop_last_jump=now
                local v=hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity
                local xz=Vector3.new(v.X,0,v.Z)
                local dir
                if xz.Magnitude>0.1 then dir=xz.Unit
                else
                    local md=hum.MoveDirection
                    if md.Magnitude>0.1 then dir=Vector3.new(md.X,0,md.Z).Unit
                    else
                        local lv=hrp.CFrame.LookVector
                        dir=Vector3.new(lv.X,0,lv.Z)
                        if dir.Magnitude>0 then dir=dir.Unit end
                    end
                end
                if dir then
                    bhop_speed=math.max(bhop_speed,hum.WalkSpeed*1.5)
                    bhop_speed=math.min(bhop_speed+ST.bhop_power*0.35,ST.bhop_power+hum.WalkSpeed*2)
                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity=Vector3.new(dir.X*bhop_speed,v.Y,dir.Z*bhop_speed)
                end
            end
            if airborne and bhop_speed>0 then
                local v=hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity
                local xz=Vector3.new(v.X,0,v.Z)
                local md=hum.MoveDirection
                local dir
                if md.Magnitude>0.1 then dir=Vector3.new(md.X,0,md.Z).Unit
                elseif xz.Magnitude>0.1 then dir=xz.Unit end
                if dir then
                    local speed=math.max(xz.Magnitude,bhop_speed)
                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity=Vector3.new(dir.X*speed,v.Y,dir.Z*speed)
                end
            end
            if ST.bhop_strafe and airborne then
                local cam=workspace.CurrentCamera
                if cam then
                    local look=cam.CFrame.LookVector
                    local yaw=math.atan2(-look.X,-look.Z)
                    if bhop_last_cam_yaw then
                        local delta=yaw-bhop_last_cam_yaw
                        while delta>math.pi do delta=delta-math.pi*2 end
                        while delta<-math.pi do delta=delta+math.pi*2 end
                        if math.abs(delta)>0.0005 then
                            local v=hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity
                            local xz=Vector3.new(v.X,0,v.Z)
                            if xz.Magnitude>1 then
                                local rotated=CFrame.fromEulerAnglesYXZ(0,delta,0)*xz
                                hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity=Vector3.new(rotated.X,v.Y,rotated.Z)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    bhop_last_cam_yaw=yaw
                end
            end
            if not airborne then
                bhop_speed=hum.WalkSpeed
                if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then hum.Jump=true end
            end
        end)
    end
    RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if ST.bhop and not bhop_conn then bhop_start()
        elseif not ST.bhop and bhop_conn then bhop_cleanup() end
    end)
    local orig_grav=workspace.Gravity
    RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
        if ST.gravity then
            if workspace.Gravity~=ST.gravity_val then workspace.Gravity=ST.gravity_val end
        elseif workspace.Gravity~=orig_grav then workspace.Gravity=orig_grav end
    end)
end
initMovement()

        ['Velvet Purple']={Brightness=1.1, ClockTime=20, Ambient={95,55,135}, OutdoorAmbient={30,15,55}, FogColor={145,90,195}, FogEnd=850, ExposureCompensation=0, Bloom=0.3, Saturation=0.18, Contrast=0.15},
