        crystal={col=Color3.fromRGB(160,245,255),rate=90,size=0.35,speed=2,lifetime=3},
        rainbow={col=Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255),rate=100,size=0.45,speed=3,lifetime=2.5},
        shadow={col=Color3.fromRGB(95,75,125),rate=55,size=0.6,speed=1,lifetime=3},
        mint={col=Color3.fromRGB(110,255,205),rate=75,size=0.45,speed=2,lifetime=2.8},
        coral={col=Color3.fromRGB(255,130,115),rate=80,size=0.5,speed=3,lifetime=2.3},
        gold={col=Color3.fromRGB(255,215,100),rate=95,size=0.45,speed=3,lifetime=2},
        sapphire={col=Color3.fromRGB(65,105,255),rate=90,size=0.45,speed=3,lifetime=2.5},
        amethyst={col=Color3.fromRGB(190,100,255),rate=85,size=0.5,speed=3,lifetime=2.5},
        pearl={col=Color3.fromRGB(235,245,255),rate=65,size=0.4,speed=1,lifetime=3},
        cinder={col=Color3.fromRGB(190,65,35),rate=75,size=0.5,speed=4,lifetime=1.9},
        breeze={col=Color3.fromRGB(175,255,220),rate=55,size=0.4,speed=2,lifetime=3},
        comet={col=Color3.fromRGB(120,190,255),rate=100,size=0.4,speed=7,lifetime=1.5},
        stardust={col=Color3.fromRGB(255,245,175),rate=115,size=0.35,speed=2,lifetime=3},
        aurum={col=Color3.fromRGB(255,175,65),rate=80,size=0.5,speed=4,lifetime=2},
        glacier={col=Color3.fromRGB(125,210,255),rate=80,size=0.45,speed=2,lifetime=3},
        dream={col=Color3.fromRGB(220,145,255),rate=75,size=0.5,speed=2,lifetime=3},
    }
    local aura_inst=nil
    local function clearAura()
        if aura_inst then pcall(function() aura_inst:Destroy() end) aura_inst=nil end
    end
    local function applyAura()
        clearAura()
        local char=LP.Character
        if not char then return end
        local preset=AURA_PRESETS[SHF.aura_type] or AURA_PRESETS.angel
        local part=char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or char:FindFirstChild("Torso")
        if not part then return end
        local att=Instance.new("Attachment")
        att.Parent=part
        local emitter=Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
        pcall(function() emitter.Texture="rbxasset://textures/particles/sparkles_main.dds" end)
        emitter.Color=ColorSequence.new(SHF.aura_col or preset.col)
        emitter.Rate=preset.rate
        emitter.Lifetime=NumberRange.new(preset.lifetime,preset.lifetime+1)
        emitter.Speed=NumberRange.new(preset.speed,preset.speed+2)
        emitter.Size=NumberSequence.new(preset.size)
        emitter.Transparency=NumberSequence.new({
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0,0.3),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1,1)
        })
        emitter.LightEmission=1
        emitter.LightInfluence=0
        emitter.Parent=att
        aura_inst=att
    end
    SHF.applyAura=applyAura
    SHF.clearAura=clearAura
    LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
        task.wait(1)
        if SHF.aura then applyAura() end
    end)

    do
        local aura2_ids = {
            angel     = "97658130917593",
            starlight = "134645216613107",
            heavenly  = "139300897520961",
            ribbon    = "132069507632161",
            sakura    = "81755778619404",
            wind      = "80694081850877",
            flow      = "119913533725648",
            star      = "73754563740680"
        }
        local aura2_cache = {}
        local aura2_particles = {}
        local aura2_on = false
        local aura2_type = "angel"
        local aura2_col = Color3.fromRGB(133, 220, 255)
        local aura2_conn = nil

        local function load_aura2(name)
            if aura2_cache[name] then return aura2_cache[name] end
            local id = aura2_ids[name]
            if not id then return nil end
            local ok, objs = pcall(game.GetObjects, game, "rbxassetid://"..id)
            if ok and objs and objs[1] then
                aura2_cache[name] = objs[1]
                return objs[1]
            end
            return nil
        end

        local function color_aura2(model,color)
            local sequence=ColorSequence.new(color)
            local function tint(object)
                if object:IsA("Light") then object.Color=color
                elseif object:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or object:IsA("Beam") or object:IsA("Trail") then
                    object.Color=sequence
                end
            end
            pcall(tint,model)
            for _,object in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do pcall(tint,object) end
        end

        local function clear_aura2()
            for i = #aura2_particles, 1, -1 do
                pcall(function() aura2_particles[i]:Destroy() end)
                aura2_particles[i] = nil
            end
        end

        local function apply_aura2()
            clear_aura2()
            local char = LP.Character
            if not char then return end
            local src = load_aura2(aura2_type)
            if not src then return end
            color_aura2(src, aura2_col)
            local cloned = src:Clone()
            local attached = 0
            for _, part in ipairs(cloned:GetChildren()) do
                local target = char:FindFirstChild(part.Name)
                if not target then
                    for _, d in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
                        if d:IsA("BasePart") and d.Name == part.Name then target = d; break end
                    end
                end
                if target and target:IsA("BasePart") then
                    for _, child in ipairs(part:GetChildren()) do
                        child.Parent = target
                        aura2_particles[#aura2_particles+1] = child
                        attached = attached + 1
                    end
                end
            end
            cloned:Destroy()
        end

        SHF.applyAura2 = function()
            if not aura2_on then return end
            task.spawn(apply_aura2)
        end
        SHF.clearAura2 = clear_aura2

        SHF.aura2Toggle = function(v)
            SHF.aura2 = v
            aura2_on = v
            if v then
                task.spawn(apply_aura2)
                if aura2_conn then pcall(function() aura2_conn:Disconnect() end) end
                aura2_conn = LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
                    task.wait(0.5)
                    if aura2_on then apply_aura2() end
                end)
            else
                if aura2_conn then pcall(function() aura2_conn:Disconnect() end) aura2_conn = nil end
                clear_aura2()
            end
        end

        SHF.aura2Type = function(t)
            aura2_type = t or aura2_type
            SHF.aura2_type = aura2_type
            if aura2_on then task.spawn(apply_aura2) end
        end

        SHF.aura2Color = function(c)
            SHF.aura2_color = c
            aura2_col = c
            for _,m in pairs(aura2_cache) do color_aura2(m,c) end
            for _,object in ipairs(aura2_particles) do color_aura2(object,c) end
        end

        LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
            task.wait(1)
            if aura2_on then apply_aura2() end
        end)
    end
end
initWorld()

-- ============================================================
-- INIT 12: INVISIBLE + COSMETICS + OVERLAYS
-- ============================================================
local function initInvisExtras()
    do
        -- Position pulse. Restore before the camera runs, without
        -- yielding a Heartbeat callback or leaving an old character displaced.
        local enabled=false
        local saved={}
        local character, pending, descendants
        local renderName="DaDaInvisibleRestore"
        SHF.invisible=false
        local function restorePulse()
            local state=pending
            pending=nil
            if not state then return end
            if state.root.Parent then
                pcall(function()
                    state.root.CFrame=state.cf
                    state.root.AssemblyLinearVelocity=state.velocity
                    state.root.AssemblyAngularVelocity=state.angular
                end)
            end
            if state.hum.Parent then
                pcall(function() state.hum.CameraOffset=state.offset end)
            end
        end
        local function ghost(obj)
            if (obj:IsA("BasePart") and obj.Name~="HumanoidRootPart")
                or obj:IsA("Decal") or obj:IsA("Texture") then
                if saved[obj]==nil then saved[obj]=obj.Transparency end
                obj.Transparency=0.5
            end
        end
        local function clearCharacter()
            restorePulse()
            if descendants then descendants:Disconnect(); descendants=nil end
            for obj,value in pairs(saved) do
                if obj.Parent then pcall(function() obj.Transparency=value end) end
            end
            table.clear(saved)
            character=nil
        end
        local function attach(char)
            clearCharacter()
            character=char
            if not char then return end
            for _,obj in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do ghost(obj) end
            descendants=char.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
                if enabled then ghost(obj) end
            end)
        end
        SHF.invisToggle=function(value)
            enabled=value==true
            SHF.invisible=enabled
            ST.quick_state.invisible=enabled
            if enabled then attach(LP.Character) else clearCharacter() end
        end
        table.insert(espLifecycle.connections,RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not enabled or espLifecycle.stopped then return end
            restorePulse()
            local char=LP.Character
            if char~=character then attach(char) end
            if not char then return end
            local root=char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local hum=char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if not root or not hum or hum.Health<=0 then return end
            local original=root.CFrame
            pending={root=root,hum=hum,cf=original,offset=hum.CameraOffset,
                velocity=root.AssemblyLinearVelocity,angular=root.AssemblyAngularVelocity}
            local displaced=original*CFrame.new(0,-200000,0)
            root.CFrame=displaced
            hum.CameraOffset=displaced:ToObjectSpace(original).Position
        end))
        RunService:BindToRenderStep(renderName,Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value-1,restorePulse)
        table.insert(espLifecycle.connections,LP.CharacterRemoving:Connect(function(char)
            if char==character then clearCharacter() end
        end))
        table.insert(espLifecycle.cleanups,function()
            SHF.invisToggle(false)
            RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(renderName)
        end)
    end
    do
        local GUN_SKINS={
            ["Golden"]=Color3.fromRGB(255,215,0),
            ["Rainbow"]=Color3.fromRGB(255,100,255),
            ["Neon"]=Color3.fromRGB(0,255,255),
            ["Dark"]=Color3.fromRGB(20,20,30),
            ["Blood"]=Color3.fromRGB(180,0,0),
        }
        local KNIFE_SKINS={
            ["Golden"]=Color3.fromRGB(255,215,0),
            ["Neon"]=Color3.fromRGB(0,255,255),
            ["Blood"]=Color3.fromRGB(180,0,0),
            ["Shadow"]=Color3.fromRGB(30,30,40),
        }
        local saved={}
        local function restoreTool(tool)
            for part,props in pairs(saved) do
                if part and part.Parent and part:IsDescendantOf(tool) then
                    pcall(function() part.Color=props.Color end)
                    pcall(function() part.Material=props.Material end)
                    saved[part]=nil
                end
            end
        end
        local function applyTool(tool, color)
            if not tool then return end
            if not color then restoreTool(tool); return end
            for _,part in ipairs(tool:GetDescendants()) do
                if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                    if not saved[part] then saved[part]={Color=part.Color,Material=part.Material} end
                    part.Color=color
                    part.Material=Enum.Material.Neon
                end
            end
        end
        local skin_conn=RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            local char=LP.Character
            local backpack=LP:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
            local function update(container, toolName, color)
                if not container then return end
                for _,tool in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
                    if tool:IsA("Tool") and tool.Name==toolName then applyTool(tool,color) end
                end
            end
            local gunColor=GUN_SKINS[SHF.gun_skin]
            local knifeColor=KNIFE_SKINS[SHF.knife_skin]
            update(char,"Gun",gunColor)
            update(backpack,"Gun",gunColor)
            update(char,"Knife",knifeColor)
            update(backpack,"Knife",knifeColor)
            for part in pairs(saved) do
                if not part or not part.Parent then saved[part]=nil end
            end
        end)
        SHF._skinDisconnect=function()
            if skin_conn then pcall(function() skin_conn:Disconnect() end); skin_conn=nil end
            for part,props in pairs(saved) do
                if part and part.Parent then
                    pcall(function() part.Color=props.Color end)
                    pcall(function() part.Material=props.Material end)
                end
            end
            table.clear(saved)
        end
    end

    -- Visual overlays are toggled with F6 (Role List) and F7 (Target HUD).
    -- No existing menu panels or Combat sections are modified.
    do
        SHF.role_list=SHF.role_list or false
        SHF.target_hud=SHF.target_hud or false
        local function makeOverlay(name,size,pos)
            local label=Instance.new("TextLabel")
            label.Name=name
            label.Size=size
            label.Position=pos
            label.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(15,18,21)
            label.BackgroundTransparency=0.15
            label.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(235,235,235)
            label.Font=Enum.Font.GothamMedium
            label.TextSize=12
            label.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            label.TextYAlignment=Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
            label.TextWrapped=true
            label.Visible=false
            label.Parent=gui
            Instance.new("UICorner",label).CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,8)
            table.insert(themeUpdaters,function(t)
                label.BackgroundColor3=t.Inline
                label.TextColor3=t.Text
            end)
            return label
        end
        local roleList=makeOverlay("DaDaRoleList",UDim2.new(0,200,0,160),UDim2.new(1,-210,0,10))
        local targetHUD=makeOverlay("DaDaTargetHUD",UDim2.new(0,200,0,80),UDim2.new(1,-210,0,180))
        local visualConn=RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            roleList.Visible=SHF.role_list
            targetHUD.Visible=SHF.target_hud
            if SHF.role_list then
                local lines={}
                local ok,round=pcall(get_round)
                local data=ok and round and round.PlayerData
                if type(data)=="table" then
                    for name,info in pairs(data) do
                        if type(info)=="table" and not info.Dead and info.Role then
                            lines[#lines+1]=tostring(name).." - "..tostring(info.Role)
                        end
                    end
                end
                table.sort(lines)
                roleList.Text=#lines>0 and table.concat(lines,"\n") or "No role data"
            end
            if SHF.target_hud then
                local best,bestDist=nil,math.huge
                local myHrp=get_hrp()
                local cam=workspace.CurrentCamera
                if myHrp and cam then
                    for _,player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                        if player~=LP and player.Character then
                            local hrp=player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                            local hum=player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                            if hrp and hum and hum.Health>0 then
                                local screen,onScreen=cam:WorldToViewportPoint(hrp.Position)
                                if onScreen and screen.Z>0 then
                                    local dist=(hrp.Position-myHrp.Position).Magnitude
                                    if dist<bestDist then
                                        bestDist=dist
                                        best={player=player,humanoid=hum}
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                if best then
                    targetHUD.Text=string.format("%s\nHP: %d/%d\nDistance: %dm",best.player.Name,math.floor(best.humanoid.Health),math.floor(best.humanoid.MaxHealth),math.floor(bestDist))
                else
                    targetHUD.Text="Target: none"
                end
            end
        end)
        local visualKeyConn=UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(input,gpe)
            if gpe then return end
            if input.KeyCode==Enum.KeyCode.F6 then
                SHF.role_list=not SHF.role_list
            elseif input.KeyCode==Enum.KeyCode.F7 then
                SHF.target_hud=not SHF.target_hud
            end
        end)
        SHF._visualDisconnect=function()
            if visualConn then pcall(function() visualConn:Disconnect() end); visualConn=nil end
            if visualKeyConn then pcall(function() visualKeyConn:Disconnect() end); visualKeyConn=nil end
            if roleList then pcall(function() roleList:Destroy() end) end
            if targetHUD then pcall(function() targetHUD:Destroy() end) end
            SHF.role_list=false
            SHF.target_hud=false
        end
    end

    do
        local ANIM_IDS={
            ["Default"]=nil,
            ["Ninja"]={idle1="rbxassetid://656117400",idle2="rbxassetid://656118341",walk="rbxassetid://656121766",run="rbxassetid://656118852",jump="rbxassetid://656117878"},
            ["Zombie"]={idle1="rbxassetid://616158929",idle2="rbxassetid://616160636",walk="rbxassetid://616168032",run="rbxassetid://616163682",jump="rbxassetid://616161997"},
            ["Pirate"]={idle1="rbxassetid://750781874",idle2="rbxassetid://750782230",walk="rbxassetid://750785693",run="rbxassetid://750783738",jump="rbxassetid://750782770"},
            ["Robot"]={idle1="rbxassetid://616088211",idle2="rbxassetid://616089559",walk="rbxassetid://616095330",run="rbxassetid://616091570",jump="rbxassetid://616090588"},
        }
        -- Keep the selection, not references to a dead character's Animate script.
        local packCharacter, packAnimate
        local originals = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
        local elapsed, lastSelection = 0, SHF.anim_pack or "Default"
        local paths = {
            {"idle", "Animation1", "idle1"}, {"idle", "Animation2", "idle2"},
            {"walk", "WalkAnim", "walk"}, {"run", "RunAnim", "run"},
            {"jump", "JumpAnim", "jump"},
        }

        local function refresh(animate, hum, changedIds)
            if not animate or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then return end
            local animator = hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
            if animator then
                for _, track in ipairs(animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
                    local animation = track.Animation
                    if animation and changedIds[animation.AnimationId] then
                        pcall(function() track:Stop(0.1) end)
                    end
                end
            end
            -- Preserve disabled scripts instead of enabling something the game disabled.
            if not animate.Disabled then
                animate.Disabled = true
                animate.Disabled = false
            end
        end

        local function restorePack()
            local changed, ids = false, {}
            for animation, id in pairs(originals) do
                if animation.Parent and animation.AnimationId ~= id then
                    ids[animation.AnimationId], ids[id] = true, true
                    animation.AnimationId = id
                    changed = true
                end
            end
            originals = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
            if changed and packCharacter then
                refresh(packAnimate, packCharacter:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid"), ids)
            end
        end

        local function syncPack()
            if espLifecycle.stopped then return end
            local char = LP.Character
            local animate = char and char:FindFirstChild("Animate")
            if char ~= packCharacter or animate ~= packAnimate then
                originals = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
                packCharacter, packAnimate = char, animate
            end
            local name = SHF.anim_pack or "Default"
            if name ~= lastSelection then
                restorePack()
                lastSelection = name
            end
            local ids = ANIM_IDS[name]
            local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if not ids or not animate or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then return end
            local changed, changedIds = false, {}
            for _, path in ipairs(paths) do
                local folder = animate:FindFirstChild(path[1])
                local animation = folder and folder:FindFirstChild(path[2])
                local id = ids[path[3]]
                if animation and animation:IsA("Animation") and id then
                    if originals[animation] == nil then originals[animation] = animation.AnimationId end
                    if animation.AnimationId ~= id then
                        changedIds[animation.AnimationId], changedIds[id] = true, true
                        animation.AnimationId = id
                        changed = true
                    end
                end
            end
            if changed then refresh(animate, hum, changedIds) end
        end

        SHF.applyAnim = function(name)
            if name ~= "Default" and not ANIM_IDS[name] then return end
            -- The two animation menus must not keep overwriting one another.
            if name ~= "Default" and SHF.animReset then SHF.animReset() end
            restorePack()
            SHF.anim_pack = name
            lastSelection = name
            syncPack()
        end
        table.insert(espLifecycle.connections, LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
            packCharacter, packAnimate = nil, nil
            originals = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
            elapsed = 0.4
            task.defer(syncPack)
        end))
        table.insert(espLifecycle.connections, LP.CharacterAppearanceLoaded:Connect(function(char)
            if char == LP.Character then task.defer(syncPack) end
        end))
        -- Also catches Animate/Animation descendants inserted after CharacterAdded,
        -- and late avatar initialization overwriting the IDs after appearance loading.
        table.insert(espLifecycle.connections, RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
            elapsed = elapsed + dt
            if elapsed < 0.4 then return end
            elapsed = 0
            syncPack()
        end))
        table.insert(espLifecycle.cleanups, function()
            restorePack()
            SHF.anim_pack = "Default"
        end)
        syncPack()
    end

    do
        local last_role=nil
        task.spawn(function()
            while task.wait(0.5) do
                if not SHF.round_notify then continue end
                local m=get_round()
                local d=m and m.PlayerData
                local me=d and d[LP.Name]
                local role=me and me.Role
                if role and role~=last_role then
                    last_role=role
                    if notify then pcall(notify,"Role",role,4) end
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        while task.wait(1) do
            if not SHF.killer_notify then continue end
            local m=get_round()
            local d=m and m.PlayerData
            if type(d)=="table" then
                for name,info in pairs(d) do
                    if type(info)=="table" and info.Role=="Murderer" and not info.Dead then
                        if not SHF.role_map[name] then
                            SHF.role_map[name]=true
                            if notify then pcall(notify,"Murderer","@"..name,5) end
                        end
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end
initInvisExtras()

-- ============================================================
-- INIT 13: NOTIFICATIONS + SOUNDS
-- ============================================================
local function initNotifications()
    notifGui=Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    notifGui.Name="DaDaNotif"
    notifGui.ResetOnSpawn=false
    notifGui.IgnoreGuiInset=true
    notifGui.DisplayOrder=101
    pcall(function() notifGui.Parent=CoreGui end)
    if not notifGui.Parent then notifGui.Parent=LP:WaitForChild("PlayerGui") end
    local camera=workspace.CurrentCamera
    local viewport=camera and camera.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(1280,720)
    local holderWidth=math.min(320,math.max(160,viewport.X-32))
    local notifHolder=Instance.new("Frame")
    notifHolder.Name="NotificationStack"
    notifHolder.AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(1,0)
    notifHolder.Size=UDim2.new(0,holderWidth,1,-80)
    notifHolder.Position=UDim2.new(1,-16,0,70)
    notifHolder.BackgroundTransparency=1
    notifHolder.BorderSizePixel=0
    notifHolder.Parent=notifGui
    local nlist=Instance.new("UIListLayout",notifHolder)
    nlist.Padding=UDim.new(0,8)
    nlist.SortOrder=Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    nlist.HorizontalAlignment=Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right
    local textService=game:GetService("TextService")
    local notificationOrder=0
    notify=function(title,desc,dur)
        if not notifGui or not notifGui.Parent or espLifecycle.stopped then return end
        -- Keep the original two-second maximum lifetime and the notify API.
        dur=math.clamp(tonumber(dur) or 2,0.6,2)
        title=tostring(title or "")
        desc=tostring(desc or "")
        local textWidth=holderWidth-44
        local titleHeight=0
        local descHeight=0
        if title~="" then
            titleHeight=math.max(18,math.ceil(textService:GetTextSize(title,14,Enum.Font.GothamBold,Vector2.new(textWidth,100000)).Y)+2)
        end
        if desc~="" then
            descHeight=math.max(18,math.ceil(textService:GetTextSize(desc,14,Enum.Font.Gotham,Vector2.new(textWidth,100000)).Y)+2)
        end
        local gap=(titleHeight>0 and descHeight>0) and 4 or 0
        local contentHeight=titleHeight+gap+descHeight
        local cardHeight=math.max(60,contentHeight+24)
        local top=math.floor((cardHeight-contentHeight)/2)
        notificationOrder=notificationOrder+1
        local wrap=Instance.new("Frame")
        wrap.Name="NotificationSlot"
        wrap.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,cardHeight)
        wrap.LayoutOrder=notificationOrder
        wrap.BackgroundTransparency=1
        wrap.BorderSizePixel=0
        wrap.Parent=notifHolder

        local card=Instance.new("CanvasGroup")
        card.Name="NotificationCard"
        card.Size=UDim2.fromScale(1,1)
        card.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(24,0)
        card.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(45,47,51)
        card.GroupTransparency=1
        card.BorderSizePixel=0
        card.Parent=wrap
        Instance.new("UICorner",card).CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,5)

        local rail=Instance.new("Frame")
        rail.Name="AccentRail"
        rail.AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0,0.5)
        rail.Position=UDim2.new(0,12,0.5,0)
        rail.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(3,28)
        rail.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(132,206,255)
        rail.BorderSizePixel=0
        rail.Parent=card
        Instance.new("UICorner",rail).CornerRadius=UDim.new(1,0)

        local t=Instance.new("TextLabel")
        t.Name="Title"
        t.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(textWidth,titleHeight)
        t.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(28,top)
        t.BackgroundTransparency=1
        t.Text=title
        t.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(245,247,250)
        t.TextSize=14
        t.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold
        t.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        t.TextYAlignment=Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
        t.TextWrapped=true
        t.Visible=titleHeight>0
        t.Parent=card

        local d=Instance.new("TextLabel")
        d.Name="Description"
        d.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(textWidth,descHeight)
        d.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(28,top+titleHeight+gap)
        d.BackgroundTransparency=1
        d.Text=desc
        d.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(187,193,202)
        d.TextSize=14
        d.Font=Enum.Font.Gotham
        d.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        d.TextYAlignment=Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
        d.TextWrapped=true
        d.Visible=descHeight>0
        d.Parent=card

        -- Clean gray surface, short blue rail, text on the right; no timer bar.
        TweenService:Create(card,TweenInfo.new(0.2,Enum.EasingStyle.Quint,Enum.EasingDirection.Out),{
            Position=UDim2.fromOffset(0,0),GroupTransparency=0
        }):Play()
        task.delay(dur-0.22,function()
            if not wrap.Parent or not card.Parent then return end
            TweenService:Create(card,TweenInfo.new(0.2,Enum.EasingStyle.Quint,Enum.EasingDirection.In),{
                Position=UDim2.fromOffset(24,0),GroupTransparency=1
            }):Play()
            task.wait(0.22)
            if wrap.Parent then wrap:Destroy() end
        end)
    end

    -- Kitty-compatible sound assets: use Roblox asset IDs directly instead of
    -- downloading .ogg files from a GitHub URL (that was the main playback failure).
    local SND_ASSETS={
        ["primordial"]="6534947869",
        ["neverlose"]="110168723447153",
        ["sparkle"]="110241936966089",
        ["mc bow"]="3442683707",
        ["skeet"]="5633695679",
        ["break"]="7149516994",
        ["rust"]="138750331387064",
        ["applepay"]="13956013041",
        ["bubble"]="6534947588",
        ["combobreak"]="2868331684",
        ["killcard"]="9102080552",
        ["xp"]="7147454322",
        ["na naxuy"]="4578740568",
        ["stony"]="3581383408",
        ["hentai"]="6442965016",
        ["TF2 Critical"]="296102734",
        ["Call of Duty"]="5952120301",
        ["Bat"]="3333907347",
        ["Saber"]="8415678813",
        ["Money"]="13956013041",
        ["LazerBeam"]="130791043",
        ["WindowsXPError"]="160715357",
        ["OSU"]="7147454322",
        ["Minecraft"]="5869422451",
        ["Among Us"]="5700183626",
        ["Bonk"]="5766898159",
        ["Vine"]="5332680810",
        ["Bruh"]="4578740568",
        ["Fatality"]="6534947869",
        ["Default Sound"]="330595293",
    }
    local SND_LIST={"primordial","neverlose","sparkle","mc bow","skeet","break","rust","applepay","bubble","combobreak","killcard","xp","na naxuy","stony","hentai","TF2 Critical","Call of Duty","Bat","Saber","Money","LazerBeam","WindowsXPError","OSU","Minecraft","Among Us","Bonk","Vine","Bruh","Fatality"}
    local snd_cache={}
    local function snd_download(name)
        local asset=SND_ASSETS[name]
        if not asset then return nil end
        asset=tostring(asset):gsub("^rbxassetid://", "")
        if not asset:match("^%d+$") then return nil end
        return "rbxassetid://"..asset
    end
    local SoundService=game:GetService("SoundService")
    local ContentProvider=game:GetService("ContentProvider")
    local function get_custom_sound(name)
        local id=snd_download(name)
        if not id then return nil end
        local cached=snd_cache[name]
        if cached and cached.Parent then return cached end
        local ok,sound=pcall(function()
            local obj=Instance.new("Sound")
            obj.Name="DaDaCustomSound_"..tostring(name):gsub("%W", "")
            obj.SoundId=id
            obj.Volume=1
            obj.Looped=false
            obj.Parent=SoundService
            return obj
        end)
        if not ok or not sound then return nil end
        snd_cache[name]=sound
        task.spawn(function() pcall(function() ContentProvider:PreloadAsync({sound}) end) end)
        return sound
    end
    local function snd_preload(name)
        local sound=get_custom_sound(name)
        if sound then task.spawn(function() pcall(function() ContentProvider:PreloadAsync({sound}) end) end) end
    end
    local function snd_play(name,vol)
        local sound=get_custom_sound(name)
        if not sound then return false end
        local ok=pcall(function()
            sound:Stop()
            sound.TimePosition=0
            sound.Volume=math.clamp(tonumber(vol) or 1,0,10)
            sound.Parent=SoundService
            sound:Play()
        end)
        return ok
    end
    SHF.snd_play=snd_play
    SHF.snd_preload=snd_preload
    SHF.snd_list=SND_LIST

    task.defer(function() snd_preload(SHF.gunshot_sound_name); snd_preload(SHF.sheriff_sound_name); snd_preload(SHF.kill_sound_name) end)

    local gun_conn=nil
    local function lp_has_gun_sh()
        local char=LP.Character
        if char and char:FindFirstChild("Gun") then return true end
        local bp=LP:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
        if bp and bp:FindFirstChild("Gun") then return true end
        return false
    end
    local function murderer_player()
        local m=get_round()
        local d=m and m.PlayerData
        if type(d)~="table" then return nil end
        for name,info in pairs(d) do
            if type(info)=="table" and info.Role=="Murderer" then return Players:FindFirstChild(name) end
        end
        return nil
    end
    -- Mute original weapon sounds as they are created/played, not only after the remote arrives.
    local mutedOriginalSounds={}
    local function isOriginalGunshot(sound)
        if not sound or not sound:IsA("Sound") then return false end
        if sound.Name:lower():find("dadacustomsound",1,true) then return false end
        local n=sound.Name:lower()
        local parent=sound.Parent
        local inGun=false
        local char=LP.Character
        local gun=char and char:FindFirstChild("Gun")
        pcall(function() inGun=gun~=nil and (sound==gun or sound:IsDescendantOf(gun)) end)
        local looksLikeShot=n:find("gunshot",1,true)~=nil or n:find("shoot",1,true)~=nil
            or n:find("fire",1,true)~=nil or n=="gun" or n=="shot" or n:find("shot",1,true)~=nil
        return looksLikeShot and (inGun or parent==SoundService or (char and sound:IsDescendantOf(char)))
    end
    local function silenceOne(sound)
        if not SHF.gunshot_sound or not isOriginalGunshot(sound) then return end
        pcall(function()
            sound.Volume=0
            sound:Stop()
            mutedOriginalSounds[sound]=true
        end)
    end
    local function silenceOriginalGunshot()
        if not SHF.gunshot_sound then return end
        local char=LP.Character
        local gun=char and char:FindFirstChild("Gun")
        if gun then for _,obj in ipairs(gun:GetDescendants()) do silenceOne(obj) end end
        if char then for _,obj in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do silenceOne(obj) end end
        for _,obj in ipairs(SoundService:GetDescendants()) do silenceOne(obj) end
    end
    SHF.silenceOriginalGunshot=silenceOriginalGunshot
    SoundService.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
        if SHF.gunshot_sound and obj:IsA("Sound") then task.defer(function() silenceOne(obj) end) end
    end)
    local function playCustomGunshot()
        if not SHF.gunshot_sound then return end
        silenceOriginalGunshot()
        snd_play(SHF.gunshot_sound_name,SHF.gunshot_sound_vol)
        -- A few games start the original Sound after Tool.Activated; mute it again next frame.
        task.defer(silenceOriginalGunshot)
    end
    local function on_shot_sh()
        -- This callback comes from the game's GunFired event and remains responsible for kill sounds.
        if SHF.gunshot_sound then silenceOriginalGunshot() end
        if not (SHF.kill_sound or SHF.sheriff_sound) then return end
        local role=my_role()
        local mp=murderer_player()
        if not mp then return end
        local mname=mp.Name
        task.delay(0.7,function()
            local m=get_round()
            local d=m and m.PlayerData
            local info=d and d[mname]
            local tgt=Players:FindFirstChild(mname)
            local hum=tgt and tgt.Character and tgt.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local killed=(info and info.Dead==true) or (hum~=nil and hum.Health<=0)
            if killed then
                if role=="Sheriff" or role=="Hero" or lp_has_gun_sh() then
                    if SHF.sheriff_sound then snd_play(SHF.sheriff_sound_name,SHF.sheriff_sound_vol) end
                elseif role=="Murderer" then
                    if SHF.kill_sound then snd_play(SHF.kill_sound_name,SHF.kill_sound_vol) end
                end
            end
        end)
    end
    -- Tool.Activated starts the replacement immediately; the remote event is retained for kill detection.
    local activatedConnections={}
    local function hookGunTool(tool)
        if not tool or not tool:IsA("Tool") or tool.Name~="Gun" or activatedConnections[tool] then return end
        activatedConnections[tool]=tool.Activated:Connect(function()
            if SHF.gunshot_sound then playCustomGunshot() end
        end)
    end
    local function scanGunTools()
        local char=LP.Character
        local bp=LP:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
        if char then
            for _,obj in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do hookGunTool(obj) end
            char.ChildAdded:Connect(function(obj) hookGunTool(obj) end)
        end
        if bp then
            for _,obj in ipairs(bp:GetChildren()) do hookGunTool(obj) end
            bp.ChildAdded:Connect(function(obj) hookGunTool(obj) end)
        end
    end
    scanGunTools()
    LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
        task.wait(0.5)
        scanGunTools()
    end)
    local function ensure_gun_hook_sh()
        if gun_conn then return end
        local ok,remote=pcall(function()
            return ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("ClientServices"):WaitForChild("WeaponService"):WaitForChild("GunFired")
        end)
        if not ok or not remote then return end
        gun_conn=remote.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(gun)
            local char=LP.Character
            if typeof(gun)=="Instance" and char and gun:IsDescendantOf(char) then
                on_shot_sh()
            end
        end)
    end
    local lastDeadState={}
    task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            local ok,round=pcall(get_round)
            local data=ok and round and round.PlayerData
            if type(data)=="table" then
                local role=my_role()
                if role=="Murderer" and SHF.kill_sound then
                    for playerName,info in pairs(data) do
                        if type(info)=="table" then
                            local dead=info.Dead==true
                            if dead and lastDeadState[playerName]~=true and playerName~=LP.Name then
                                snd_play(SHF.kill_sound_name, SHF.kill_sound_vol)
                            end
                            lastDeadState[playerName]=dead
                        end
                    end
                else
                    for playerName,info in pairs(data) do
                        if type(info)=="table" then lastDeadState[playerName]=info.Dead==true end
                    end
                end
            end
            if SHF.gunshot_sound or SHF.kill_sound or SHF.sheriff_sound then
                ensure_gun_hook_sh()
            end
            task.wait(0.25)
        end
    end)
end
initNotifications()-- ============================================================
-- INIT 14: МЕНЮ
-- ============================================================
-- DaDa UI v5: native menu; gameplay code and config IDs preserved.
local function createDaDaV5()

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local UIS = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local TS = game:GetService("TweenService")
local RS = game:GetService("RunService")
local Stats = game:GetService("Stats")
local LP = Players.LocalPlayer

local GUI_NAME = "DaDaMenu_V5"
local W, H = 620, 440
local V2 = Vector2.new

local Library = { Flags = {}, Connections = {}, Unloaded = false }

local Theme = {
	Accent = Color3.fromRGB(235, 40, 75),
	Text = Color3.fromRGB(236, 226, 229),
	Dim = Color3.fromRGB(135, 116, 123),
	Item = Color3.fromRGB(32, 22, 27),
	Panel = Color3.fromRGB(20, 12, 16),
}

-- ============ helpers ============
local function clamp(x, a, b) return x < a and a or (x > b and b or x) end

local function tw(o, t, p, style, dir)
	local tr = TS:Create(o, TweenInfo.new(t, style or Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, dir or Enum.EasingDirection.Out), p)
	tr:Play()
	return tr
end

local function new(class, props, children)
	local o = Instance.new(class)
	props = props or {}
	local parent = props.Parent
	if o:IsA("GuiObject") then o.BorderSizePixel = 0 end
	for k, v in pairs(props) do
		if k ~= "Parent" then o[k] = v end
	end
	for _, c in ipairs(children or {}) do c.Parent = o end
	if parent then o.Parent = parent end
	return o
end

local function corner(r) return new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, r) }) end

local function stroke(color, thick, trans)
	return new("UIStroke", {
		Color = color, Thickness = thick or 1, Transparency = trans or 0,
		ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border,
	})
end

local function text(p)
	p.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	p.Font = p.Font or Enum.Font.Gotham
	p.TextSize = p.TextSize or 11
	p.TextColor3 = p.TextColor3 or Theme.Text
	p.TextXAlignment = p.TextXAlignment or Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	p.TextTruncate = p.TextTruncate or Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
	return new("TextLabel", p)
end

local function conn(signal, fn)
	local c = signal:Connect(function(...)
		if not Library.Unloaded then return fn(...) end
	end)
	table.insert(Library.Connections, c)
	return c
end

local function safe(fn, ...)
	if Library.Unloaded then return end
	if type(fn) ~= "function" then return end
	local ok2, e = pcall(fn, ...)
	if not ok2 then warn("[DaDa] callback: " .. tostring(e)) end
end

local function grad(parent, c0, c1, rot)
	return new("UIGradient", { Color = ColorSequence.new(c0, c1), Rotation = rot or 90, Parent = parent })
end

local themed = {}
local function accent(o, prop, fn)
	fn = fn or function(c) return c end
	table.insert(themed, { o = o, p = prop, f = fn })
	o[prop] = fn(Theme.Accent)
end

local function setAccent(c)
	Theme.Accent = c
	for i = #themed, 1, -1 do
		local t = themed[i]
		if t.o and t.o.Parent then
			tw(t.o, 0.25, { [t.p] = t.f(c) })
		else
			table.remove(themed, i)
		end
	end
end

local function draggable(handle, target)
	local dragging, start, startPos, moved = false, nil, nil, false
	conn(handle.InputBegan, function(i)
		if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			dragging, moved = true, false
			start, startPos = i.Position, target.Position
			local c
			c = i.Changed:Connect(function()
				if i.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
					dragging = false
					c:Disconnect()
				end
			end)
		end
	end)
	conn(UIS.InputChanged, function(i)
		if dragging and (i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
			local d = i.Position - start
			if d.Magnitude > 6 then moved = true end
			target.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + d.X, startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + d.Y)
		end
	end)
	return function() return moved end
end

-- ============ gui root ============
for _, host in ipairs({ (gethui and gethui()) or nil, game:GetService("CoreGui"), LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui") }) do
	pcall(function()
		local old = host:FindFirstChild(GUI_NAME)
		if old then old:Destroy() end
	end)
end

local Gui = new("ScreenGui", {
	Name = GUI_NAME, ResetOnSpawn = false, IgnoreGuiInset = true,
	ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling, DisplayOrder = 999,
})
do
	local placed = false
	if gethui then placed = pcall(function() Gui.Parent = gethui() end) end
	if not placed then placed = pcall(function() Gui.Parent = game:GetService("CoreGui") end) end
	if not placed or not Gui.Parent then Gui.Parent = LP:WaitForChild("PlayerGui") end
end

-- ============ notifications ============
local NotifyHolder = new("Frame", {
	Size = UDim2.new(0, 220, 1, -20), Position = UDim2.new(1, -230, 0, 10),
	BackgroundTransparency = 1, Parent = Gui,
}, { new("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 6), VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Bottom, SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }) })

function Library:Notify(title, msg, dur)
	if Library.Unloaded or not Gui.Parent then return end
	title, msg = tostring(title or ""), tostring(msg or "")
	dur = math.clamp(tonumber(dur) or 2, 0.6, 2)
	local textService = game:GetService("TextService")
	local textWidth = 196
	local titleHeight = math.max(14, textService:GetTextSize(title, 11, Enum.Font.GothamBold, Vector2.new(textWidth, 100000)).Y)
	local messageHeight = math.max(14, textService:GetTextSize(msg, 10, Enum.Font.Gotham, Vector2.new(textWidth, 100000)).Y)
	local cardHeight = math.max(44, titleHeight + messageHeight + 16)
	local holder = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, cardHeight), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Parent = NotifyHolder })
	local f = new("Frame", {
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(1, 20, 0, 0),
		BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(18, 11, 14), Parent = holder,
	}, { corner(7) })
	local st = stroke(Theme.Accent, 1, 0.3); st.Parent = f
	accent(st, "Color")
	local bar = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 1, -12), Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 6), Parent = f }, { corner(2) })
	accent(bar, "BackgroundColor3")
	text({ Text = tostring(title), Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 11, Position = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 6), Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, titleHeight), TextWrapped = true, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None, Parent = f })
	text({ Text = tostring(msg or ""), TextSize = 10, TextColor3 = Theme.Dim, Position = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 8 + titleHeight), Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, messageHeight), TextWrapped = true, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None, Parent = f })
	tw(f, 0.3, { Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
	task.delay(dur, function()
		if not f.Parent then return end
		local t = tw(f, 0.3, { Position = UDim2.new(1, 20, 0, 0) })
		t.Completed:Wait()
		holder:Destroy()
	end)
end

-- ============ tab icons (drawn from frames) ============
local function makeIcon(par, kind)
	local h = new("Frame", {
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 14), Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0.5, -7),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1, Parent = par,
	})
	local function ring(cx, cy, w, hh, r, th)
		local o = new("Frame", {
			Position = UDim2.fromOffset(cx, cy), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, hh), AnchorPoint = V2(0.5, 0.5),
			BackgroundTransparency = 1, Parent = h,
		}, { corner(r) })
		local st = stroke(Theme.Accent, th or 1.5, 0); st.Parent = o
		accent(st, "Color")
		return o
	end
	local function fillb(cx, cy, w, hh, r, rot)
		local o = new("Frame", {
			Position = UDim2.fromOffset(cx, cy), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, hh), AnchorPoint = V2(0.5, 0.5),
			Rotation = rot or 0, Parent = h,
		}, { corner(r or 1) })
		accent(o, "BackgroundColor3")
		return o
	end
	if kind == "game" then
		ring(7, 7, 14, 9, 3)
		fillb(4.5, 7, 4, 1.5); fillb(4.5, 7, 1.5, 4)
		fillb(10, 6, 2, 2, 1); fillb(11.5, 8.5, 2, 2, 1)
	elseif kind == "visuals" then
		ring(7, 7, 14, 8, 4)
		fillb(7, 7, 4, 4, 2)
	elseif kind == "world" then
		ring(7, 7, 12, 12, 6)
		ring(7, 7, 5, 12, 3, 1)
		fillb(7, 7, 12, 1)
	elseif kind == "player" then
		fillb(7, 4, 5, 5, 3)
		fillb(7, 10.5, 10, 6, 4)
	elseif kind == "models" then
		local d = ring(7, 7, 9, 9, 1, 1.5); d.Rotation = 45
		fillb(7, 7, 3, 3, 1)
	elseif kind == "animations" then
		ring(7, 7, 13, 13, 7, 1.5)
		fillb(8, 7, 5, 5, 1, 45)
	elseif kind == "emotes" then
		fillb(7, 7, 14, 3, 1); fillb(7, 7, 3, 14, 1)
		fillb(7, 7, 14, 3, 1, 45); fillb(7, 7, 14, 3, 1, -45)
		local c = ring(7, 7, 9, 9, 5, 1.5)
		c.BackgroundTransparency = 0
		c.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(22, 12, 16)
	elseif kind == "target" then
		ring(7, 7, 11, 11, 6, 1.5)
		fillb(7, 7, 3, 3, 2)
		fillb(7, 1, 1.5, 3); fillb(7, 13, 1.5, 3)
		fillb(1, 7, 3, 1.5); fillb(13, 7, 3, 1.5)
	else
		ring(7, 7, 9, 9, 5, 1.5)
	end
	return h
end

-- ============ window ============
function Library:CreateWindow(cfg)
	cfg = cfg or {}
	local Window = { Tabs = {}, Current = nil, Keybinds = {}, Visible = true, UserScale = 1, ExternalToggleKeys = true }
	local logoLetter = cfg.Name or "D"

	local Main = new("Frame", {
		Name = "Main", Size = UDim2.fromOffset(W, H), AnchorPoint = V2(0.5, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), Parent = Gui,
	}, { corner(12) })
	grad(Main, Color3.fromRGB(28, 11, 18), Color3.fromRGB(9, 7, 9), 60)
	local mst = stroke(Theme.Accent, 1.5, 0.15); mst.Parent = Main
	accent(mst, "Color")
	new("UIGradient", {
		Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
			NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0.65), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0),
		}), Rotation = 45, Parent = mst,
	})

	local scale = new("UIScale", { Parent = Main })
	local viewportConn
	local function updateResponsiveScale()
		local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
		if not cam or not Main.Parent then return end
		local vp = cam.ViewportSize
		local fit = math.max(0.15, math.min((vp.X - 16) / W, (vp.Y - 24) / H))
		scale.Scale = math.min(fit, math.max(0.15, math.min(fit, 1) * Window.UserScale))
	end
	Window.UpdateScale = updateResponsiveScale
	function Window:SetScale(value) self.UserScale = value; updateResponsiveScale() end
	updateResponsiveScale()
	local function bindViewportCamera()
		if viewportConn then viewportConn:Disconnect(); viewportConn = nil end
		local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
		if cam then
			viewportConn = cam:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ViewportSize"):Connect(updateResponsiveScale)
			table.insert(Library.Connections, viewportConn)
		end
	end
	bindViewportCamera()
	conn(workspace:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CurrentCamera"), function()
		bindViewportCamera()
		updateResponsiveScale()
	end)

	-- glowing rings around the logo letter
	local function halo(size, thick)
		local o = new("Frame", {
			Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size, size), AnchorPoint = V2(0.5, 0.5),
			Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 40), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Parent = Side,
		}, { corner(size) })
		local st = stroke(Theme.Accent, thick, 0.6); st.Parent = o
		accent(st, "Color")
		return st
	end
	local h1, h2 = halo(64, 1.5), halo(72, 1)
	local sweep = new("UIGradient", {
		Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
			NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.35, 0.85),
			NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.7, 0.85), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0),
		}), Parent = lst,
	})
	local ht = 0
	conn(RS.RenderStepped, function(dt)
		if not Main.Visible then return end
		ht += dt
		h1.Transparency = 0.5 + 0.3 * math.sin(ht * 2.2)
		h2.Transparency = 0.72 + 0.22 * math.sin(ht * 2.2 + 1.6)
		sweep.Rotation = (ht * 120) % 360
	end)
	local lt = text({

	accent(lt, "TextColor3")
	text({
		Text = cfg.Subtitle or "MIRROR SI EDITION", TextSize = 8, TextColor3 = Theme.Dim, Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium,
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 78), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 12), Parent = Side,
	})

	local TabList = new("ScrollingFrame", {
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, 98), Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 1, -98 - 58),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1, ScrollBarThickness = 0, CanvasSize = UDim2.new(),
		AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, Parent = Side,
	}, { new("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 4), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }) })

	-- profile

		BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Item, Image = "", Parent = Prof,
	}, { corner(15) })
	task.spawn(function()
		pcall(function()
			Av.Image = Players:GetUserThumbnailAsync(LP.UserId, Enum.ThumbnailType.HeadShot, Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size100x100)
		end)

	text({ Text = LP.DisplayName, Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 9, Position = UDim2.new(0, 44, 0, 8), Size = UDim2.new(1, -48, 0, 14), TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, Parent = Prof })
	text({ Text = "@" .. LP.Name, TextSize = 8, TextColor3 = Theme.Dim, Position = UDim2.new(0, 44, 0, 22), Size = UDim2.new(1, -48, 0, 12), TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, Parent = Prof })

	-- top bar

		Rotation = 45, Parent = Top,
	})
	accent(mh, "BackgroundColor3")

	local Search = new("TextBox", {
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -230, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0, 30, 0, 0),

	local FPS = text({
		Text = "60 FPS", Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 8, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
		Position = UDim2.new(1, -54, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0, 46, 1, 0), Parent = Top,
	})
	accent(FPS, "TextColor3")

	local dotsHolder = new("Frame", {
		Size = UDim2.new(0, 110, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(1, -168, 0, 0),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1, Parent = Top,
	}, { new("UIListLayout", {
		FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal, Padding = UDim.new(0, 6),
		VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center, HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right,

	local palette = {

	-- content
	local Content = new("Frame", {
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -174, 1, -60), Position = UDim2.new(0, 166, 0, 52),
		BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), ClipsDescendants = true, Parent = Main,
	}, { corner(9) })
	grad(Content, Color3.fromRGB(22, 10, 15), Color3.fromRGB(10, 7, 9), 70)
	local cst = stroke(Theme.Accent, 1, 0.5); cst.Parent = Content
	accent(cst, "Color")

	-- ===== smooth constellation (persistent lines, no pool reassign => no teleporting) =====
	do
		local layer = new("Frame", {
			Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1, ClipsDescendants = true, ZIndex = 1, Parent = Content,
		})
		local N, MAXD = 20, 135
		local pts, dots, lines = {}, {}, {}
		for i = 1, N do
			pts[i] = {
				x = math.random(), y = math.random(), a = math.random() * 6.2832,
				s = 0.03 + math.random() * 0.035, turn = (math.random() - 0.5) * 0.7,
			}
			local ds = 3 + math.random(0, 2)
			dots[i] = new("Frame", {
				Size = UDim2.fromOffset(ds, ds), AnchorPoint = V2(0.5, 0.5),
				BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Accent, BackgroundTransparency = 0.05, ZIndex = 1, Parent = layer,
			}, { corner(3) })
		end
		local pairsList = {}
		for i = 1, N - 1 do
			for j = i + 1, N do
				local l = new("Frame", {
					AnchorPoint = V2(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 1), BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Accent,
					Visible = false, ZIndex = 1, Parent = layer,
				})
				table.insert(pairsList, { i = i, j = j, l = l })
			end
		end
		local PI = math.pi
		local px, py = {}, {}
		conn(RS.RenderStepped, function(dt)
			if not Main.Visible then return end
			dt = math.min(dt, 0.05)
			local sz = layer.AbsoluteSize
			if sz.X < 2 or sz.Y < 2 then return end
			local aspect = sz.X / sz.Y
			for i = 1, N do
				local p = pts[i]
				p.a += p.turn * dt
				p.x += math.cos(p.a) * p.s * dt / aspect * 1.4
				p.y += math.sin(p.a) * p.s * dt * 1.4
				if p.x < 0.02 then p.x = 0.02; p.a = PI - p.a elseif p.x > 0.98 then p.x = 0.98; p.a = PI - p.a end
				if p.y < 0.02 then p.y = 0.02; p.a = -p.a elseif p.y > 0.98 then p.y = 0.98; p.a = -p.a end
				px[i], py[i] = p.x * sz.X, p.y * sz.Y
				dots[i].Position = UDim2.new(p.x, 0, p.y, 0)
			end
			local ac = Theme.Accent
			-- Keep constellation points synchronized with the live accent picker.
			for i = 1, N do
				dots[i].BackgroundColor3 = ac
			end
			for _, pr in ipairs(pairsList) do
				local dx, dy = px[pr.j] - px[pr.i], py[pr.j] - py[pr.i]
				local d = math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy)
				local l = pr.l
				if d < MAXD then
					local a = 1 - d / MAXD
					l.Visible = true
					l.BackgroundColor3 = ac
					l.BackgroundTransparency = 1 - a * 0.75
					l.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(d, 1)
					l.Position = UDim2.new((px[pr.i] + px[pr.j]) / 2 / sz.X, 0, (py[pr.i] + py[pr.j]) / 2 / sz.Y, 0)
					l.Rotation = math.deg(math.atan2(dy, dx))
				elseif l.Visible then
					l.Visible = false
				end
			end
		end)
	end

	-- fps
	do
		local frames, last = 0, os.clock()
		conn(RS.RenderStepped, function()
			frames += 1
			local now = os.clock()
			if now - last >= 0.5 then
				FPS.Text = math.floor(frames / (now - last)) .. " FPS"
				frames, last = 0, now
			end
		end)
	end

	-- search
	local function applySearch()
		local q = Search.Text:lower()
		for _, tab in ipairs(Window.Tabs) do
			for _, sec in ipairs(tab.Sections) do
				local any = false
				for _, it in ipairs(sec.Items) do
					local m = q == "" or it.name:find(q, 1, true) ~= nil or sec.Title:lower():find(q, 1, true) ~= nil
					it.frame.Visible = m
					if m then any = true end
				end
				sec.Frame.Visible = any or q == ""
			end
		end
	end
	conn(Search:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"), applySearch)

	draggable(Top, Main)
	draggable(Logo, Main)

	conn(Main:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"), function()
		Window.Visible = Main.Visible
		if not Main.Visible then Window.ActiveSlider = nil end
	end)
	function Window:SetVisible(v)
		Window.Visible = v
		Main.Visible = v
	end
	function Window:Toggle() Window:SetVisible(not Main.Visible) end

	conn(UIS.InputBegan, function(i, gp)
		if Library.Unloaded then return end
		if i.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then return end
		if not Window.ExternalToggleKeys and not gp and i.KeyCode == (Window.ToggleKey or Enum.KeyCode.RightShift) then
			Window:Toggle()
			return
		end
		if gp then return end
		for _, kb in ipairs(Window.Keybinds) do
			if not kb.Listening and kb.Key and kb.Key == i.KeyCode then safe(kb.Callback) end
		end
	end)

	-- Shared slider router also tracks the exact touch, so a second finger cannot move it.
	conn(UIS.InputChanged, function(input)
		local active = Window.ActiveSlider
		if not active or not active.bar.Parent then return end
		local touch = active.input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
		if (touch and input == active.input) or (not touch and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement) then
			active.move(input.Position.X)
		end
	end)
	conn(UIS.InputEnded, function(input)
		local active = Window.ActiveSlider
		if active and (input == active.input or (active.input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1)) then
			Window.ActiveSlider = nil
		end
	end)

	-- compact semi-transparent DaDa hotbar: tap to toggle menu
	do
		local hotbar = new("TextButton", {
			Name = "DaDaHotbar", Size = UDim2.fromOffset(258, 25),
			Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0.4, 0),
			BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(13, 8, 14),
			BackgroundTransparency = 0.22, Text = "",
			AutoButtonColor = false, ClipsDescendants = true, Parent = Gui,
		}, { corner(12) })
		local hs = stroke(Theme.Accent, 1.2, 0.12); hs.Parent = hotbar
		accent(hs, "Color")
		local bgGrad = grad(hotbar, Color3.fromRGB(34, 14, 36), Color3.fromRGB(10, 8, 12), 0)
		bgGrad.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
			NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.18),
			NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.18),
		})
		local brand = text({
			Text = "DaDa", Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 10,
			TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
			Position = UDim2.fromOffset(7, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(38, 25),
			Parent = hotbar,
		})
		accent(brand, "TextColor3")
		local fpsLabel = text({
			Text = "-- FPS", Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 9,
			Position = UDim2.fromOffset(50, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(50, 25),
			Parent = hotbar,
		})
		local pingLabel = text({
			Text = "-- ms", Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 9,
			Position = UDim2.fromOffset(105, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(47, 25),
			Parent = hotbar,
		})
		local separator = new("Frame", {
			Position = UDim2.fromOffset(156, 6), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(1, 13),
			BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Accent, BackgroundTransparency = 0.3, Parent = hotbar,
		})
		accent(separator, "BackgroundColor3")
		local avatar = new("ImageLabel", {
			Name = "UserAvatar", BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			Size = UDim2.fromOffset(19, 19), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(162, 3),
			ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop, Parent = hotbar,
		}, { corner(10) })
		local avatarStroke = stroke(Theme.Accent, 1, 0.2); avatarStroke.Parent = avatar
		accent(avatarStroke, "Color")
		pcall(function()
			local image = Players:GetUserThumbnailAsync(
				LP.UserId, Enum.ThumbnailType.HeadShot, Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size48x48
			)
			avatar.Image = image
		end)
		local username = text({
			Text = LP.Name, Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 9,
			TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
			TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
			Position = UDim2.fromOffset(186, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(64, 25),
			Parent = hotbar,
		})
		local dot = new("Frame", {
			Size = UDim2.fromOffset(3, 3), Position = UDim2.new(1, -7, 0.5, -1),
			BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Accent, Parent = hotbar,
		}, { corner(3) })
		accent(dot, "BackgroundColor3")
		local pulseClock = 0
		local wasMoved = draggable(hotbar, hotbar)
		conn(hotbar.MouseButton1Click, function()
			if not wasMoved() then
				tw(hotbar, 0.1, {Size = UDim2.fromOffset(252, 23)})
				task.delay(0.1, function()
					if hotbar.Parent then tw(hotbar, 0.14, {Size = UDim2.fromOffset(258, 25)}) end
				end)
				Window:Toggle()
			end
		end)
		local frameCount, frameStamp = 0, os.clock()
		conn(RS.RenderStepped, function(dt)
			pulseClock += dt
			hs.Transparency = 0.12 + (math.sin(pulseClock * 2.4) + 1) * 0.12
			frameCount += 1
			local now = os.clock()
			if now - frameStamp >= 0.25 then
				local fps = math.floor(frameCount / (now - frameStamp) + 0.5)
				fpsLabel.Text = tostring(fps) .. " FPS"
				frameCount, frameStamp = 0, now
				local ping = "—"
				pcall(function()
					local stat = Stats.Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]
					ping = tostring(math.floor(stat:GetValue() + 0.5))
				end)
				pingLabel.Text = ping .. " ms"
			end
		end)
	end

	-- ============ tabs ============
	local pages = Instance.new("Folder")
	pages.Parent = Content

	local function styleTab(t, act)
		for _, tr in ipairs(t.Tw) do tr:Cancel() end
		t.Tw = {}
		local function g(o, p) table.insert(t.Tw, tw(o, 0.22, p)) end
		g(t.Btn, { BackgroundTransparency = act and 0.68 or 1 })
		g(t.BtnStroke, { Transparency = act and 0.35 or 1 })
		t.Bar.Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0, 0)
		t.Bar.Visible = act
		if act then g(t.Bar, { Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0, 20) }) end
	end

	function Window:SelectTab(tab)
		if Window.Current == tab then return end
		for _, t in ipairs(Window.Tabs) do
			styleTab(t, t == tab)
			t.Page.Visible = (t == tab)
		end
		tab.Page.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 10)
		tw(tab.Page, 0.25, { Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0) })
		Window.Current = tab
	end

	function Window:AddTab(name, sub, icon)
		local Tab = { Sections = {}, Count = { left = 0, right = 0 }, Name = name, Tw = {} }

		local Btn = new("TextButton", {
			Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 38), BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Accent, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, LayoutOrder = #Window.Tabs, Parent = TabList,
		}, { corner(7) })
		accent(Btn, "BackgroundColor3")
		local bst = stroke(Theme.Accent, 1, 1); bst.Parent = Btn
		accent(bst, "Color")
		local Bar = new("Frame", {
			Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0, 0), AnchorPoint = V2(0, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, 0),
			Visible = false, Parent = Btn,
		}, { corner(2) })
		accent(Bar, "BackgroundColor3")
		makeIcon(Btn, icon or tostring(name):lower())
		text({
			Text = name, Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 10,
			Position = UDim2.new(0, 30, 0, 6), Size = UDim2.new(1, -34, 0, 14), Parent = Btn,
		})
		text({
			Text = sub or "", TextSize = 8, TextColor3 = Theme.Dim,
			Position = UDim2.new(0, 30, 0, 20), Size = UDim2.new(1, -34, 0, 12), Parent = Btn,
		})
		conn(Btn.MouseEnter, function()
			if Window.Current ~= Tab then tw(Btn, 0.15, { BackgroundTransparency = 0.9 }) end
		end)
		conn(Btn.MouseLeave, function()
			if Window.Current ~= Tab then tw(Btn, 0.15, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }) end
		end)

		local Page = new("ScrollingFrame", {
			Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1, ScrollBarThickness = 2,
			ScrollBarImageColor3 = Theme.Accent, CanvasSize = UDim2.new(),
			AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, Visible = false, ZIndex = 2, Parent = pages,
		}, { new("UIPadding", { PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 8), PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 8), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 8), PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 8) }) })
		accent(Page, "ScrollBarImageColor3")

		local Left = new("Frame", {
			Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -4, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Parent = Page,
		}, { new("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 8), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }) })
		local Right = new("Frame", {
			Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -4, 0, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 4, 0, 0),
			AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Parent = Page,
		}, { new("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 8), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }) })

		Tab.Btn, Tab.BtnStroke, Tab.Bar, Tab.Page = Btn, bst, Bar, Page
		Tab.Left, Tab.Right = Left, Right
		conn(Btn.MouseButton1Click, function() Window:SelectTab(Tab) end)

		-- ============ sections ============
		function Tab:AddSection(title, side)
			side = side or (Tab.Count.left <= Tab.Count.right and "left" or "right")
			Tab.Count[side] += 1
			local Section = { Items = {}, Counter = 0, Title = tostring(title) }

			local Frame = new("Frame", {
				Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
				BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(16, 9, 12), BackgroundTransparency = 0.15,
				LayoutOrder = Tab.Count[side], Parent = (side == "left") and Left or Right,
			}, { corner(7), new("UIListLayout", { SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }) })
			local fst2 = stroke(Theme.Accent, 1, 0.5); fst2.Parent = Frame
			accent(fst2, "Color")

			local Head = new("TextButton", {
				Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 26), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false,
				LayoutOrder = 1, Parent = Frame,
			})
			text({ Text = title, Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 10, Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -30, 1, 0), Parent = Head })
			local Arrow = text({
				Text = "v", Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 9, TextColor3 = Theme.Dim,
				TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, Position = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 26), Parent = Head,
			})
			local hl = new("Frame", {
				Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 1), Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 1, -1),
				BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Accent, BackgroundTransparency = 0.6, Parent = Head,
			})
			accent(hl, "BackgroundColor3")

			local Body = new("Frame", {
				Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
				LayoutOrder = 2, Parent = Frame,
			}, {
				new("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 3), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }),
				new("UIPadding", { PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 6), PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 8), PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 10), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 10) }),
			})
			local open = true
			conn(Head.MouseButton1Click, function()
				open = not open
				Body.Visible = open
				Arrow.Text = open and "v" or ">"
			end)

			Section.Frame, Section.Body = Frame, Body
			table.insert(Tab.Sections, Section)

			local function item(name, h, noSearch)
				Section.Counter += 1
				local f = new("Frame", {
					Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, h), BackgroundTransparency = 1,
					LayoutOrder = Section.Counter, Parent = Body,
				})
				if not noSearch then table.insert(Section.Items, { name = tostring(name):lower(), frame = f }) end
				return f
			end

			function Section:Mount(frame, name)
				Section.Counter += 1
				frame.LayoutOrder = Section.Counter
				frame.Parent = Body
				table.insert(Section.Items, {name = tostring(name or title):lower(), frame = frame})
				return frame
			end

			local function flagOf(o, name)
				return o.Flag or (title .. "/" .. name .. "#" .. (Section.Counter + 1))
			end

			function Section:AddLabel(t)
				local f = item(t, 18)
				local l = text({ Text = t, TextColor3 = Theme.Dim, TextSize = 10, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), Parent = f })
				return { Set = function(_, v) l.Text = tostring(v) end, Frame = f, Label = l }
			end

			-- toggle
			function Section:AddToggle(o)
				local name = o.Name or "toggle"
				local flag = flagOf(o, name)
				local state = o.Default and true or false
				Library.Flags[flag] = state
				local f = item(name, 22)
				local btn = new("TextButton", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, Parent = f })
				local box2 = new("Frame", {
					Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 14), Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, -7),
					BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Item, Parent = btn,
				}, { corner(4) })
				local bs = stroke(Theme.Accent, 1, 0.55); bs.Parent = box2
				accent(bs, "Color")
				local fill = new("Frame", {
					Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0), AnchorPoint = V2(0.5, 0.5),
					BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Accent, Parent = box2,
				}, { corner(4) })
				accent(fill, "BackgroundColor3")
				local chk = text({
					Text = "v", Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 9, TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
					TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), TextTransparency = 1, ZIndex = 2, Parent = box2,
				})
				local lbl = text({
					Text = name, TextColor3 = state and Theme.Text or Theme.Dim,
					Position = UDim2.new(0, 24, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -44, 1, 0), Parent = btn,
				})
				if o.More then
					text({
						Text = "...", Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 11, TextColor3 = Theme.Dim,
						TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, Position = UDim2.new(1, -18, 0, -3), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(18, 22), Parent = btn,
					})
				end
				local T = {}
				local function render(anim)
					local sz = state and UDim2.fromOffset(14, 14) or UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
					local col = state and Theme.Text or Theme.Dim
					local ct = state and 0 or 1
					if anim then
						tw(fill, 0.18, { Size = sz }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
						tw(lbl, 0.18, { TextColor3 = col })
						tw(chk, 0.18, { TextTransparency = ct })
					else
						fill.Size, lbl.TextColor3, chk.TextTransparency = sz, col, ct
					end
				end
				function T:Set(v, silent)
					state = v and true or false
					Library.Flags[flag] = state
					render(true)
					if not silent then safe(o.Callback, state) end
				end
				function T:Get() return state end
				conn(btn.MouseButton1Click, function() T:Set(not state) end)
				conn(btn.MouseEnter, function() if not state then tw(lbl, 0.12, { TextColor3 = Theme.Text }) end end)
				conn(btn.MouseLeave, function() if not state then tw(lbl, 0.12, { TextColor3 = Theme.Dim }) end end)
				render(false)
				return T
			end

			local function makeBar(parent, pos, size, init, cb)
				local bar = new("TextButton", {
					Position = pos, Size = size, BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Item, Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, Parent = parent,
				}, { corner(3) })
				local fill = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(init, 0, 1, 0), BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Accent, Parent = bar }, { corner(3) })
				accent(fill, "BackgroundColor3")
				local knob = new("Frame", {
					Size = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 8), AnchorPoint = V2(0.5, 0.5),
					Position = UDim2.new(init, 0, 0.5, 0), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), ZIndex = 3, Parent = bar,
				}, { corner(4) })
				local dragging = false
				local function setA(a, silent)
					a = clamp(a, 0, 1)
					fill.Size = UDim2.new(a, 0, 1, 0)
					knob.Position = UDim2.new(a, 0, 0.5, 0)
					if not silent then cb(a) end
				end
				local function fromX(x) setA((x - bar.AbsolutePosition.X) / math.max(bar.AbsoluteSize.X, 1)) end
				conn(bar.InputBegan, function(i)
					if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
						dragging = true
						Window.ActiveSlider = {input = i, move = fromX, bar = bar}
						fromX(i.Position.X)
					end
				end)
				return bar, fill, knob, setA
			end

			function Section:AddSlider(o)
				local name = o.Name or "slider"
				local flag = flagOf(o, name)
				local min, max, inc = o.Min or 0, o.Max or 100, o.Increment or 1
				local val = clamp(o.Default or min, min, max)
				Library.Flags[flag] = val
				local f = item(name, 34)
				text({ Text = name, TextColor3 = Theme.Dim, Size = UDim2.new(1, -50, 0, 16), Parent = f })
				local vl = text({
					Text = tostring(val) .. (o.Suffix or ""), TextColor3 = Theme.Text, Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 10,
					TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, Position = UDim2.new(1, -50, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0, 50, 0, 16), Parent = f,
				})
				local T = {}
				local setA
				local bar, fill, knob
				bar, fill, knob, setA = makeBar(f, UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 22), UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 6), (val - min) / (max - min), function(a)
					local v = min + (max - min) * a
					v = math.floor(v / inc + 0.5) * inc
					v = tonumber(string.format("%.4f", v))
					val = clamp(v, min, max)
					Library.Flags[flag] = val
					vl.Text = tostring(val) .. (o.Suffix or "")
					if setA then setA((val - min) / (max - min), true) end
					safe(o.Callback, val)
				end)
				function T:Set(v, silent)
					val = clamp(v, min, max)
					Library.Flags[flag] = val
					vl.Text = tostring(val) .. (o.Suffix or "")
					setA((val - min) / (max - min), true)
					if not silent then safe(o.Callback, val) end
				end
				function T:Get() return val end
				return T
			end

			function Section:AddButton(o)
				local name = o.Name or "button"
				local f = item(name, 26)
				local b = new("TextButton", {
					Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -2), BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Item, Text = name,
					Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 10, TextColor3 = Theme.Text, AutoButtonColor = false, Parent = f,
				}, { corner(5) })
				local bs = stroke(Theme.Accent, 1, 0.45); bs.Parent = b
				accent(bs, "Color")
				conn(b.MouseEnter, function() tw(b, 0.12, { BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(46, 30, 37) }) end)
				conn(b.MouseLeave, function() tw(b, 0.12, { BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Item }) end)
				conn(b.MouseButton1Click, function()
					b.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Accent
					tw(b, 0.3, { BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Item })
					safe(o.Callback)
				end)
				return {Button = b, Frame = f, Set = function(_, value) b.Text = tostring(value) end}
			end

			function Section:AddTextbox(o)
				local name = o.Name or "input"
				local flag = flagOf(o, name)
				Library.Flags[flag] = o.Default or ""
				local f = item(name, 26)
				text({ Text = name, TextColor3 = Theme.Dim, Size = UDim2.new(0.4, 0, 1, 0), Parent = f })
				local tb = new("TextBox", {
					Size = UDim2.new(0.6, 0, 1, -4), Position = UDim2.new(0.4, 0, 0, 2), BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Item,
					Text = o.Default or "", PlaceholderText = o.Placeholder or "...", PlaceholderColor3 = Theme.Dim,
					TextColor3 = Theme.Text, Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, TextSize = 10, ClearTextOnFocus = false, Parent = f,
				}, { corner(4) })
				conn(tb.FocusLost, function()
					Library.Flags[flag] = tb.Text
					safe(o.Callback, tb.Text)
				end)
				return {Box = tb, Frame = f, Get = function() return tb.Text end,
					Set = function(_, value, silent)
						tb.Text = tostring(value or "")
						Library.Flags[flag] = tb.Text
						if not silent then safe(o.Callback, tb.Text) end
					end}
			end

			function Section:AddDropdown(o)
				local name = o.Name or "dropdown"
				local flag = flagOf(o, name)
				local options = o.Options or {}
				local cur = o.Default or options[1]
				Library.Flags[flag] = cur
				local f = item(name, 24)
				f.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
				new("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 3), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, Parent = f })
				local head = new("TextButton", {
					Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 24), BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Item, Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false,
					LayoutOrder = 1, Parent = f,
				}, { corner(5) })
				local hs = stroke(Theme.Accent, 1, 0.6); hs.Parent = head
				accent(hs, "Color")
				text({ Text = name, TextColor3 = Theme.Dim, Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, 0), Parent = head })
				local cl = text({
					Text = tostring(cur), Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 10, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
					Position = UDim2.new(0.4, 0, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0.6, -8, 1, 0), Parent = head,
				})
				accent(cl, "TextColor3")
				local list = new("Frame", {
					Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
					Visible = false, LayoutOrder = 2, Parent = f,
				}, { new("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 2), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }) })
				local T = {}
				local function pick(v, silent)
					cur = v
					Library.Flags[flag] = v
					cl.Text = tostring(v)
					if not silent then safe(o.Callback, v) end
				end
				local optionConnections = {}
				local function rebuild()
					local obsolete = {}
					for _, connection in ipairs(optionConnections) do connection:Disconnect(); obsolete[connection] = true end
					for i = #Library.Connections, 1, -1 do
						if obsolete[Library.Connections[i]] then table.remove(Library.Connections, i) end
					end
					table.clear(optionConnections)
					for _, child in ipairs(list:GetChildren()) do
						if child:IsA("GuiObject") then child:Destroy() end
					end
					for idx, opt in ipairs(options) do
					local ob = new("TextButton", {
						Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 20), BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Item, BackgroundTransparency = 0.4,
						Text = tostring(opt), Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, TextSize = 10, TextColor3 = Theme.Text,
						AutoButtonColor = false, LayoutOrder = idx, Parent = list,
					}, { corner(4) })
					table.insert(optionConnections, conn(ob.MouseEnter, function() tw(ob, 0.1, { BackgroundTransparency = 0 }) end))
					table.insert(optionConnections, conn(ob.MouseLeave, function() tw(ob, 0.1, { BackgroundTransparency = 0.4 }) end))
					table.insert(optionConnections, conn(ob.MouseButton1Click, function()
						pick(opt)
						list.Visible = false
					end))
				end
				end
				rebuild()
				conn(head.MouseButton1Click, function() list.Visible = not list.Visible end)
				function T:Set(v, silent) pick(v, silent) end
				function T:SetOptions(items)
					options = {}
					for _, value in ipairs(items or {}) do options[#options + 1] = value end
					rebuild()
				end
				function T:Get() return cur end
				return T
			end

			function Section:AddKeybind(o)
				local name = o.Name or "keybind"
				local flag = flagOf(o, name)
				local kb = { Key = o.Default, Callback = o.Callback, Listening = false }
				table.insert(Window.Keybinds, kb)
				Library.Flags[flag] = kb.Key
				local f = item(name, 24)
				text({ Text = name, TextColor3 = Theme.Dim, Size = UDim2.new(0.6, 0, 1, 0), Parent = f })
				local b = new("TextButton", {
					Size = UDim2.new(0, 70, 0, 18), Position = UDim2.new(1, -70, 0.5, -9), BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Item,
					Text = kb.Key and kb.Key.Name or "None", Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 9, TextColor3 = Theme.Text,
					AutoButtonColor = false, Parent = f,
				}, { corner(4) })
				local bs = stroke(Theme.Accent, 1, 0.55); bs.Parent = b
