				accent(bs, "Color")
				conn(b.MouseButton1Click, function()
					kb.Listening = true
					b.Text = "..."
				end)
				conn(UIS.InputBegan, function(i)
					if not kb.Listening then return end
					if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
						kb.Listening = false
						if i.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Escape then
							kb.Key = nil
							b.Text = "None"
						else
							kb.Key = i.KeyCode
							b.Text = i.KeyCode.Name
						end
						Library.Flags[flag] = kb.Key
						if o.OnChange then safe(o.OnChange, kb.Key) end
					end
				end)
				local T = {}
				function T:Get() return kb.Key end
				return T
			end

			function Section:AddColorPicker(o)
				local name = o.Name or "color"
				local flag = flagOf(o, name)
				local col = o.Default or Theme.Accent
				local h, s, v = col:ToHSV()
				Library.Flags[flag] = col
				local f = item(name, 24)
				f.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
				new("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 4), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, Parent = f })
				local head = new("TextButton", {
					Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false,
					LayoutOrder = 1, Parent = f,
				})
				text({ Text = name, TextColor3 = Theme.Dim, Size = UDim2.new(0.7, 0, 1, 0), Parent = head })
				local sw = new("Frame", {
					Size = UDim2.fromOffset(26, 14), Position = UDim2.new(1, -26, 0.5, -7), BackgroundColor3 = col, Parent = head,
				}, { corner(4) })
				stroke(Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 1, 0.7).Parent = sw
				local panel = new("Frame", {
					Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 52), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Visible = false, LayoutOrder = 2, Parent = f,
				})
				local function apply(silent)
					col = Color3.fromHSV(h, s, v)
					Library.Flags[flag] = col
					sw.BackgroundColor3 = col
					if not silent then safe(o.Callback, col) end
				end
				local hb = makeBar(panel, UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 4), UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 10), h, function(a) h = a; apply() end)
				new("UIGradient", { Color = ColorSequence.new({
					ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromHSV(0, 1, 1)), ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.17, Color3.fromHSV(0.17, 1, 1)),
					ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.33, Color3.fromHSV(0.33, 1, 1)), ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.fromHSV(0.5, 1, 1)),
					ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.67, Color3.fromHSV(0.67, 1, 1)), ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.83, Color3.fromHSV(0.83, 1, 1)),
					ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromHSV(1, 1, 1)),
				}), Parent = hb })
				local hf = hb:FindFirstChildOfClass("Frame"); if hf then hf.BackgroundTransparency = 1 end
				makeBar(panel, UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 22), UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 10), s, function(a) s = a; apply() end)
				makeBar(panel, UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 40), UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 10), v, function(a) v = a; apply() end)
				conn(head.MouseButton1Click, function() panel.Visible = not panel.Visible end)
				local T = {}
				function T:Get() return col end
				function T:Set(c)
					h, s, v = c:ToHSV()
					apply()
				end
				return T
			end

			return Section
		end

		table.insert(Window.Tabs, Tab)
		if #Window.Tabs == 1 then
			Window.Current = nil
			Window:SelectTab(Tab)
		end
		return Tab
	end

	Window.Main = Main
	Window.Gui = Gui
	Window.FPSLabel = FPS
	Window.Sidebar = Side
	Window.Scale = scale
	Window.ThemeName = "Dark"
	function Window:SetMenuTheme(name)
		self.ThemeName = name
		setAccent(name == "Halloween" and Color3.fromRGB(255, 111, 25) or Color3.fromRGB(235, 40, 75))
	end
	return Window
end

function Library:Unload()
	if Library.Unloaded then return end
	Library.Unloaded = true
	for _, c in ipairs(Library.Connections) do
		pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end)
	end
	table.clear(Library.Connections)
	pcall(function()
		if Gui and Gui.Parent then Gui:Destroy() end
	end)
end


function Library:SetAccent(color) setAccent(color) end
function Library:GetAccent() return Theme.Accent end
local Window = Library:CreateWindow({Name = "D", Subtitle = "DADA SI EDITION"})
return Library, Window

end

-- Native MM2 Skinchanger. Renderer adapted from the supplied skinchanger by belfor.
-- The weapon catalogue is loaded remotely; no weapon/mesh table is embedded here.
local function mountDaDaSkinChanger(panel, pal)
    local DATA_URL = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/mavey184-cyber/Menu/refs/heads/main/MM2_SkinMeshData.lua"
    local LocalPlayer = LP
    local MESHES = {}
    local active, enabled, loading = true, false, false
    local generation = 0
    local selected = {Knife = nil, Gun = nil}
    local items, filtered = {}, {}
    local records, templates = {}, {}
    local inventoryChanges = {}
    local lastChoice, filterType, page = nil, "All", 1
    local PAGE_SIZE = 9
    local render, filterItems, loadCatalogue
    local statusLabel, selectionLabel, enabledBinding
    local statusText = "Loading weapon catalogue..."
    local statusColor = pal.muted
    local function alive()
        return active and not espLifecycle.stopped
    end
    local function setStatus(message, color)
        statusText = tostring(message)
        statusColor = color or pal.muted
        if statusLabel and statusLabel.Parent and alive() then
            statusLabel.Text = statusText
            statusLabel.TextColor3 = statusColor
        end
    end
    local function connect(signal, callback)
        local connection = signal:Connect(callback)
        table.insert(espLifecycle.connections, connection)
        return connection
    end

local function trimAsset(v)
    if type(v) == 'string' then
        return (v:gsub('^%s+', ''):gsub('%s+$', ''))
    end

    return v
end
local function applyProps(inst, props, skip)
    for k, v in pairs(props or {})do
        if not (skip and skip[k]) then
            if k == 'MeshId' or k == 'TextureId' or k == 'TextureID' or k == 'Texture' then
                v = trimAsset(v)
            end

            pcall(function()
                inst[k] = v
            end)
        end
    end
end
local function createMeshPart(props)
    local meshId = trimAsset(props.MeshId or '')
    local size = props.Size or Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
    local part

    pcall(function()
        part = game:GetService('InsertService'):CreateMeshPartAsync(meshId, Enum.CollisionFidelity.Box, Enum.RenderFidelity.Precise)
    end)

    if part then
        pcall(function()
            part.Size = size
        end)

        return part
    end

    local p = Instance.new('Part')

    p.Size = size

    local sm = Instance.new('SpecialMesh')

    sm.MeshType = Enum.MeshType.FileMesh

    pcall(function()
        sm.MeshId = meshId
    end)
    pcall(function()
        sm.TextureId = trimAsset(props.TextureID or '')
    end)

    sm.Parent = p

    return p
end

local CHROMA = {
    Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0),
    Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 0),
    Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0),
    Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 255),
    Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 255),
    Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 255),
}

local function chromaColor(t)
    local n = #CHROMA
    local phase = t % n
    local i = math.floor(phase)

    return CHROMA[i + 1]:Lerp(CHROMA[((i + 1) % n) + 1], phase - i)
end
local function isChroma(name, data)
    if data.Meta and data.Meta.Chroma == true then
        return true
    end

    return name:sub(-6) == 'Chroma'
end

local CHROMA_FLASH_RATE = 1.8
local CHROMA_LAYER = 'rbxassetid://18363392181'

local function startChroma(overlay)
    local flashParts, smoothDecals, smoothOther = {}, {}, {}
    local tagged = false

    for _, d in ipairs(overlay:GetDescendants())do
        local role = d:GetAttribute('MM2Chroma')

        if role then
            tagged = true

            if role == 'part' then
                flashParts[#flashParts + 1] = d
            elseif role == 'decal' and d:IsA('Decal') then
                pcall(function()
                    d.Texture = CHROMA_LAYER
                end)

                smoothDecals[#smoothDecals + 1] = d
            elseif role == 'fire' then
                smoothOther[#smoothOther + 1] = d
            end
        end
    end

    if not tagged then
        for _, d in ipairs(overlay:GetDescendants())do
            if d:IsA('BasePart') and (d.Material == Enum.Material.Neon or d.Name:lower():find('light')) then
                flashParts[#flashParts + 1] = d
            elseif d:IsA('Decal') and d.Name:lower():find('chroma') then
                smoothDecals[#smoothDecals + 1] = d
            elseif d:IsA('Fire') then
                smoothOther[#smoothOther + 1] = d
            end
        end

        if #smoothDecals == 0 then
            smoothOther[#smoothOther + 1] = overlay
        end
    end
    if #flashParts == 0 and #smoothDecals == 0 and #smoothOther == 0 then
        return nil
    end

    return RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        local now = os.clock()
        local col = chromaColor(now)

        for _, s in ipairs(smoothDecals)do
            s.Color3 = col
        end
        for _, s in ipairs(smoothOther)do
            if s:IsA('Fire') then
                s.Color = col
            elseif s:IsA('BasePart') then
                s.Color = col
            end
        end

        local step = math.floor(now * CHROMA_FLASH_RATE)

        for i, b in ipairs(flashParts)do
            b.Color = CHROMA[((step + i - 1) % #CHROMA) + 1]
        end
    end)
end

local STRUCTURAL = {
    WeldConstraint = true,
    Weld = true,
    Motor6D = true,
    RigidConstraint = true,
    Bone = true,
    Snap = true,
    ManualWeld = true,
    Rotate = true,
    RotateP = true,
    RotateV = true,
}

local function chromaTag(inst, node)
    if not (inst and node.Tags) then
        return
    end

    for _, t in ipairs(node.Tags)do
        local r = (t == 'ChromaPart' and 'part') or (t == 'ChromaDecal' and 'decal') or (t == 'ChromaFire' and 'fire') or nil

        if r then
            pcall(function()
                inst:SetAttribute('MM2Chroma', r)
            end)
        end
    end
end
local function instantiateNode(node)
    local cls = node.Class

    if STRUCTURAL[cls] then
        return nil
    end

    local inst

    if cls == 'MeshPart' then
        inst = createMeshPart(node.Props)

        applyProps(inst, node.Props, {
            MeshId = true,
            Size = true,
            RelCF = true,
            CanCollide = true,
        })
    else
        local ok, res = pcall(Instance.new, cls)

        if not ok or not res then
            return nil
        end

        inst = res

        applyProps(inst, node.Props, {RelCF = true})
    end

    inst.Name = node.Name or inst.Name
    chromaTag(inst, node)

    return inst
end
local function rebuildTree(node, parentInst, root, idToInst, parts, deferred)
    for _, child in ipairs(node.Children or {})do
        local inst = instantiateNode(child)

        if inst then
            idToInst[child.Id] = inst

            if inst:IsA('BasePart') then
                inst.Anchored, inst.CanCollide, inst.CanQuery, inst.CanTouch, inst.Massless = false, false, false, false, true
                parts[#parts + 1] = {
                    inst = inst,
                    relcf = child.Props.RelCF,
                }
                inst.Parent = root

                rebuildTree(child, inst, root, idToInst, parts, deferred)
            elseif inst:IsA('Attachment') then
                if child.Props.RelCF then
                    pcall(function()
                        inst.CFrame = child.Props.RelCF
                    end)
                end

                inst.Parent = root

                rebuildTree(child, inst, root, idToInst, parts, deferred)
            elseif inst:IsA('Beam') or inst:IsA('Trail') then
                inst.Parent = parentInst
                deferred[#deferred + 1] = {
                    inst = inst,
                    a0 = child.Att0,
                    a1 = child.Att1,
                }

                rebuildTree(child, inst, root, idToInst, parts, deferred)
            else
                inst.Parent = parentInst

                rebuildTree(child, inst, root, idToInst, parts, deferred)
            end
        else
            rebuildTree(child, parentInst, root, idToInst, parts, deferred)
        end
    end
end
local function buildFullOverlay(data)
    local rn = data.Model

    if not rn then
        return nil
    end

    local root

    if rn.Class == 'MeshPart' then
        root = createMeshPart(rn.Props)

        applyProps(root, rn.Props, {
            MeshId = true,
            Size = true,
            RelCF = true,
            CanCollide = true,
        })
    else
        root = Instance.new('Part')

        applyProps(root, rn.Props, {
            RelCF = true,
            CanCollide = true,
        })
    end

    chromaTag(root, rn)

    root.Anchored, root.CanCollide, root.CanQuery, root.CanTouch, root.Massless = false, false, false, false, true

    local idToInst = {
        [rn.Id] = root,
    }
    local parts, deferred = {}, {}

    rebuildTree(rn, root, root, idToInst, parts, deferred)

    for _, d in ipairs(deferred)do
        if d.a0 and idToInst[d.a0] then
            pcall(function()
                d.inst.Attachment0 = idToInst[d.a0]
            end)
        end
        if d.a1 and idToInst[d.a1] then
            pcall(function()
                d.inst.Attachment1 = idToInst[d.a1]
            end)
        end
    end

    return {
        root = root,
        parts = parts,
    }
end
local function finalizeOverlay(built, targetCF)
    local root = built.root

    pcall(function()
        root.CFrame = targetCF
    end)

    for _, p in ipairs(built.parts)do
        if p.relcf then
            pcall(function()
                p.inst.CFrame = targetCF * p.relcf
            end)
        end

        local w = Instance.new('WeldConstraint')

        w.Part0 = p.inst
        w.Part1 = root
        w.Parent = p.inst
    end

    return root
end

local PARTICLE_CLASSES = {
    ParticleEmitter = true,
    Fire = true,
    Smoke = true,
    Sparkles = true,
    PointLight = true,
    SpotLight = true,
    SurfaceLight = true,
}

local function buildFlatOverlay(data)
    local root, meshes, decals, effects

    do
        root, meshes, decals, effects = nil, {}, {}, {}

        for _, e in ipairs(data.Display or {})do
            if e.Path == '(root)' then
                root = e
            elseif e.Class == 'SpecialMesh' then
                meshes[#meshes + 1] = e
            elseif e.Class == 'Decal' or e.Class == 'Texture' then
                decals[#decals + 1] = e
            elseif PARTICLE_CLASSES[e.Class] then
                effects[#effects + 1] = e
            end
        end
    end

    if not root then
        return nil
    end

    local part

    if root.Class == 'MeshPart' then
        part = createMeshPart(root.Props)

        applyProps(part, root.Props, {
            MeshId = true,
            Size = true,
            CanCollide = true,
        })
    else
        part = Instance.new('Part')
        part.Size = root.Props.Size or Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)

        applyProps(part, root.Props, {
            Size = true,
            CanCollide = true,
        })

        for _, m in ipairs(meshes)do
            local sm = Instance.new('SpecialMesh')

            sm.MeshType = Enum.MeshType.FileMesh

            applyProps(sm, m.Props)

            sm.Parent = part
        end
    end

    part.Anchored, part.CanCollide, part.CanQuery, part.CanTouch, part.Massless = false, false, false, false, true

    for _, d in ipairs(decals)do
        local dc = Instance.new('Decal')

        applyProps(dc, d.Props)

        dc.Parent = part
    end
    for _, e in ipairs(effects)do
        local ok, inst = pcall(Instance.new, e.Class)

        if ok and inst then
            applyProps(inst, e.Props)

            inst.Parent = part
        end
    end

    return {
        root = part,
        parts = {},
    }
end
local function buildAnyOverlay(data)
    if data.Model then
        return buildFullOverlay(data)
    end
    if data.Display then
        return buildFlatOverlay(data)
    end

    return nil
end

    local function dropRecord(base)
        local record = records[base]
        if not record then return end
        records[base] = nil
        if record.chroma then pcall(function() record.chroma:Disconnect() end) end
        if record.overlay then pcall(function() record.overlay:Destroy() end) end
        for _, saved in ipairs(record.hidden) do
            pcall(function() saved.inst[saved.prop] = saved.value end)
        end
    end
    local function clearRecords()
        local bases = {}
        for base in pairs(records) do bases[#bases + 1] = base end
        for _, base in ipairs(bases) do dropRecord(base) end
    end
    local function clearTemplates()
        for _, template in pairs(templates) do pcall(function() template:Destroy() end) end
        table.clear(templates)
    end
    local function hideOriginal(record, base)
        local function hide(instance)
            if instance == record.overlay or instance:IsDescendantOf(record.overlay)
                or record.seen[instance] then return end
            local property
            if instance:IsA('BasePart') or instance:IsA('Decal') or instance:IsA('Texture') then
                property = 'Transparency'
            elseif instance:IsA('ParticleEmitter') or instance:IsA('Trail')
                or instance:IsA('Beam') or instance:IsA('Fire')
                or instance:IsA('Smoke') or instance:IsA('Sparkles')
                or instance:IsA('Light') then
                property = 'Enabled'
            end
            if not property then return end
            local ok = pcall(function()
                local value = instance[property]
                instance[property] = property == 'Transparency' and 1 or false
                record.hidden[#record.hidden + 1] = {inst=instance, prop=property, value=value}
            end)
            if ok then record.seen[instance] = true end
        end
        hide(base)
        for _, descendant in ipairs(base:GetDescendants()) do hide(descendant) end
    end
    local function collectTargets()
        local targets = {}
        local character = LocalPlayer.Character
        local backpack = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass('Backpack')
        local function scan(container)
            if not container then return end
            for _, tool in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
                if tool:IsA('Tool') then
                    local slot
                    if tool.Name == 'Gun' or CollectionService:HasTag(tool, 'Weapon_Gun') then
                        slot = 'Gun'
                    elseif tool.Name == 'Knife' or CollectionService:HasTag(tool, 'Weapon_Knife') then
                        slot = 'Knife'
                    end
                    local handle = slot and tool:FindFirstChild('Handle')
                    if handle and handle:IsA('BasePart') then targets[handle] = slot end
                end
            end
        end
        scan(character)
        scan(backpack)
        if character then
            for _, slot in ipairs({'Knife', 'Gun'}) do
                local ref = character:FindFirstChild('DisplayRef' .. slot)
                local base = ref and ref:IsA('ObjectValue') and ref.Value
                if base and base:IsA('Model') then
                    base = base.PrimaryPart or base:FindFirstChildWhichIsA('BasePart', true)
                end
                if base and base:IsA('BasePart') and base.Parent then targets[base] = slot end
            end
        end
        return targets
    end
    local function getTemplate(name, version)
        if templates[name] then return templates[name] end
        local data = MESHES[name]
        if not data then return nil end
        local built = buildAnyOverlay(data)
        if not built or not built.root then
            error('Cannot rebuild the mesh for ' .. tostring(name))
        end
        if not alive() or not enabled or generation ~= version then
            built.root:Destroy()
            return nil
        end
        local ok, result = pcall(finalizeOverlay, built, CFrame.new())
        if not ok then built.root:Destroy(); error(result) end
        result.Name = 'DaDaSkinOverlay'
        result.Archivable = true
        result:SetAttribute('DaDaSkinKey', name)
        templates[name] = result
        return result
    end
    local retryAfter = {}
    local function reconcile()
        if not enabled or not next(MESHES) then return end
        local targets = collectTargets()
        local stale = {}
        for base, record in pairs(records) do
            if not base.Parent or targets[base] ~= record.slot or selected[record.slot] ~= record.name
                or not record.overlay.Parent then
                stale[#stale + 1] = base
            end
        end
        for _, base in ipairs(stale) do dropRecord(base) end
        for base, slot in pairs(targets) do
            if not alive() or not enabled then break end
            local name = selected[slot]
            if name and MESHES[name] then
                if records[base] then
                    hideOriginal(records[base], base)
                elseif not retryAfter[base] or os.clock() >= retryAfter[base] then
                    local version = generation
                    local overlay, attached
                    local ok, err = pcall(function()
                        local template = getTemplate(name, version)
                        if not template or not alive() or not enabled or generation ~= version
                            or selected[slot] ~= name or not base.Parent
                            or collectTargets()[base] ~= slot then return end
                        overlay = template:Clone()
                        if not overlay then error('Cannot clone ' .. name) end
                        -- A cloned welded tree is unparented here: move every part together.
                        local delta = base.CFrame * overlay.CFrame:Inverse()
                        overlay.CFrame = delta * overlay.CFrame
                        for _, part in ipairs(overlay:GetDescendants()) do
                            if part:IsA('BasePart') then part.CFrame = delta * part.CFrame end
                        end
                        local weld = Instance.new('WeldConstraint')
                        weld.Part0, weld.Part1, weld.Parent = overlay, base, overlay
                        local record = {overlay=overlay, hidden={}, seen={}, name=name, slot=slot}
                        records[base] = record
                        hideOriginal(record, base)
                        overlay.Parent = base
                        record.chroma = isChroma(name, MESHES[name]) and startChroma(overlay) or nil
                        attached = true
                    end)
                    if not ok then
                        dropRecord(base)
                        if overlay and not attached then pcall(function() overlay:Destroy() end) end
                        retryAfter[base] = os.clock() + 5
                        warn('[DaDa Skinchanger] ' .. tostring(err))
                        setStatus('Mesh error: ' .. tostring(err), pal.danger)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        for base in pairs(retryAfter) do
            if not targets[base] then retryAfter[base] = nil end
        end
    end
    local function updateSelections()
        if selectionLabel and selectionLabel.Label then
            selectionLabel:Set('Knife: ' .. (selected.Knife or '—') .. '  |  Gun: ' .. (selected.Gun or '—'))
            selectionLabel.Label.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
        end
        if render then render() end
    end
    local function setEnabled(value)
        enabled = value == true
        generation += 1
        table.clear(retryAfter)
        if not enabled then
            clearRecords()
            setStatus('Disabled. Original weapon models restored.')
        else
            setStatus('Enabled. Select a skin below; equip a normal Knife / Gun to see it.')
        end
        if render then render() end
    end
    local function choose(key)
        if not alive() then return end
        local data = MESHES[key]
        local slot = data and data.Meta and data.Meta.ItemType
        if slot ~= 'Knife' and slot ~= 'Gun' then error('Unknown weapon type: ' .. tostring(key)) end
        selected[slot], lastChoice = key, key
        -- Keep at most the two selected weapon templates, not every browsed skin.
        for cachedName, template in pairs(templates) do
            if cachedName ~= selected.Knife and cachedName ~= selected.Gun then
                template:Destroy()
                templates[cachedName] = nil
            end
        end
        generation += 1
        table.clear(retryAfter)
        -- Prevent the old Extras color-only skins from recoloring the rebuilt weapon.
        if slot == 'Gun' then SHF.gun_skin = 'None' else SHF.knife_skin = 'None' end
        if not enabled then enabledBinding.apply(true) end
        local stale = {}
        for base, record in pairs(records) do
            if record.slot == slot then stale[#stale + 1] = base end
        end
        for _, base in ipairs(stale) do dropRecord(base) end
        updateSelections()
        setStatus('Selected ' .. tostring(data.Meta.ItemName or key) .. ' (' .. slot .. '). Local visual only.', pal.success)
    end

    -- Optional original inventory workflow: client-side add + EquipService.
    -- Selection/rendering does not depend on these game internals being available.
    local function addAndEquipSelected()
        if not lastChoice or not MESHES[lastChoice] then
            setStatus('Select a weapon card first.', pal.danger)
            return
        end
        local key = lastChoice
        local function child(parent, name)
            local object = parent and (parent:FindFirstChild(name) or parent:WaitForChild(name, 5))
            assert(object, 'MM2 object is missing: ' .. name)
            assert(alive(), 'Skinchanger unloaded')
            return object
        end
        local ok, err = pcall(function()
            local modules = child(ReplicatedStorage, 'Modules')
            local profile = require(child(modules, 'ProfileData'))
            local services = child(ReplicatedStorage, 'ClientServices')
            local equip = require(child(services, 'EquipService'))
            local owned = profile.Weapons and profile.Weapons.Owned
            assert(type(owned) == 'table', 'ProfileData.Weapons.Owned is unavailable')
            assert(alive(), 'Skinchanger unloaded')
            if not inventoryChanges[key] then
                local old = owned[key]
                if (tonumber(old) or 0) < 1 then
                    owned[key] = 1
                    inventoryChanges[key] = {owned=owned, old=old, assigned=1}
                    local remotes = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild('Remotes')
                    local inventory = remotes and remotes:FindFirstChild('Inventory')
                    local changed = inventory and inventory:FindFirstChild('InventoryDataChanged')
                    if changed then pcall(function() changed:Fire('Weapons', key, owned[key]) end) end
                end
            end
            equip:EquipItem('Weapons', key)
        end)
        if not alive() then return end
        if ok then setStatus('Added / equipped locally: ' .. key .. '. This does not grant a server-owned item.', pal.success)
        else setStatus('Local inventory: ' .. tostring(err), pal.danger) end
    end

    enabledBinding = panel:Toggle('Enable Skinchanger', false, setEnabled, nil,
        {get=function() return enabled end})
    panel:Dropdown('Weapon Type', {'All', 'Knife', 'Gun'}, 'All', function(value)
        filterType = value
        if filterItems then filterItems() end
    end, {get=function() return filterType end})
    selectionLabel = panel:Label('Knife: —  |  Gun: —')

    local root = Instance.new('Frame')
    root.Name = 'SkinchangerCatalogue'
    root.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 456)
    root.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    panel:Mount(root, 'Skinchanger weapon skins catalogue search')
    local search = Instance.new('TextBox')
    search.Name = 'SkinSearch'
    search.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30)
    search.BackgroundColor3, search.BorderSizePixel = pal.control, 0
    search.Text, search.PlaceholderText = '', 'Search name, ID, rarity...'
    search.ClearTextOnFocus = false
    search.TextColor3, search.PlaceholderColor3 = pal.text, pal.muted
    search.TextSize, search.Font = 12, Enum.Font.GothamMedium
    search.Parent = root
    Instance.new('UICorner', search).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
    local padding = Instance.new('UIPadding', search)
    padding.PaddingLeft, padding.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 8), UDim.new(0, 8)
    local grid = Instance.new('Frame')
    grid.Name = 'WeaponCards'
    grid.Position, grid.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 38), UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 324)
    grid.BackgroundTransparency, grid.Parent = 1, root
    local layout = Instance.new('UIGridLayout', grid)
    layout.CellSize, layout.CellPadding = UDim2.new(1/3, -4, 0, 104), UDim2.fromOffset(6, 6)
    layout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    local empty = Instance.new('TextLabel')
    empty.Position, empty.Size = grid.Position, grid.Size
    empty.BackgroundTransparency, empty.TextWrapped = 1, true
    empty.TextColor3, empty.Font, empty.TextSize = pal.muted, Enum.Font.GothamMedium, 12
    empty.Parent = root
    local footer = Instance.new('TextLabel')
    footer.Position, footer.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(40, 374), UDim2.new(1, -80, 0, 28)
    footer.BackgroundTransparency, footer.TextColor3 = 1, pal.text
    footer.TextSize, footer.Font, footer.Parent = 11, Enum.Font.GothamMedium, root
    local function nav(label, position)
        local button = Instance.new('TextButton')
        button.Size, button.Position, button.Text = UDim2.fromOffset(34, 28), position, label
        button.BackgroundColor3, button.BorderSizePixel, button.TextColor3 = pal.control, 0, pal.text
        button.Font, button.TextSize, button.Parent = Enum.Font.GothamBold, 16, root
        Instance.new('UICorner', button).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 7)
        return button
    end
    local previous = nav('‹', UDim2.fromOffset(0, 374))
    local following = nav('›', UDim2.new(1, -34, 0, 374))
    statusLabel = Instance.new('TextLabel')
    statusLabel.Name = 'SkinchangerStatus'
    statusLabel.Position, statusLabel.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 410), UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 44)
    statusLabel.BackgroundTransparency, statusLabel.TextWrapped = 1, true
    statusLabel.TextXAlignment, statusLabel.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
    statusLabel.TextSize, statusLabel.Font = 11, Enum.Font.Gotham
    statusLabel.Text, statusLabel.TextColor3, statusLabel.Parent = statusText, statusColor, root

    local function imageFor(meta)
        local image = meta.Image or meta.Icon or meta.Thumbnail
        if type(image) == 'number' then return 'rbxassetid://' .. tostring(image) end
        if type(image) == 'string' and image ~= '' then
            local id = image:match('[?&][Aa]sset[Ii]d=(%d+)')
            if id then return 'rbxassetid://' .. id end
            if image:match('^%d+$') then return 'rbxassetid://' .. image end
            return image
        end
        local id = tostring(meta.ItemID or ''):match('%d+')
        return id and ('rbxthumb://type=Asset&id=' .. id .. '&w=150&h=150') or ''
    end
    render = function()
        if not alive() or not root.Parent then return end
        for _, child in ipairs(grid:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA('GuiObject') then child:Destroy() end
        end
        local pageCount = math.max(1, math.ceil(#filtered / PAGE_SIZE))
        page = math.clamp(page, 1, pageCount)
        footer.Text = string.format('%d / %d · %d skins', page, pageCount, #filtered)
        previous.Active, following.Active = page > 1, page < pageCount
        previous.TextTransparency = page > 1 and 0 or 0.65
        following.TextTransparency = page < pageCount and 0 or 0.65
        empty.Visible = #filtered == 0
        empty.Text = loading and 'Loading remote catalogue...'
            or (#items == 0 and 'Catalogue unavailable. Press Reload Catalogue.' or 'No matching skins')
        local first = (page - 1) * PAGE_SIZE + 1
        for index = first, math.min(first + PAGE_SIZE - 1, #filtered) do
            local item = filtered[index]
            local key = item.key
            local card = Instance.new('TextButton')
            card.Name, card.Text = 'Skin_' .. key, ''
            card.BackgroundColor3, card.BorderSizePixel = pal.control, 0
            card.LayoutOrder, card.Parent = index, grid
            Instance.new('UICorner', card).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
            if selected[item.slot] == key then
                local border = Instance.new('UIStroke', card)
                border.Color, border.Thickness = pal.blue, 2
            end
            local image = Instance.new('ImageLabel')
            image.Position, image.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(4, 2), UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 56)
            image.BackgroundTransparency, image.ScaleType = 1, Enum.ScaleType.Fit
            image.Image, image.Parent = item.image, card
            local name = Instance.new('TextLabel')
            name.Position, name.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(3, 58), UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 28)
            name.BackgroundTransparency, name.TextWrapped = 1, true
            name.TextColor3, name.TextSize, name.Font = pal.text, 10, Enum.Font.GothamMedium
            name.Text, name.TextTruncate, name.Parent = item.name, Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, card
            local detail = Instance.new('TextLabel')
            detail.Position, detail.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(3, 86), UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 16)
            detail.BackgroundTransparency, detail.TextColor3 = 1, pal.muted
            detail.TextSize, detail.Font = 9, Enum.Font.Gotham
            detail.Text = item.slot .. (item.rarity ~= '' and (' · ' .. item.rarity) or '')
            detail.TextTruncate, detail.Parent = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, card
            -- Card connections die with their Instances on the next render.
            card.Activated:Connect(function()
                if not alive() then return end
                local ok, err = pcall(choose, key)
                if not ok then setStatus(tostring(err), pal.danger) end
            end)
        end
    end
    filterItems = function()
        filtered = {}
        local query = search.Text:lower():gsub('^%s+', ''):gsub('%s+$', '')
        for _, item in ipairs(items) do
            if (filterType == 'All' or item.slot == filterType)
                and (query == '' or item.haystack:find(query, 1, true)) then
                filtered[#filtered + 1] = item
            end
        end
        page = 1
        render()
    end
    connect(search:GetPropertyChangedSignal('Text'), filterItems)
    connect(previous.Activated, function() page -= 1; render() end)
    connect(following.Activated, function() page += 1; render() end)
    panel:Button('Add + Equip Selected (local inventory)', function()
        task.spawn(addAndEquipSelected)
    end)
    panel:Button('Restore Original Weapons', function()
        selected.Knife, selected.Gun, lastChoice = nil, nil, nil
        enabledBinding.apply(false)
        clearTemplates()
        updateSelections()
    end)
    panel:Button('Reload Catalogue', function()
        if loadCatalogue then task.spawn(loadCatalogue) end
    end)
    panel:Label('Cosmetics only. No server inventory / ownership changes.')

    loadCatalogue = function()
        if loading or not alive() then return end
        loading = true
        setStatus('Downloading MM2_SkinMeshData.lua...')
        render()
        local ok, result = pcall(function()
            assert(type(loadstring) == 'function', 'loadstring is unavailable in this executor')
            local source = game:HttpGet(DATA_URL)
            assert(type(source) == 'string' and #source > 0, 'Empty HTTP response')
            if not alive() then return nil end
            local chunk, compileError = loadstring(source, '@MM2_SkinMeshData.lua')
            assert(chunk, 'Catalogue compile error: ' .. tostring(compileError))
            local data = chunk()
            assert(type(data) == 'table', 'Catalogue must return a table')
            local newItems = {}
            for key, entry in pairs(data) do
                if type(key) == 'string' and type(entry) == 'table' and type(entry.Meta) == 'table'
                    and (entry.Model or entry.Display) then
                    local meta = entry.Meta
                    if meta.ItemType == 'Knife' or meta.ItemType == 'Gun' then
                        local name = tostring(meta.ItemName or key)
                        local rarity = tostring(meta.Rarity or '')
                        newItems[#newItems + 1] = {key=key, name=name, rarity=rarity,
                            slot=meta.ItemType, image=imageFor(meta),
                            haystack=(key .. ' ' .. name .. ' ' .. rarity .. ' ' .. meta.ItemType):lower()}
                    end
                end
            end
            assert(#newItems > 0, 'Catalogue has no compatible Knife / Gun entries')
            table.sort(newItems, function(a, b)
                if a.name:lower() ~= b.name:lower() then return a.name:lower() < b.name:lower() end
                return a.key < b.key
            end)
            return {meshes=data, items=newItems}
        end)
        loading = false
        if not alive() then return end
        if ok and result then
            generation += 1
            clearRecords()
            clearTemplates()
            table.clear(retryAfter)
            MESHES, items = result.meshes, result.items
            for _, slot in ipairs({'Knife', 'Gun'}) do
                if selected[slot] and not MESHES[selected[slot]] then selected[slot] = nil end
            end
            if lastChoice and not MESHES[lastChoice] then lastChoice = nil end
            setStatus(string.format('Loaded %d skins. Click a card to apply; normal Knife / Gun required.', #items), pal.success)
            updateSelections()
        else
            setStatus('Catalogue load failed: ' .. tostring(result) .. '. Press Reload Catalogue.', pal.danger)
            warn('[DaDa Skinchanger] ' .. tostring(result))
            if notify then pcall(notify, 'Skinchanger', 'Catalogue failed to load. Use Reload Catalogue.') end
        end
        filterItems()
    end
    local function destroy()
        if not active then return end
        active, enabled = false, false
        generation += 1
        clearRecords()
        clearTemplates()
        for key, change in pairs(inventoryChanges) do
            -- Never overwrite a later inventory update made by the game.
            if change.owned[key] == change.assigned then change.owned[key] = change.old end
        end
        table.clear(inventoryChanges)
        table.clear(retryAfter)
        table.clear(MESHES)
        table.clear(items)
        table.clear(filtered)
    end
    table.insert(espLifecycle.cleanups, destroy)
    task.spawn(loadCatalogue)
    task.spawn(function()
        while alive() do
            local ok, err = pcall(reconcile)
            if not ok and alive() then
                warn('[DaDa Skinchanger] ' .. tostring(err))
                setStatus('Skinchanger: ' .. tostring(err), pal.danger)
            end
            task.wait(0.15)
        end
    end)
end

local function initMenu()
    local menuLibrary,menuWindow=createDaDaV5()
    gui=menuWindow.Gui
    win=menuWindow.Main
    -- Keep the existing notify(title, message, duration) API, now rendered by V5.
    if notifGui then notifGui:Destroy(); notifGui=nil end
    notify=function(title,message,duration)
        if espLifecycle.stopped then return end
        menuLibrary:Notify(title,message,duration)
    end
    local FONT_REGULAR,FONT_MEDIUM,FONT_BOLD=Enum.Font.Gotham,Enum.Font.GothamMedium,Enum.Font.GothamBold
    local pal={
        canvas=Color3.fromRGB(9,7,9),panel=Color3.fromRGB(20,12,16),sidebar=Color3.fromRGB(26,13,18),
        content=Color3.fromRGB(22,10,15),card=Color3.fromRGB(16,9,12),control=Color3.fromRGB(32,22,27),
        controlHover=Color3.fromRGB(46,30,37),border=Color3.fromRGB(98,43,58),hairline=Color3.fromRGB(65,29,40),
        text=Color3.fromRGB(236,226,229),muted=Color3.fromRGB(135,116,123),desc=Color3.fromRGB(135,116,123),
        blue=Color3.fromRGB(235,40,75),blueText=Color3.new(1,1,1),danger=Color3.fromRGB(255,69,58),
        success=Color3.fromRGB(82,224,120),toggleOff=Color3.fromRGB(32,22,27),toggleOn=Color3.fromRGB(235,40,75),
        knobOff=Color3.new(1,1,1),pill=Color3.fromRGB(46,30,37),rowOn=Color3.fromRGB(46,30,37),
    }
    getgenv().DaDa_MenuPal=pal
    getgenv().DaDa_UIV5={Library=menuLibrary,Window=menuWindow}
    table.insert(espLifecycle.cleanups,function()
        menuLibrary:Unload()
        if getgenv().DaDa_UIV5 and getgenv().DaDa_UIV5.Library==menuLibrary then
            getgenv().DaDa_UIV5=nil
            getgenv().DaDa_MenuPal=nil
        end
    end)
    table.insert(themeUpdaters,function(theme)
        if menuLibrary.Unloaded then return end
        menuLibrary:SetAccent(theme.Accent)
        pal.blue=theme.Accent
    end)
    local menuTabs={}
    local specialTabs={}
    for _,record in ipairs({
        {1,"Combat","game functions","game"},{8,"Farm","automatic farming","game"},
        {2,"Fling","target functions","target"},{3,"Movement","local player","player"},
        {4,"Visuals","player visuals","visuals"},{5,"World","world visuals","world"},
        {10,"Skinchanger","MM2 weapon skins","models"},{11,"Animations","custom animations","animations"},
        {12,"Emotes","emote library","emotes"},{6,"Extras","sounds and utilities","player"},
        {7,"Config","settings and saved configs","models"},{9,"Info","credits and community","game"},
    }) do
        local tab=menuWindow:AddTab(record[2],record[3],record[4])
        menuTabs[record[1]]=tab
        if record[1]>=10 then
            tab.Left.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,0)
            tab.Right.Visible=false
        end
    end
    specialTabs["Skinchanger"]=menuTabs[10]
    specialTabs["Animations"]=menuTabs[11]
    specialTabs["Emotes"]=menuTabs[12]
    local uiRegistry={}
    -- Each control owns its value, validator, renderer and real callback.
    -- Legacy aliases stay paint-only for hotkeys and skybox mutual exclusion.
    local uiConfig={bindings={},byId={},busy=false,busySince=0}
    -- A click must never be swallowed forever by a stuck "busy" flag.
    function uiConfig.blocked()
        if espLifecycle.stopped then return true end
        if uiConfig.busy then
            if os.clock()-(uiConfig.busySince or 0)>5 then uiConfig.busy=false else return true end
        end
        return false
    end
    function uiConfig.add(kind,id,default,callback,render,spec,validate)
        spec=spec or {}
        id=spec.id or id
        assert(not uiConfig.byId[id],"Duplicate config control: "..id)
        local binding={kind=kind,id=id,default=default,value=default,legacy=spec.legacy,
            fromLegacy=spec.fromLegacy,callback=callback,render=render,validate=validate}
        function binding.read()
            if spec.get then
                local ok,value=pcall(spec.get)
                if ok and value~=nil then
                    local valid,normalized=validate(value)
                    if valid then return normalized end
                end
            end
            return binding.value
        end
        function binding.paint(value,animate)
            local ok,normalized=validate(value)
            if not ok then return false,normalized end
            binding.value=normalized
            render(normalized,animate==true)
            return true
        end
        function binding.apply(value)
            local ok,normalized=validate(value)
            if not ok then return false,normalized end
            if callback then
                local success,err=pcall(callback,normalized)
                if not success then
                    if spec.get then binding.paint(binding.read(),false)
                    else binding.value=normalized; binding.paint(normalized,true) end
                    warn("[DaDa Config] "..id..": "..tostring(err))
                    return false,tostring(err)
                end
            end
            -- Read back the authoritative state: a callback may reject a change.
            binding.value=normalized
            binding.paint(binding.read(),true)
            return true
        end
        uiConfig.bindings[#uiConfig.bindings+1]=binding
        uiConfig.byId[id]=binding
        if binding.legacy then
            local alias=binding.legacy:match("^[^_]+_(.+)$")
            if alias then uiRegistry[alias]=function(value) return binding.paint(value,true) end end
        end
        binding.paint(binding.read(),false)
        return binding
    end
    function uiConfig.colorName(value,colors,fallback)
        for name,color in pairs(colors) do
            if value==color then return name end
        end
        return fallback
    end
    uiConfig.overlayColors={Purple=Color3.fromRGB(190,90,255),Red=Color3.fromRGB(255,65,80),
        Blue=Color3.fromRGB(70,155,255),Green=Color3.fromRGB(80,255,140),
        White=Color3.fromRGB(245,245,255),Gold=Color3.fromRGB(255,190,65)}
    function uiConfig.sync()
        if uiConfig.blocked() then return end
        for _,binding in ipairs(uiConfig.bindings) do
            local value=binding.read()
            if value~=binding.value then binding.paint(value,false) end
        end
        ST.quick_state.silent=SL.enabled==true
        ST.quick_state.esp=ST.esp_on==true
        ST.quick_state.fly=ST.fly==true
        ST.quick_state.bhop=ST.bhop==true
        ST.quick_state.inf_jump=ST.inf_jump==true
        ST.quick_state.getgun=ST.getgun==true
        ST.quick_state.auto_shoot=SL.auto_on==true
        ST.quick_state.invisible=SHF.invisible==true
    end
    getgenv().DaDa_ConfigUI=uiConfig
    do
        local elapsed=0
        table.insert(espLifecycle.connections,RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
            elapsed=elapsed+dt
            if elapsed<0.2 then return end
            elapsed=0
            if gui and gui.Parent then uiConfig.sync() end
        end))
        table.insert(espLifecycle.cleanups,function()
            if getgenv().DaDa_ConfigUI==uiConfig then getgenv().DaDa_ConfigUI=nil end
        end)
    end

    -- Native V5 widgets, original callbacks and original config keys.
    makePanel=function(tabIndex,side,title)
        local tab=specialTabs[title] or menuTabs[tabIndex]
        assert(tab,"Unknown DaDa tab: "..tostring(tabIndex))
        local section=tab:AddSection(title,specialTabs[title] and "left" or side)
        local P={body=section.Body,section=section}
        local configPanel=tostring(tabIndex).."/"..side.."/"..title
        local function idFor(kind,label,spec)
            return (spec and spec.id) or (configPanel.."/"..kind.."/"..label)
        end
        local function event(binding,control,value)
            if not binding then return end
            if uiConfig.blocked() then control:Set(binding.read(),true); return end
            local ok= binding.apply(value)
            if not ok then control:Set(binding.read(),true) end
            uiConfig.sync()
        end
        function P:Mount(frame,name)
            if frame:IsA("TextButton") then
                frame.BackgroundColor3=pal.control
                frame.BackgroundTransparency=0
                frame.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,26)
                frame.TextColor3=pal.text
                frame.TextSize=11
                frame.AutoButtonColor=false
            end
            return section:Mount(frame,name)
        end
        function P:Toggle(label,default,callback,flag,spec)
            local binding,control
            control=section:AddToggle({Name=label,Flag=idFor("Toggle",label,spec),Default=default,
                Callback=function(value) event(binding,control,value) end})
            binding=uiConfig.add("Toggle",configPanel.."/Toggle/"..label,default==true,callback,
                function(value) control:Set(value,true) end,spec,function(value)
                    if type(value)~="boolean" then return false,"expected boolean" end
                    return true,value
                end)
            if flag then uiRegistry[flag]=function(value) return binding.paint(value,true) end end
            binding.control=control
            return binding
        end
        function P:Slider(label,default,min,max,callback,spec)
            local binding,control
            local step=(spec and spec.step) or ((default%1~=0 or min%1~=0 or max%1~=0) and 0.01 or 1)
            control=section:AddSlider({Name=label,Flag=idFor("Slider",label,spec),Default=default,
                Min=min,Max=max,Increment=step,Callback=function(value) event(binding,control,value) end})
            binding=uiConfig.add("Slider",configPanel.."/Slider/"..label,default,callback,
                function(value) control:Set(value,true) end,spec,function(value)
                    if type(value)~="number" or value~=value or math.abs(value)==math.huge then return false,"expected finite number" end
                    if value<min-0.000001 or value>max+0.000001 then return false,"outside slider range" end
                    return true,math.clamp(math.floor(value/step+0.5)*step,min,max)
                end)
            binding.control=control
            return binding
        end
        function P:Dropdown(label,values,default,callback,spec)
            local binding,control
            control=section:AddDropdown({Name=label,Flag=idFor("Dropdown",label,spec),Options=values,
                Default=default,Callback=function(value) event(binding,control,value) end})
            binding=uiConfig.add("Dropdown",configPanel.."/Dropdown/"..label,default,callback,
                function(value) control:Set(value,true) end,spec,function(value)
                    if type(value)~="string" then return false,"expected dropdown string" end
                    for _,item in ipairs(values) do if item==value then return true,value end end
                    return false,"unknown dropdown option: "..value
                end)
            function binding.setOptions(items)
                local copy={}
                for _,value in ipairs(items or {}) do copy[#copy+1]=value end
                table.clear(values)
                for _,value in ipairs(copy) do values[#values+1]=value end
                if #values==0 then values[1]="loading..." end
                control:SetOptions(values)
                binding.paint(binding.read(),false)
            end
            binding.control=control
            return binding
        end
        function P:Button(label,callback)
            return section:AddButton({Name=label,Callback=function()
                if uiConfig.blocked() then return end
                if callback then callback() end
                uiConfig.sync()
            end})
        end
        function P:Label(label) return section:AddLabel(label) end
        function P:Textbox(options) return section:AddTextbox(options) end
        return P
    end
    local visualColors={
        names={"Default","Black","White","Gray","Red","Green","Blue","Yellow","Orange","Purple","Pink","Cyan","Gold"},
        values={Black=Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0),White=Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255),Gray=Color3.fromRGB(128,128,128),
            Red=Color3.fromRGB(255,50,50),Green=Color3.fromRGB(50,255,90),Blue=Color3.fromRGB(50,110,255),
            Yellow=Color3.fromRGB(255,255,0),Orange=Color3.fromRGB(255,130,0),Purple=Color3.fromRGB(170,70,255),
            Pink=Color3.fromRGB(255,100,190),Cyan=Color3.fromRGB(0,220,255),Gold=Color3.fromRGB(255,195,45)}
    }
    -- COMBAT
    local cA=makePanel(1,"left","Silent Aim")
    cA:Toggle("Silent Aim",false,function(v)
        SL.enabled=v
        ST.quick_state.silent=v
        if v then task.spawn(function() pcall(SL_installHooks); pcall(SL_refresh) end)
        else if _G.DaDa_SL_restore_origin then pcall(_G.DaDa_SL_restore_origin) end end
    end,"silent",{get=function() return SL.enabled end, legacy="SL_enabled"})
    cA:Toggle("Prediction",true,function(v) SL.predict=v end,nil,{get=function() return SL.predict end, legacy="SL_predict"})
    cA:Toggle("Force Shoot",false,function(v)
        SL.force=v
        if not v and _G.DaDa_SL_restore_origin then pcall(_G.DaDa_SL_restore_origin) end
    end,nil,{get=function() return SL.force end, legacy="SL_force"})
    cA:Slider("Origin",15,0,40,function(v) SL.stand_off=v end,{get=function() return SL.stand_off end, legacy="SL_stand_off"})
    cA:Dropdown("Hit Part",{"Head","UpperTorso","Torso","HumanoidRootPart"},"UpperTorso",function(v) ST.hit_part=v end,{get=function() return ST.hit_part end, legacy="ST_hit_part"})
    local cB=makePanel(1,"right","Auto Shoot")
    cB:Toggle("Auto Shoot",false,function(v) SL.auto_on=v; ST.quick_state.auto_shoot=v end,nil,{get=function() return SL.auto_on end, legacy="SL_auto_on"})
    cB:Slider("Auto Delay",0,0,600,function(v) SL.auto_delay=v/1000 end,{get=function() return SL.auto_delay*1000 end, legacy="SL_auto_delay", fromLegacy=function(v) return v*1000 end})
    local cInvis=makePanel(1,"left","Invisible")
    cInvis:Toggle("Invisible",false,function(v)
        SHF.invisToggle(v)
    end,"invisible",{get=function() return SHF.invisible==true end,legacy="SHF_invisible"})
    local cLock=makePanel(1,"right","Aim Lock")
    cLock:Toggle("Aim Lock",false,function(v) ST.aim_lock=v end,nil,{get=function() return ST.aim_lock end})
    cLock:Toggle("Prediction",true,function(v) ST.aim_lock_predict=v end,nil,{get=function() return ST.aim_lock_predict end})
    cLock:Dropdown("Target",{"Nearest","Sheriff","Murder"},"Nearest",function(v) ST.aim_lock_target=v end,{get=function() return ST.aim_lock_target end})
    cLock:Slider("Prediction ms",120,0,500,function(v) ST.aim_lock_lead=v/1000 end,{get=function() return ST.aim_lock_lead*1000 end})
    local cC=makePanel(1,"left","Get Gun")
    cC:Toggle("Auto Get Gun",false,function(v) ST.getgun=v; ST.quick_state.getgun=v end,nil,{get=function() return ST.getgun end, legacy="ST_getgun"})
    cC:Dropdown("Mode",{"Touch","Teleport","PlayerTP","AutoGrab"},"Touch",function(v) ST.getgun_mode=v end,{get=function() return ST.getgun_mode end, legacy="ST_getgun_mode"})
    cC:Slider("Interval",15,5,100,function(v) ST.getgun_interval=v/100 end,{get=function() return ST.getgun_interval*100 end, legacy="ST_getgun_interval", fromLegacy=function(v) return v*100 end})
    cC:Toggle("Teleport Back",true,function(v) ST.getgun_tp_back=v end,nil,{get=function() return ST.getgun_tp_back end, legacy="ST_getgun_tp_back"})
    local cD=makePanel(1,"right","Knife / Murderer")
    cD:Toggle("Knife Throwing",false,function(v) SHF.setKnifeThrowing(v) end,nil,{id="1/right/Knife / Murderer/Toggle/Auto Knife Throw",get=function() return ST.auto_knife_throw end, legacy="ST_auto_knife_throw"})
    cD:Toggle("Knife Prediction",true,function(v) ST.knife_predict=v end,nil,{get=function() return ST.knife_predict end, legacy="ST_knife_predict"})
    cD:Slider("Knife Lead %",100,0,300,function(v) ST.knife_lead=v end,{get=function() return ST.knife_lead end, legacy="ST_knife_lead"})
    cD:Slider("Knife Range",400,10,2000,function(v) ST.knife_range=v end,{get=function() return ST.knife_range end,legacy="ST_knife_range"})
    cD:Slider("Knife Speed",96,40,400,function(v) ST.knife_speed=v end,{get=function() return ST.knife_speed end,legacy="ST_knife_speed"})
    cD:Slider("Knife Air %",35,0,120,function(v) ST.knife_air=v end,{get=function() return ST.knife_air end,legacy="ST_knife_air"})
    cD:Slider("Knife Offset ms",0,-80,220,function(v) ST.knife_offset=v end,{get=function() return ST.knife_offset end,legacy="ST_knife_offset"})
    cD:Slider("Auto Throw Interval",1,0.25,3,function(v) ST.knife_interval=v end,{get=function() return ST.knife_interval end})
    cD:Dropdown("Auto Throw Mode",{"Remote","Game Input"},"Remote",function(v) ST.knife_auto_mode=v end,{get=function() return ST.knife_auto_mode end})
    cD:Dropdown("Throw Input",{"E","Right Mouse"},"E",function(v) ST.knife_input=v end,{get=function() return ST.knife_input end,legacy="ST_knife_input"})
    cD:Slider("Throw Hold ms",150,50,500,function(v) ST.knife_hold=v/1000 end,{get=function() return ST.knife_hold*1000 end,legacy="ST_knife_hold",fromLegacy=function(v) return v*1000 end})
    cD:Button("Knife Diagnostics",function() if SHF.knifeDiagnostics then SHF.knifeDiagnostics() end end)
    cD:Toggle("Kill Aura",false,function(v) ST.killaura=v end,nil,{get=function() return ST.killaura end, legacy="ST_killaura"})
    cD:Toggle("Auto Knife",false,function(v) ST.autoknife=v end,nil,{get=function() return ST.autoknife end, legacy="ST_autoknife"})
    cD:Slider("Kill Distance",30,5,60,function(v) ST.killaura_dist=v end,{get=function() return ST.killaura_dist end, legacy="ST_killaura_dist"})

    do
        -- Server-local target selection is intentionally not saved in configs.
        local panel=makePanel(1,"left","Teleport")
        local placeholder="Select player"
        local selectedName=placeholder
        local resolvingRole=false
        local picker=panel.section:AddDropdown({
            Name="Player",Flag="dada/teleport/player",Options={placeholder},Default=placeholder,
            Callback=function(value)
                if not uiConfig.blocked() then selectedName=value end
            end
        })
        local function refreshPlayers(leaving)
            if espLifecycle.stopped then return end
            local names={}
            for _,other in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if other~=LP and other~=leaving then names[#names+1]=other.Name end
            end
            table.sort(names,function(a,b) return string.lower(a)<string.lower(b) end)
            local available=false
            for _,name in ipairs(names) do
                if name==selectedName then available=true; break end
            end
            if not available then selectedName=placeholder end
            table.insert(names,1,placeholder)
            picker:SetOptions(names)
            picker:Set(selectedName,true)
        end
        local function aliveRoot(other)
            local character=other and other.Character
            local humanoid=character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local root=character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if character and character.Parent and humanoid and humanoid.Health>0
                and root and root:IsA("BasePart") then
                return root,character
            end
        end
        local function moveTo(destination)
            if espLifecycle.stopped then return end
            local root,character=aliveRoot(LP)
            if not root then notify("Teleport","Your character is not alive"); return end
            local ok,err=pcall(function()
                -- Preserve the root-to-model-pivot offset, including custom rigs.
                local pivotOffset=root.CFrame:ToObjectSpace(character:GetPivot())
                root.AssemblyLinearVelocity=Vector3.zero
                root.AssemblyAngularVelocity=Vector3.zero
                character:PivotTo(destination*pivotOffset)
            end)
            if not ok then
                warn("[DaDa Teleport] "..tostring(err))
                notify("Teleport","Teleport failed")
            end
        end
        local function teleportTo(other)
            if espLifecycle.stopped then return end
            if not other or other.Parent~=Players or other==LP then
                notify("Teleport","Select an available player")
                return
            end
            local targetRoot=aliveRoot(other)
            if not targetRoot then notify("Teleport","Target character is not alive"); return end
            moveTo(targetRoot.CFrame*CFrame.new(0,0,4))
        end
        local function teleportToRole(wanted)
            if resolvingRole or espLifecycle.stopped then return end
            resolvingRole=true
            task.spawn(function()
                local ok,err=pcall(function()
                    local roles={}
                    local modules=ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Modules")
                    -- Do not enter get_round's unbounded WaitForChild outside MM2.
                    if modules and modules:FindFirstChild("CurrentRoundClient") then
                        roles=get_roles()
                    end
                    if espLifecycle.stopped then return end
                    local ownRoot=aliveRoot(LP)
                    if not ownRoot then notify("Teleport","Your character is not alive"); return end
                    local chosen,best=nil,math.huge
                    for _,other in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                        local root=other~=LP and aliveRoot(other)
                        if root then
                            local role=roles[other.Name]
                            local character=other.Character
                            local backpack=other:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
                            if holds(character,"Knife") or holds(backpack,"Knife") then
                                role="Murderer"
                            elseif role~="Murderer" and (holds(character,"Gun") or holds(backpack,"Gun")) then
                                role="Sheriff"
                            end
                            local matches=role==wanted or (wanted=="Sheriff" and role=="Hero")
                            local distance=(root.Position-ownRoot.Position).Magnitude
                            if matches and distance<best then chosen,best=other,distance end
                        end
                    end
                    if chosen then teleportTo(chosen)
                    else notify("Teleport",wanted.." not found among living players") end
                end)
                resolvingRole=false
                if not ok and not espLifecycle.stopped then
                    warn("[DaDa Teleport] "..tostring(err))
                    notify("Teleport","Could not resolve the role")
                end
            end)
        end
        panel:Button("Teleport to Player",function()
            teleportTo(Players:FindFirstChild(selectedName))
        end)
        panel:Button("Teleport to Sheriff",function() teleportToRole("Sheriff") end)
        panel:Button("Teleport to Murderer",function() teleportToRole("Murderer") end)
        panel:Button("Teleport to Lobby",function()
            moveTo(CFrame.new(527.477722,461.372375,37.355587,1,0,0,0,1,0,0,0,1))
        end)
        panel:Button("Refresh Players",function() refreshPlayers() end)
        table.insert(espLifecycle.connections,Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function() refreshPlayers() end))
        table.insert(espLifecycle.connections,Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(refreshPlayers))
        refreshPlayers()
    end

    -- Vote Duper: uses the original Auto Vote behavior under the requested label.
    -- This is ordinary map voting; it does not fake or inflate server vote counts.

        SHF.mapvote_dupe=false
        SHF.mapvote=v
        if notify then pcall(function() notify("Vote Duper",v and "Enabled" or "Disabled") end) end
    end,"DaDaVoteDuper",{get=function() return SHF.mapvote end, legacy="SHF_mapvote"})

    voteHint.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,18)

    local voteGrid=Instance.new("UIGridLayout")

    local voteSlots={}
    local function deepFind(parent,name)
        if not parent then return nil end
        local found=parent:FindFirstChild(name,true)
        if found then return found end
        return nil
    end
    local function findVoteLobby()
        for _,lobbyName in ipairs({"RegularLobby","SummerLobby","Lobby"}) do
            local lobby=workspace:FindFirstChild(lobbyName) or workspace:FindFirstChild(lobbyName,true)
            if lobby then return lobby end
        end
        return nil
    end
    local function getImageSource(root)
        if not root then return "" end
        local function read(obj)
            if obj:IsA("ImageLabel") or obj:IsA("ImageButton") then
                return obj.Image
            elseif obj:IsA("Decal") or obj:IsA("Texture") then
                return obj.Texture
            end
            local ok,img=pcall(function() return obj.Image end)
            if ok and type(img)=="string" and img~="" then return img end
            return ""
        end
        local direct=read(root)
        if direct~="" then return direct end
        for _,obj in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
            local img=read(obj)
            if img~="" then return img end
        end
        return ""
    end
    local function getMapTitle(model)
        local voteInfo=deepFind(model,"VoteInfoGui")
        local mapInfo=deepFind(model,"MapInfoGui")
        local title=(voteInfo and deepFind(voteInfo,"MapName"))
            or (mapInfo and deepFind(mapInfo,"MapName"))
            or deepFind(model,"MapName")
        if title and (title:IsA("TextLabel") or title:IsA("TextButton") or title:IsA("TextBox")) then
            local value=title.Text
            if value and value~="" and value~="MAP NAME" then return value end
        end
        return nil
    end
    local function getMapImage(model)
        local mapInfo=deepFind(model,"MapInfoGui")
        local icon=(mapInfo and deepFind(mapInfo,"MapIcon")) or deepFind(model,"MapIcon")
        local img=getImageSource(icon or mapInfo)
        if img=="" then
            -- Some lobby versions put the map thumbnail outside MapInfoGui.
            img=getImageSource(model)
        end
        return img
    end
    for i=1,3 do
        local card=Instance.new("ImageButton")
        card.Name="MapCardSlot"..i
        card.LayoutOrder=i
        card.Size=UDim2.new(0.32,0,0,104)
        card.BackgroundColor3=pal.control
        card.BackgroundTransparency=0.05
        card.BorderSizePixel=0
        card.Image=""
        card.AutoButtonColor=true
        card.Parent=voteCards
        Instance.new("UICorner",card).CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,6)
        local image=Instance.new("ImageLabel")
        image.Name="MapImage"
        image.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,1,-24)
        image.BackgroundColor3=pal.card
        image.BackgroundTransparency=0
        image.Image=""
        image.ScaleType=Enum.ScaleType.Crop
        image.Parent=card
        image.ZIndex=card.ZIndex+1
        image.Active=false
        Instance.new("UICorner",image).CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,6)
        local name=Instance.new("TextLabel")
        name.Name="MapName"
        name.Size=UDim2.new(1,-4,0,22)
        name.Position=UDim2.new(0,2,1,-22)
        name.BackgroundColor3=pal.card
        name.BackgroundTransparency=0.05
        name.TextColor3=pal.text
        name.Text="Searching..."
        name.TextSize=9
        name.TextWrapped=true
        name.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold
        name.ZIndex=image.ZIndex+1
        name.Parent=card
        name.Active=false
        Instance.new("UICorner",name).CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,4)
        voteSlots[i]={card=card,image=image,name=name,mapName=nil,model=nil}
        card.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            local selectedMap=voteSlots[i].mapName
            if selectedMap and selectedMap~="" then
                local slot=voteSlots[i]
                -- The clicked card is the authoritative target for Auto Vote.
                getgenv().DaDa_SelectedMapName = selectedMap
                getgenv().DaDa_MapPriority = selectedMap
                getgenv().DaDa_SelectedVoteModel = slot and slot.model or nil
                -- Restart the worker so it immediately re-reads the selected map.
                -- Selection is shared by Auto Vote and VoteDupe; restart either active worker.
                local restartAutoVote = SHF.mapvote
                local restartVoteDupe = SHF.mapvote_dupe
                if restartAutoVote or restartVoteDupe then
                    SHF.mapvote = false
                    SHF.mapvote_dupe = false
                    task.delay(0.12, function()
                        if voteCards and voteCards.Parent and getgenv().DaDa_SelectedMapName == selectedMap then
                            SHF.mapvote = restartAutoVote
                            SHF.mapvote_dupe = restartVoteDupe
                        end
                    end)
                end
                if notify then pcall(function() notify("Auto Vote","Selected map: "..selectedMap) end) end
            else
                if notify then pcall(function() notify("Auto Vote","Map not detected yet. Wait for the lobby cards to load.") end) end
            end
        end)
    end
    local function refreshVoteCards()
        local lobby=findVoteLobby()
        local padsRoot=lobby and deepFind(lobby,"VotePads")
        local candidates={}
        local seen={}
        local function addCandidate(model)
            if not model or seen[model] then return end
            local mapName=getMapTitle(model)
            if mapName then
                seen[model]=true
                local pad=deepFind(model,"Pad")
                if not (pad and pad:IsA("BasePart")) then
                    pad=model:IsA("BasePart") and model or model:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart",true)
                end
                local pos=pad and pad.Position or Vector3.zero
                candidates[#candidates+1]={name=mapName,image=getMapImage(model),model=model,x=pos.X,z=pos.Z}
            end
        end
        if padsRoot then
            -- The game's VotePads child order is the canonical slot order. Do not sort by
            -- map name or world coordinates: those can separate an image from its pad.
            for _,obj in ipairs(padsRoot:GetChildren()) do addCandidate(obj) end
            -- Some lobby variants wrap the three vote models in one extra folder.
            if #candidates==0 then
                for _,obj in ipairs(padsRoot:GetDescendants()) do
                    if obj.Name:match("VotePad%d*") or obj.Name:match("Detector%d*") then addCandidate(obj) end
                end
            end
        end
        if #candidates==0 and lobby then
            for _,obj in ipairs(lobby:GetDescendants()) do
                if obj.Name:match("VotePad%d*") or obj.Name:match("Detector%d*") then addCandidate(obj) end
            end
        end
        for i,slot in ipairs(voteSlots) do
            local entry=candidates[i]
            if entry then
                slot.mapName=entry.name
                slot.model=entry.model
                slot.name.Text=entry.name
                slot.image.Image=entry.image or ""
                slot.card.Visible=true
            else
                slot.mapName=nil
                slot.model=nil
                slot.name.Text="Waiting for map..."
                slot.image.Image=""
                slot.card.Visible=true
            end
        end
        voteHint.Text=(#candidates>0) and "Tap a map to prioritize Auto Vote" or "Waiting for lobby map cards..."
    end
    task.spawn(function()
        while voteCards.Parent do
            pcall(refreshVoteCards)
            task.wait(1)
        end
    end)
