    -- Shared with the later Gun Beam controls in this same initMenu scope.
    local vB
    -- VISUALS: selection is an editing mask; each role keeps its own live profile.
    do
        local api=ST._espRoles
        local roles=makePanel(4,"left","ESP Roles")
        roles:Toggle("ESP",ST.esp_on,function(v) ST.esp_on=v; ST.quick_state.esp=v end,"esp",
            {get=function() return ST.esp_on end,legacy="ST_esp_on"})
        roles:Label("Select roles to EDIT together (not visibility)")
        for _,role in ipairs(api.order) do
            roles:Toggle(role,true,function(v) api.selected[role]=v end,nil,
                {get=function() return api.selected[role] end})
        end
        local summary=roles:Label("")
        local function updateSummary()
            local selected={}
            for _,role in ipairs(api.order) do if api.selected[role] then selected[#selected+1]=role end end
            summary:Set(#selected==0 and "No edit targets selected" or "Editing: "..table.concat(selected,", "))
        end
        -- Persistent, per-role values. Old global ESP configs migrate into every role.
        for _,role in ipairs(api.order) do
            local profile=api.profiles[role]
            for _,key in ipairs(api.fields) do
                local default=profile[key]
                local function validate(value)
                    if key=="esp_color" then
                        if value=="Role" or api.colors[value] then return true,value end
                        return false,"unknown ESP color"
                    elseif key=="esp_max_dist" then
                        if type(value)=="number" and value==value and value>=100 and value<=3000 then return true,value end
                        return false,"ESP distance must be 100..3000"
                    end
                    if type(value)=="boolean" then return true,value end
                    return false,"expected boolean"
                end
                uiConfig.add("Value","esp-profile/"..role.."/"..key,default,
                    function(value) profile[key]=value end,function() end,
                    {get=function() return profile[key] end,legacy=key:sub(1,4)=="esp_" and key~="esp_color" and ("ST_"..key) or nil},validate)
            end
        end
        local effects=makePanel(4,"left","ESP Chams Settings")
        local basic=makePanel(4,"left","ESP Settings")
        local extra=makePanel(4,"right","ESP Extras")
        vB=extra
        local function groupToggle(panel,label,key)
            local binding=panel:Toggle(label,api.read(key),function(v) api.write(key,v) end,nil,
                {get=function() return api.read(key) end})
            binding.transient=true
        end
        groupToggle(basic,"Enable Profile","enabled")
        for _,item in ipairs({{"Outline","esp_outline"},{"Chams","esp_chams"},{"Cube v1","esp_cube_v1"},
            {"Cube v2","esp_cube_v2"},{"Solid Body","esp_solid_body"},{"Fill Chams","esp_fill_chams"}}) do
            groupToggle(effects,item[1],item[2])
        end
        for _,item in ipairs({{"Box","esp_box"},{"Name","esp_name"},{"Distance","esp_dist"},{"Health Bar","esp_health"}}) do
            groupToggle(basic,item[1],item[2])
        end
        for _,item in ipairs({{"Skeleton","esp_skel"},{"Snapline","esp_line"},{"Flags","esp_flags"}}) do
            groupToggle(extra,item[1],item[2])
        end
        extra:Slider("Max Distance",1000,100,3000,function(v) api.write("esp_max_dist",v) end,
            {get=function() return api.read("esp_max_dist") end}).transient=true
        effects:Dropdown("ESP Color",{"Role","Red","Blue","Green","White","Purple","Gold","Cyan"},"Role",
            function(v) api.write("esp_color",v) end,{get=function() return api.read("esp_color") end}).transient=true
        extra:Label("Mixed values: showing the preview / first selected role")
        local previewPanel=makePanel(4,"right","ESP Preview")
        previewPanel:Dropdown("Preview Role",api.order,api.preview,function(v) api.preview=v end,
            {get=function() return api.preview end})
        previewPanel:Label("Preview ignores master ESP; changes are live")
        -- ViewportFrame does not render Highlight. Separate mesh silhouettes and a
        -- tinted viewport reproduce its fill/outline without touching the real avatar.
        local host=Instance.new("Frame")
        host.Name="LocalPlayerESPPreview"; host.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,300)
        host.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(18,19,26); host.BorderSizePixel=0
        host.ClipsDescendants=true
        local rounding=Instance.new("UICorner"); rounding.CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,7); rounding.Parent=host
        previewPanel:Mount(host,"Rotating local player preview")
        local function viewport(name,z)
            local view=Instance.new("ViewportFrame")
            view.Name=name; view.Size=UDim2.fromScale(1,1); view.BackgroundTransparency=1
            view.BorderSizePixel=0; view.ZIndex=z; view.Ambient=Color3.new(1,1,1)
            view.LightColor=Color3.new(0,0,0); view.Parent=host
            local world=Instance.new("WorldModel"); world.Parent=view
            local camera=Instance.new("Camera"); camera.FieldOfView=35; camera.Parent=view
            view.CurrentCamera=camera
            return view,world,camera
        end
        local outlineView,outlineWorld,outlineCamera=viewport("OutlineSilhouette",2)
        local bodyView,bodyWorld,bodyCamera=viewport("Avatar",3)
        local tintView,tintWorld,tintCamera=viewport("ChamsFill",4)
        local cubeView,cubeWorld,cubeCamera=viewport("Cubes",5)
        local function frame(name,color)
            local object=Instance.new("Frame"); object.Name=name; object.BorderSizePixel=0
            object.BackgroundColor3=color or Color3.new(1,1,1); object.ZIndex=8
            object.Visible=false; object.Parent=host
            return object
        end
        local function label(name,size)
            local object=Instance.new("TextLabel"); object.Name=name
            object.BackgroundTransparency=1; object.Font=Enum.Font.Gotham
            object.TextSize=size; object.TextStrokeTransparency=0.25
            object.TextColor3=Color3.new(1,1,1); object.ZIndex=9
            object.Visible=false; object.Parent=host; return object
        end
        local box=frame("Box"); box.BackgroundTransparency=1
        local boxStroke=Instance.new("UIStroke"); boxStroke.Thickness=1.5; boxStroke.Parent=box
        local nameText=label("Name",12); local distanceText=label("Distance",11)
        local flagText=label("Role",11); local statusText=label("Status",10)
        statusText.Visible=true; statusText.Size=UDim2.new(1,-12,0,18)
        statusText.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(6,6); statusText.Text="Loading character..."
        local healthBG=frame("HealthBackground",Color3.new(0,0,0))
        local healthBar=frame("HealthBar")
        local snapline=frame("Snapline")
        local bones={}
        for i=1,20 do bones[i]=frame("Bone"..i) end
        local rig6={{"Head","Torso"},{"Torso","Left Arm"},{"Torso","Right Arm"},{"Torso","Left Leg"},{"Torso","Right Leg"}}
        local rig15={{"Head","UpperTorso"},{"UpperTorso","LowerTorso"},{"LowerTorso","LeftUpperLeg"},
            {"LeftUpperLeg","LeftLowerLeg"},{"LeftLowerLeg","LeftFoot"},{"LowerTorso","RightUpperLeg"},
            {"RightUpperLeg","RightLowerLeg"},{"RightLowerLeg","RightFoot"},{"UpperTorso","LeftUpperArm"},
            {"LeftUpperArm","LeftLowerArm"},{"LeftLowerArm","LeftHand"},{"UpperTorso","RightUpperArm"},
            {"RightUpperArm","RightLowerArm"},{"RightLowerArm","RightHand"}}
        local model,neutralPivot,characterSource
        local fills,outlines,rigParts,voxels={},{},{},{}
        local radius,height,angle=4,6,0
        local rebuild=true
        local cubeMode=""
        local function clearPreview()
            bodyWorld:ClearAllChildren(); outlineWorld:ClearAllChildren()
            tintWorld:ClearAllChildren(); cubeWorld:ClearAllChildren()
            model=nil; characterSource=nil; neutralPivot=nil
            fills={}; outlines={}; rigParts={}; voxels={}; cubeMode=""
        end
        local function monoPart(source,parent,grow)
            local copy=source:Clone()
            for _,child in ipairs(copy:GetDescendants()) do
                if child:IsA("DataModelMesh") then
                    if child:IsA("SpecialMesh") then
                        child.TextureId=""; child.VertexColor=Vector3.new(1,1,1)
                        if grow then child.Scale=child.Scale*1.045 end
                    end
                else child:Destroy() end
            end
            if copy:IsA("MeshPart") then copy.TextureID="" end
            copy.Anchored=true; copy.CanCollide=false; copy.CanTouch=false; copy.CanQuery=false
            copy.CastShadow=false; copy.Material=Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
            copy.Reflectance=0; copy.Transparency=0; copy.LocalTransparencyModifier=0
            if grow then copy.Size=copy.Size*1.045 end
            copy.Parent=parent; return copy
        end
        local function buildPreview()
            local source=LP.Character
            if not source or not source:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                clearPreview(); statusText.Text="Waiting for character..."; return
            end
            local old=source.Archivable
            local ok,copy=pcall(function() source.Archivable=true; return source:Clone() end)
            pcall(function() source.Archivable=old end)
            if not ok or not copy then statusText.Text="Character clone unavailable"; return end
            clearPreview()
            -- Destroy executable objects BEFORE attaching anything to a WorldModel.
            for _,object in ipairs(copy:GetDescendants()) do
                if object:IsA("LuaSourceContainer") or object:IsA("Tool")
                    or object:IsA("Highlight") or object:IsA("PVAdornment")
                    or object:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or object:IsA("Trail")
                    or object:IsA("Beam") or object:IsA("Sound")
                    or object:IsA("BillboardGui") or object:IsA("SurfaceGui") then
                    object:Destroy()
                elseif object:IsA("BasePart") then
                    object.Anchored=true; object.CanCollide=false; object.CanTouch=false
                    object.CanQuery=false; object.LocalTransparencyModifier=0
                end
            end
            for _,object in ipairs(copy:GetDescendants()) do
                if object:IsA("JointInstance") or object:IsA("Constraint") then object:Destroy() end
            end
            model=copy; characterSource=source
            local center,size=model:GetBoundingBox()
            model:PivotTo(CFrame.new(-center.Position)*model:GetPivot())
            neutralPivot=model:GetPivot(); height=math.max(size.Y,1)
            radius=math.max(size.X,size.Z,height)*0.5
            model.Parent=bodyWorld
            for _,part in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
                if part:IsA("BasePart") and part.Name~="HumanoidRootPart" and part.Transparency<1 then
                    local isRig=part.Parent==model
                    fills[#fills+1]={source=part,copy=monoPart(part,tintWorld,false),rig=isRig}
                    outlines[#outlines+1]={source=part,copy=monoPart(part,outlineWorld,true)}
                    if isRig then rigParts[#rigParts+1]=part end
                end
            end
            rebuild=false
        end
        local function newVoxel(part,size,offset,transparency)
            local copy=Instance.new("Part"); copy.Anchored=true
            copy.CanCollide=false; copy.CanTouch=false; copy.CanQuery=false
            copy.Material=Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic; copy.Size=size
            copy.Transparency=transparency; copy.Parent=cubeWorld
            voxels[#voxels+1]={source=part,copy=copy,offset=offset}
        end
        local function updateCubes(cfg,col)
            local mode=(cfg.esp_cube_v1 and "1" or "0")..(cfg.esp_cube_v2 and "1" or "0")
            if mode~=cubeMode then
                cubeWorld:ClearAllChildren(); voxels={}; cubeMode=mode
                if cfg.esp_cube_v1 then
                    local root=model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if root then
                        local low=Vector3.new(math.huge,math.huge,math.huge)
                        local high=Vector3.new(-math.huge,-math.huge,-math.huge)
                        for _,part in ipairs(rigParts) do
                            local relative=root.CFrame:ToObjectSpace(part.CFrame)
                            for x=-1,1,2 do for y=-1,1,2 do for z=-1,1,2 do
                                local v=relative:PointToWorldSpace(part.Size*Vector3.new(x,y,z)/2)
                                low=Vector3.new(math.min(low.X,v.X),math.min(low.Y,v.Y),math.min(low.Z,v.Z))
                                high=Vector3.new(math.max(high.X,v.X),math.max(high.Y,v.Y),math.max(high.Z,v.Z))
                            end end end
                        end
                        if #rigParts>0 then newVoxel(root,high-low+Vector3.new(0.15,0.15,0.15),CFrame.new((high+low)/2),0.72) end
                    end
                end
                if cfg.esp_cube_v2 then
                    for _,part in ipairs(rigParts) do
                        local size=part.Size
                        local nx,ny,nz=math.clamp(math.ceil(size.X/0.7),1,4),math.clamp(math.ceil(size.Y/0.7),1,6),math.clamp(math.ceil(size.Z/0.7),1,4)
                        local cell=Vector3.new(size.X/nx,size.Y/ny,size.Z/nz)
                        local edge=math.min(cell.X,cell.Y,cell.Z)*0.88
                        for x=1,nx do for y=1,ny do for z=1,nz do
                            newVoxel(part,Vector3.new(edge,edge,edge),CFrame.new(-size.X/2+(x-0.5)*cell.X,-size.Y/2+(y-0.5)*cell.Y,-size.Z/2+(z-0.5)*cell.Z),0.18)
                        end end end
                    end
                end
            end
            for _,pair in ipairs(voxels) do pair.copy.CFrame=pair.source.CFrame*pair.offset; pair.copy.Color=col end
        end
        local function shown()
            local node=host
            while node do
                if node:IsA("GuiObject") and not node.Visible then return false end
                if node:IsA("ScreenGui") and not node.Enabled then return false end
                node=node.Parent
            end
            return host.Parent~=nil
        end
        local function project(point,w,h)
            local p=bodyCamera.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(point)
            if p.Z>=-0.01 then return nil end
            local scale=h/(2*math.tan(math.rad(bodyCamera.FieldOfView/2)))
            return Vector2.new(w/2+p.X*scale/-p.Z,h/2-p.Y*scale/-p.Z)
        end
        local function segment(object,a,b,col)
            local delta=b-a
            object.AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5,0.5)
            object.Position=UDim2.fromOffset((a.X+b.X)/2,(a.Y+b.Y)/2)
            object.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(delta.Magnitude,1)
            object.Rotation=math.deg(math.atan2(delta.Y,delta.X))
            object.BackgroundColor3=col; object.Visible=true
        end
        local function textAt(object,value,x,y,width,col,visible)
            object.Text=value; object.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(x,y)
            object.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(width,16); object.TextColor3=col; object.Visible=visible
        end
        local overlays={box,nameText,distanceText,flagText,healthBG,healthBar,snapline}
        -- AbsoluteSize already includes every ancestor UIScale, but Offset values
        -- written to the overlays get scaled again. Work in unscaled units.
        local function guiScale(object)
            local s=1; local node=object
            while node and not node:IsA("LayerCollector") do
                local sc=node:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIScale")
                if sc then s=s*sc.Scale end
                node=node.Parent
            end
            return (s>0.001) and s or 1
        end
        local function drawPreview(dt)
            if not shown() then return end
            for _,object in ipairs(overlays) do object.Visible=false end
            for _,object in ipairs(bones) do object.Visible=false end
            if rebuild or characterSource~=LP.Character then buildPreview() end
            if not model then return end
            local uiS=guiScale(host)
            local w,h=host.AbsoluteSize.X/uiS,host.AbsoluteSize.Y/uiS
            if w<2 or h<2 then return end
            local cfg=api.profiles[api.preview]; local col=api.color(api.preview,cfg)
            -- Negative yaw turns the front of the avatar towards the viewer's right.
            angle=(angle+math.rad(12)*dt)%(2*math.pi)
            model:PivotTo(CFrame.Angles(0,math.pi-angle,0)*neutralPivot)
            local halfFov=math.tan(math.rad(35/2))
            local d=math.max(height*0.5/halfFov,radius/(halfFov*(w/h)))+radius
            local cameraCF=CFrame.lookAt(Vector3.new(0,0,d*1.16),Vector3.zero)
            for _,camera in ipairs({bodyCamera,tintCamera,outlineCamera,cubeCamera}) do camera.CFrame=cameraCF end
            outlineView.Visible=cfg.enabled and cfg.esp_outline
            tintView.Visible=cfg.enabled and (cfg.esp_chams or cfg.esp_fill_chams or cfg.esp_solid_body)
            tintView.ImageTransparency=(cfg.esp_fill_chams or cfg.esp_solid_body) and 0 or 0.5
            cubeView.Visible=cfg.enabled and (cfg.esp_cube_v1 or cfg.esp_cube_v2)
            for _,pair in ipairs(fills) do
                pair.copy.CFrame=pair.source.CFrame; pair.copy.Color=col
                pair.copy.Transparency=(cfg.esp_solid_body and not (cfg.esp_chams or cfg.esp_fill_chams) and not pair.rig) and 1 or 0
            end
            for _,pair in ipairs(outlines) do pair.copy.CFrame=pair.source.CFrame; pair.copy.Color=col end
            if cubeView.Visible then updateCubes(cfg,col) end
            statusText.Text=api.preview..(cfg.enabled and "  |  12 deg/s" or "  |  profile disabled")
            statusText.TextColor3=col
            if not cfg.enabled then return end
            local root=model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not root then return end
            local loX,loY,hiX,hiY=math.huge,math.huge,-math.huge,-math.huge
            -- Same fixed root-relative bounds as the in-world Drawing ESP.
            for x=-1,1,2 do for y=-1,1,2 do for z=-1,1,2 do
                local p=project(root.CFrame:PointToWorldSpace(Vector3.new(x*2,y*2.75,z)),w,h)
                if not p then return end
                loX=math.min(loX,p.X); loY=math.min(loY,p.Y); hiX=math.max(hiX,p.X); hiY=math.max(hiY,p.Y)
            end end end
            local bw,bh=hiX-loX,hiY-loY
            box.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(loX,loY); box.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(bw,bh)
            boxStroke.Color=col; box.Visible=cfg.esp_box
            textAt(nameText,LP.Name,loX-40,loY-18,bw+80,col,cfg.esp_name)
            textAt(distanceText,"10m",loX,hiY+2,bw,col,cfg.esp_dist)
            textAt(flagText,api.preview,loX-20,hiY+(cfg.esp_dist and 18 or 2),bw+40,col,cfg.esp_flags)
            healthBG.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(loX-6,loY); healthBG.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(3,bh)
            local hum=characterSource and characterSource:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local hf=hum and math.clamp(hum.Health/math.max(hum.MaxHealth,1),0,1) or 1
            healthBar.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(loX-6,loY+bh*(1-hf)); healthBar.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(3,bh*hf)
            healthBar.BackgroundColor3=col; healthBar.Visible=cfg.esp_health; healthBG.Visible=cfg.esp_health
            if cfg.esp_line then segment(snapline,Vector2.new(w/2,h),Vector2.new((loX+hiX)/2,hiY),col) end
            if cfg.esp_skel then
                local rig=model:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") and rig15 or rig6
                for i,pair in ipairs(rig) do
                    local a,b=model:FindFirstChild(pair[1]),model:FindFirstChild(pair[2])
                    if a and b then
                        local ap,bp=project(a.Position,w,h),project(b.Position,w,h)
                        if ap and bp then segment(bones[i],ap,bp,col) end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        table.insert(espLifecycle.connections,LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function() rebuild=true end))
        table.insert(espLifecycle.connections,LP.CharacterAppearanceLoaded:Connect(function() rebuild=true end))
        local previewWarning=0
        table.insert(espLifecycle.connections,RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
            if espLifecycle.stopped then return end
            local ok,err=pcall(drawPreview,dt)
            if not ok then
                if os.clock()-previewWarning>5 then previewWarning=os.clock(); warn("[DaDa ESP Preview] "..tostring(err)) end
                statusText.Text="Preview unavailable (see console)"
            end
        end))
        table.insert(espLifecycle.cleanups,function() clearPreview(); host:Destroy() end)
        updateSummary()
        local summaryTime=0
        table.insert(espLifecycle.connections,RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
            summaryTime=summaryTime+dt
            if summaryTime>=0.2 then summaryTime=0; updateSummary() end
        end))
    end

    local vArrows=makePanel(4,"right","Arrows")
    vArrows:Toggle("Arrows",false,function(v) ST.arrows=v end,nil,{get=function() return ST.arrows end})
    vArrows:Dropdown("Mode",{"Always","Hide On Screen"},"Always",function(v) ST.arrows_mode=v end,{get=function() return ST.arrows_mode end})
    vArrows:Slider("Distance from Center",160,40,520,function(v) ST.arrows_radius=v end,{get=function() return ST.arrows_radius end})
    vArrows:Slider("Arrow Size",26,12,64,function(v) ST.arrows_size=v end,{get=function() return ST.arrows_size end})

    local wA=makePanel(5,"left","Visuals")
    wA:Toggle("Fullbright",false,function(v) ST.fullbright=v end,nil,{get=function() return ST.fullbright end, legacy="ST_fullbright"})
    wA:Toggle("FOV Changer",false,function(v) ST.fov_changer=v end,nil,{get=function() return ST.fov_changer end, legacy="ST_fov_changer"})
    wA:Slider("FOV",90,30,120,function(v) ST.fov_value=v end,{get=function() return ST.fov_value end, legacy="ST_fov_value"})
    wA:Toggle("Color Mode",false,function(v) ST.world_color=v end,nil,{id="5/left/Visuals/Toggle/World Color",get=function() return ST.world_color end, legacy="ST_world_color"})
    do
        local function component(key)
            return math.clamp(math.floor(ST.world_color_val[key]*255+0.5),0,255)
        end
        local function setComponent(key,value)
            local c=ST.world_color_val
            ST.world_color_val=Color3.fromRGB(
                key=="R" and value or math.floor(c.R*255+0.5),
                key=="G" and value or math.floor(c.G*255+0.5),
                key=="B" and value or math.floor(c.B*255+0.5))
        end
        for _,entry in ipairs({{"Red","R"},{"Green","G"},{"Blue","B"}}) do
            local key=entry[2]
            wA:Slider("Color "..entry[1],255,0,255,function(v) setComponent(key,v) end,
                {get=function() return component(key) end})
        end
    end
    wA:Toggle("World Transparency",false,function(v) ST.char_transparency=v end,nil,{id="5/left/Visuals/Toggle/Transparency",get=function() return ST.char_transparency end, legacy="ST_char_transparency"})
    wA:Slider("Transparency Amount",0.3,0,1,function(v) ST.char_trans_val=v end,{id="5/left/Visuals/Slider/Transp Val",get=function() return ST.char_trans_val end, legacy="ST_char_trans_val"})
    local wB=makePanel(5,"right","Skybox")

        SHF.selectBaseSky(v)

    -- Extra local-only post-processing visuals
    local localFx = {}
    local function setLocalFx(key, className, enabled, configure)
        local effect = localFx[key]
        if enabled then
            if not effect or not effect.Parent then
                effect = Instance.new(className)
                effect.Name = "DaDa_Local_" .. key
                effect.Parent = Lighting
                localFx[key] = effect
            end
            if configure then pcall(configure, effect) end
            effect.Enabled = true
        elseif effect then
            pcall(function() effect.Enabled = false end)
        end
    end
    wC:Toggle("Color Correction",false,function(v)
        SHF.local_color_correction=v
        setLocalFx("ColorCorrection", "ColorCorrectionEffect", v, function(e)
            e.Brightness=0.02; e.Contrast=0.08; e.Saturation=0.12
            e.TintColor=Color3.fromRGB(235,225,255)
        end)
    end,nil,{get=function() return SHF.local_color_correction end, legacy="SHF_local_color_correction"})
    local wD=makePanel(5,"right","Skybox / FX")
    wD:Toggle("Premium Skybox",false,function(v)
        SHF.setPremiumSkybox(v)
    end,"prem_skybox",{get=function() return SHF.prem_skybox end, legacy="SHF_prem_skybox"})
    wD:Dropdown("Premium Preset",{"Jungle","Blossom","RedNight","Purple","Foggy"},"Jungle",function(v)
        SHF.prem_skybox_name=v
        if SHF.refreshSkybox then SHF.refreshSkybox() end
    end,{get=function() return SHF.prem_skybox_name end, legacy="SHF_prem_skybox_name"})
    wD:Toggle("World FX",false,function(v) SHF.world_fx=v end,nil,{get=function() return SHF.world_fx end, legacy="SHF_world_fx"})
    wD:Dropdown("World FX Type",{"Snow","Sakura"},"Snow",function(v) SHF.world_fx_type=v end,{get=function() return SHF.world_fx_type end, legacy="SHF_world_fx_type"})

    wD:Toggle("Aura",false,function(v)
        SHF.aura=v
        if v and SHF.applyAura then SHF.applyAura()
        elseif SHF.clearAura then SHF.clearAura() end
    end,nil,{get=function() return SHF.aura end, legacy="SHF_aura"})
    wD:Dropdown("Aura Type",{"angel","wind","starlight","heavenly","aurora","flame","frost","rose","void","emerald","solar","lunar","ocean","toxic","sakura","plasma","storm","ember","crystal","rainbow","shadow","mint","coral","gold","sapphire","amethyst","pearl","cinder","breeze","comet","stardust","aurum","glacier","dream"},"angel",function(v)
        SHF.aura_type=v
        if SHF.aura and SHF.applyAura then SHF.applyAura() end
    end,{get=function() return SHF.aura_type end, legacy="SHF_aura_type"})

    local wE=makePanel(5,"left","Aura v2")
    wE:Toggle("Aura v2",false,function(v)
        SHF.aura2 = v
        if type(SHF.aura2Toggle) == "function" then
            local ok,err = pcall(SHF.aura2Toggle, v)
            if not ok then warn("[DaDa] Aura v2 toggle: " .. tostring(err)) end
        elseif not v and type(SHF.clearAura2) == "function" then
            pcall(SHF.clearAura2)
        end
    end,{get=function() return SHF.aura2 end, legacy="SHF_aura2"})
    wE:Dropdown("Type",{"angel","starlight","heavenly","ribbon","sakura","wind","flow","star"},"angel",function(v)
        if SHF.aura2Type then SHF.aura2Type(v) end
    end,{get=function() return SHF.aura2_type end, legacy="SHF_aura2_type"})
    wE:Dropdown("Color",visualColors.names,"Default",function(v)
        if SHF.aura2Color then SHF.aura2Color(visualColors.values[v] or Color3.fromRGB(133,220,255)) end
    end,{get=function() return uiConfig.colorName(SHF.aura2_color,visualColors.values,"Default") end,legacy="SHF_aura2_color",fromLegacy=function(value) assert(typeof(value)=="Color3","expected Color3"); return uiConfig.colorName(value,visualColors.values,"Default") end})

    -- Native full-width MM2 Skinchanger (external mesh catalogue).
    do
        local skinPanel=makePanel(10,"left","Skinchanger")
        mountDaDaSkinChanger(skinPanel,pal)
    end

    -- Gun Beam: actual GunFired events only. Hold 2.5s, fade for 1s, then destroy.
    local beamEnabled=false
    local beamColor=Color3.fromRGB(190,90,255)
    local beamStyle="Neon"
    local shotHookConn
    local activeShotBeams={}
    local beamFadeConn
    local function destroyShotBeam(shot)
        activeShotBeams[shot]=nil
        if shot.startPart then pcall(function() shot.startPart:Destroy() end) end
        if shot.endPart then pcall(function() shot.endPart:Destroy() end) end
    end
    local function clearGunBeam()
        if shotHookConn then shotHookConn:Disconnect(); shotHookConn=nil end
        if beamFadeConn then beamFadeConn:Disconnect(); beamFadeConn=nil end
        for shot in pairs(activeShotBeams) do destroyShotBeam(shot) end
    end
    local function updateShotBeams()
        local now=os.clock()
        for shot in pairs(activeShotBeams) do
            local age=now-shot.createdAt
            if not shot.beam.Parent or not shot.startPart.Parent or not shot.endPart.Parent then
                destroyShotBeam(shot)
            elseif age>=3.5 then
                shot.beam.Transparency=NumberSequence.new(1)
                destroyShotBeam(shot)
            elseif age>2.5 then
                local alpha=math.clamp((age-2.5)/1,0,1)
                local eased=alpha*alpha*(3-2*alpha)
                shot.beam.Transparency=NumberSequence.new(0.08+0.92*eased)
            end
        end
        if not next(activeShotBeams) and beamFadeConn then
            beamFadeConn:Disconnect()
            beamFadeConn=nil
        end
    end
    table.insert(espLifecycle.cleanups,function()
        beamEnabled=false
        clearGunBeam()
    end)
    local function createShotBeam(posA,posB)
        if espLifecycle.stopped or not beamEnabled or typeof(posA)~="Vector3" or typeof(posB)~="Vector3" then return end
        local shot={createdAt=os.clock()}
        local ok=pcall(function()
            local startPart=Instance.new("Part")
            shot.startPart=startPart
            startPart.Name="DaDaGunBeamShotStart"
            startPart.Anchored=true
            startPart.CanCollide=false
            startPart.CanTouch=false
            startPart.CanQuery=false
            startPart.Transparency=1
            startPart.Size=Vector3.new(0.05,0.05,0.05)
            startPart.Position=posA
            startPart.Parent=workspace

            local endPart=Instance.new("Part")
            shot.endPart=endPart
            endPart.Name="DaDaGunBeamShotEnd"
            endPart.Anchored=true
            endPart.CanCollide=false
            endPart.CanTouch=false
            endPart.CanQuery=false
            endPart.Transparency=1
            endPart.Size=Vector3.new(0.05,0.05,0.05)
            endPart.Position=posB
            endPart.Parent=workspace

            local a0=Instance.new("Attachment")
            a0.Name="DaDaGunBeamShotStartAttachment"
            a0.Parent=startPart
            local a1=Instance.new("Attachment")
            a1.Name="DaDaGunBeamShotEndAttachment"
            a1.Parent=endPart
            local beam=Instance.new("Beam")
            beam.Name="DaDaGunBeamShot"
            beam.Attachment0=a0
            beam.Attachment1=a1
            beam.Color=ColorSequence.new(beamColor)
            beam.Width0=0.08
            beam.Width1=0.025
            beam.FaceCamera=true
            beam.LightEmission=1
            beam.TextureSpeed=2
            beam.Transparency=NumberSequence.new(0.08)
            if beamStyle=="Neon" then
                beam.LightInfluence=0
            elseif beamStyle=="Thin" then
                beam.Width0=0.025; beam.Width1=0.01
            elseif beamStyle=="Pulse" then
                beam.Width0=0.12; beam.Width1=0.04
                beam.Texture="rbxasset://textures/particles/sparkles_main.dds"
            end
            beam.Parent=startPart
            shot.beam=beam
        end)
        if not ok then destroyShotBeam(shot); return end
        activeShotBeams[shot]=true
        if not beamFadeConn then beamFadeConn=RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(updateShotBeams) end
    end
    local function setupGunBeam()
        if shotHookConn then shotHookConn:Disconnect(); shotHookConn=nil end
        if not beamEnabled then return end
        local ok,remote=pcall(function()
            return ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("ClientServices"):WaitForChild("WeaponService"):WaitForChild("GunFired")
        end)
        if not ok or not remote or espLifecycle.stopped or not beamEnabled then return end
        if shotHookConn then shotHookConn:Disconnect(); shotHookConn=nil end
        shotHookConn=remote.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(gun,posA,posB)
            if not beamEnabled then return end
            createShotBeam(posA,posB)
        end)
    end
    vB:Toggle("Gun Beam",false,function(v)
        beamEnabled=v
        if v then setupGunBeam() else clearGunBeam() end
    end,"DaDaGunBeam",{get=function() return beamEnabled end})
    vB:Dropdown("Beam Style",{"Neon","Thin","Pulse"},"Neon",function(v)
        beamStyle=v
    end,{get=function() return beamStyle end})
    vB:Dropdown("Beam Color",{"Purple","Red","Blue","Green","White","Gold"},"Purple",function(v)
        local colors={Purple=Color3.fromRGB(190,90,255),Red=Color3.fromRGB(255,65,80),Blue=Color3.fromRGB(70,155,255),Green=Color3.fromRGB(80,255,140),White=Color3.fromRGB(245,245,255),Gold=Color3.fromRGB(255,190,65)}
        beamColor=colors[v] or colors.Purple
    end,{get=function() return uiConfig.colorName(beamColor,uiConfig.overlayColors,"Purple") end})

    do
        -- Event-driven timer. Duration is configurable; GunFired does not supply it.
        local panel=makePanel(4,"left","Gun Cooldown")
        local settings={enabled=false,onlyMine=false,scale=1,duration=3}
        local startedAt=nil
        local shotRemote,shotConnection=nil,nil
        local nextRemoteCheck=0
        local hud=nil
        local hudScale,percent,track,fill,lowerTrack,lowerFill
        local lastAccent=nil
        local function rounded(object,radius)
            local corner=Instance.new("UICorner")
            corner.CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,radius)
            corner.Parent=object
        end
        local function ensureHud()
            if hud then return end
            hud=Instance.new("ScreenGui")
            hud.Name="DaDaGunCooldownHUD"
            hud.ResetOnSpawn=false
            hud.IgnoreGuiInset=false
            hud.DisplayOrder=35
            hud.ZIndexBehavior=Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
            hud.Enabled=false
            local window=Instance.new("Frame")
            window.Name="Cooldown"
            window.AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5,1)
            window.Position=UDim2.new(0.5,0,1,-36)
            window.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(184,64)
            window.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0)
            window.BackgroundTransparency=0
            window.BorderSizePixel=0
            window.Parent=hud
            rounded(window,10)
            hudScale=Instance.new("UIScale")
            hudScale.Scale=settings.scale
            hudScale.Parent=window
            percent=Instance.new("TextLabel")
            percent.Name="Percent"
            percent.BackgroundTransparency=1
            percent.Size=UDim2.new(1,-24,0,24)
            percent.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(12,5)
            percent.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold
            percent.TextSize=15
            percent.Text="100%"
            percent.Parent=window
            local function bar(name,y)
                local background=Instance.new("Frame")
                background.Name=name
                background.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(12,y)
                background.Size=UDim2.new(1,-24,0,6)
                background.BorderSizePixel=0
                background.ClipsDescendants=true
                background.Parent=window
                rounded(background,3)
                local value=Instance.new("Frame")
                value.Name="Fill"
                value.Size=UDim2.fromScale(1,1)
                value.BorderSizePixel=0
                value.Parent=background
                rounded(value,3)
                return background,value
            end
            track,fill=bar("Progress",34)
            track.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(5,5,5)
            lowerTrack,lowerFill=bar("DarkProgress",46)
            -- Use the hub's existing GUI parent (including gethui environments).
            hud.Parent=gui.Parent
        end
        local function progress(now)
            if not startedAt then return 1 end
            local value=math.clamp((now-startedAt)/settings.duration,0,1)
            if value>=1 then startedAt=nil end
            return value
        end
        local function draw(now)
            if not hud then return end
            local value=progress(now)
            percent.Text=tostring(math.floor(value*100+0.000001)).."%"
            fill.Size=UDim2.fromScale(value,1)
            lowerFill.Size=UDim2.fromScale(value,1)
            fill.Visible=value>0
            lowerFill.Visible=value>0
            local color=menuLibrary:GetAccent()
            if color~=lastAccent then
                lastAccent=color
                percent.TextColor3=color
                fill.BackgroundColor3=color
                lowerTrack.BackgroundColor3=Color3.new(color.R*0.07,color.G*0.07,color.B*0.07)
                lowerFill.BackgroundColor3=Color3.new(color.R*0.22,color.G*0.22,color.B*0.22)
            end
        end
        local function disconnectShot()
            if shotConnection then shotConnection:Disconnect(); shotConnection=nil end
            shotRemote=nil
        end
        local function gunFired()
            if espLifecycle.stopped or not settings.enabled then return end
            -- Deliberately follows the requested inventory/hand rule, not guesses
            -- about optional shooter arguments in different GunFired versions.
            if settings.onlyMine and not lp_has_gun() then return end
            startedAt=os.clock()
            draw(startedAt)
        end
        local function ensureShotHook()
            local services=ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("ClientServices")
            local weapons=services and services:FindFirstChild("WeaponService")
            local remote=weapons and weapons:FindFirstChild("GunFired")
            if remote and not remote:IsA("RemoteEvent") then remote=nil end
            if remote==shotRemote and shotConnection and shotConnection.Connected then return end
            disconnectShot()
            if remote then
                shotRemote=remote
                shotConnection=remote.OnClientEvent:Connect(gunFired)
            end
        end
        panel:Toggle("Gun Cooldown",false,function(value)
            settings.enabled=value
            startedAt=nil
            if value then
                ensureHud()
                hud.Enabled=true
                ensureShotHook()
                nextRemoteCheck=0
                draw(os.clock())
            else
                if hud then hud.Enabled=false end
                disconnectShot()
            end
        end,nil,{get=function() return settings.enabled end})
        panel:Toggle("Only My Cooldown",false,function(value)
            settings.onlyMine=value
        end,nil,{get=function() return settings.onlyMine end})
        panel:Slider("Size %",100,50,200,function(value)
            settings.scale=value/100
            if hudScale then hudScale.Scale=settings.scale end
        end,{step=5,get=function() return settings.scale*100 end})
        panel:Slider("Cooldown Seconds",3,0.1,10,function(value)
            local now=os.clock()
            local current=progress(now)
            settings.duration=value
            if startedAt then startedAt=now-current*value end
            if settings.enabled then draw(now) end
        end,{step=0.05,get=function() return settings.duration end})
        table.insert(espLifecycle.connections,RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
            if not settings.enabled or espLifecycle.stopped then return end
            local now=os.clock()
            if now>=nextRemoteCheck then
                nextRemoteCheck=now+0.5
                ensureShotHook()
            end
            draw(now)
        end))
        table.insert(espLifecycle.cleanups,function()
            settings.enabled=false
            startedAt=nil
            disconnectShot()
            if hud then hud:Destroy(); hud=nil end
        end)
    end

    -- EXTRAS
    do
        -- The six-part model from dih.txt; geometry stays client-side.
        local panel=makePanel(6,"left","Troll")
        local settings={enabled=false,size=1,collision=false}
        local active=nil
        local function clearModel()
            local state=active
            active=nil
            if not state then return end
            for _,connection in ipairs(state.connections) do connection:Disconnect() end
            if state.model then state.model:Destroy() end
        end
        local function forgetBodyPart(state,bodyPart)
            local constraints=state.ignored[bodyPart]
            if not constraints then return end
            state.ignored[bodyPart]=nil
            for _,constraint in ipairs(constraints) do constraint:Destroy() end
        end
        local function ignoreBodyPart(state,bodyPart)
            if not settings.collision or not bodyPart:IsA("BasePart") or state.ignored[bodyPart] then return end
            local constraints={}
            state.ignored[bodyPart]=constraints
            for _,part in ipairs(state.parts) do
                local constraint=Instance.new("NoCollisionConstraint")
                constraint.Name="IgnoreOwner"
                constraint.Part0=part
                constraint.Part1=bodyPart
                constraint.Parent=state.constraints
                constraints[#constraints+1]=constraint
            end
        end
        local function syncCollision(state)
            -- Disable first; establish owner exclusions before enabling physics.
            for _,part in ipairs(state.parts) do part.CanCollide=false end
            if settings.collision then
                for _,bodyPart in ipairs(state.character:GetDescendants()) do
                    ignoreBodyPart(state,bodyPart)
                end
            else
                state.constraints:ClearAllChildren()
                table.clear(state.ignored)
            end
            for _,part in ipairs(state.parts) do part.CanCollide=settings.collision end
        end
        local function smoothDirection(previous,target,alpha,forward)
            local direction=previous:Lerp(target,alpha)
            if direction.Magnitude<0.0001 then return forward end
            direction=direction.Unit
            -- A sharp turn must not bend the model back through the owner.
            if direction:Dot(forward)<0.25 then return forward end
            return direction
        end
        local function poseModel(state,dt,reset)
            local torso=state.torso
            local scale=settings.size
            local frame=torso.CFrame
            local forward=frame.LookVector
            if reset or dt>0.2 or (torso.Position-state.lastPosition).Magnitude>12 then
                state.dir1,state.dir2,state.dir3=forward,forward,forward
            else
                local frames=math.clamp(dt,0,0.1)*60
                state.dir1=smoothDirection(state.dir1,forward,1-0.65^frames,forward)
                state.dir2=smoothDirection(state.dir2,state.dir1,1-0.75^frames,forward)
                state.dir3=smoothDirection(state.dir3,state.dir2,1-0.82^frames,forward)
            end
            state.lastPosition=torso.Position
            local radius=0.75*scale
            -- The back surface, not the center, is mounted to the torso.
            -- Large sizes expand forward; small sizes move inward automatically.
            local mountZ=-torso.Size.Z*0.5-radius+math.min(0.03,0.06*scale)
            local mountY=-torso.Size.Y*0.4
            -- Keep both mounting points on the torso even at the largest size.
            local lateral=math.min(0.32*scale,math.max(0,torso.Size.X*0.5-0.05))
            state.parts[1].Size=Vector3.new(1.5,1.5,1.5)*scale
            state.parts[2].Size=Vector3.new(1.5,1.5,1.5)*scale
            state.parts[1].CFrame=frame*CFrame.new(-lateral,mountY,mountZ)
            state.parts[2].CFrame=frame*CFrame.new(lateral,mountY,mountZ)
            local p0=(frame*CFrame.new(0,mountY+0.9*scale,mountZ)).Position
            local segmentLength=1.7*scale
            local p1=p0+state.dir1*segmentLength
            local p2=p1+state.dir2*segmentLength
            local p3=p2+state.dir3*segmentLength
            local function cylinder(part,a,b)
                part.Size=Vector3.new((b-a).Magnitude,0.9*scale,0.9*scale)
                part.CFrame=CFrame.new((a+b)*0.5,b)*CFrame.Angles(0,math.pi*0.5,0)
            end
            cylinder(state.parts[3],p0,p1)
            cylinder(state.parts[4],p1,p2)
            cylinder(state.parts[5],p2,p3)
            state.parts[6].Size=Vector3.new(1.3,1.3,1.3)*scale
            state.parts[6].CFrame=CFrame.new(p3)
        end
        local function buildModel(character,torso,humanoid)
            local model=Instance.new("Model")
            model.Name="DaDa_Dih"
            local constraints=Instance.new("Folder")
            constraints.Name="OwnerCollisionExclusions"
            constraints.Parent=model
            local state={character=character,torso=torso,humanoid=humanoid,model=model,
                constraints=constraints,ignored={},parts={},connections={},
                dir1=torso.CFrame.LookVector,dir2=torso.CFrame.LookVector,
                dir3=torso.CFrame.LookVector,lastPosition=torso.Position}
            active=state
            local function part(name,shape,color)
                local object=Instance.new("Part")
                object.Name=name
                object.Shape=shape
                object.Anchored=true
                object.Massless=true
                object.CanCollide=false
                object.CanTouch=false
                object.CanQuery=false
                object.Material=Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
                object.Color=color
                object.Parent=model
                state.parts[#state.parts+1]=object
            end
            local white=Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
            part("Base1",Enum.PartType.Ball,white)
            part("Base2",Enum.PartType.Ball,white)
            part("Segment1",Enum.PartType.Cylinder,white)
            part("Segment2",Enum.PartType.Cylinder,white)
            part("Segment3",Enum.PartType.Cylinder,white)
            part("End",Enum.PartType.Ball,Color3.fromRGB(255,210,225))
            poseModel(state,0,true)
            syncCollision(state)
            -- A workspace container avoids the hub's own-character Noclip,
            -- Chams, avatar cloning and tool-visual passes changing this model.
            model.Parent=workspace
            state.connections[#state.connections+1]=character.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(object)
                if active==state then ignoreBodyPart(state,object) end
            end)
            state.connections[#state.connections+1]=character.DescendantRemoving:Connect(function(object)
                if active==state then forgetBodyPart(state,object) end
            end)
            state.connections[#state.connections+1]=humanoid.Died:Connect(function()
                if active==state then clearModel() end
            end)
        end
        local function updateModel(dt)
            if espLifecycle.stopped or not settings.enabled then
                if active then clearModel() end
                return
            end
            local character=LP.Character
            local humanoid=character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local torso=character and (character:FindFirstChild("LowerTorso") or character:FindFirstChild("Torso"))
            if not character or not character.Parent or not humanoid or humanoid.Health<=0
                or not torso or not torso:IsA("BasePart") then
                if active then clearModel() end
                return
            end
            if active and (active.character~=character or active.torso~=torso
                or active.humanoid~=humanoid or active.model.Parent~=workspace) then
                clearModel()
            end
            if not active then buildModel(character,torso,humanoid) end
            poseModel(active,dt,false)
        end
        panel:Toggle("Dih",false,function(value)
            settings.enabled=value
            if value then updateModel(0) else clearModel() end
        end,nil,{get=function() return settings.enabled end})
        panel:Slider("Dih Size",1,0.1,5,function(value)
            settings.size=value
            if active then poseModel(active,0,true) end
        end,{step=0.05,get=function() return settings.size end})
        panel:Toggle("Dih Collision",false,function(value)
            settings.collision=value
            if active then syncCollision(active) end
        end,nil,{get=function() return settings.collision end})
        panel:Label("Client-side model / collision")
        -- Update before physics, not after it, so collision follows the owner.
        table.insert(espLifecycle.connections,RunService.PreSimulation:Connect(updateModel))
        table.insert(espLifecycle.connections,LP.CharacterRemoving:Connect(function(character)
            if active and active.character==character then clearModel() end
        end))
        table.insert(espLifecycle.cleanups,function()
            settings.enabled=false
            clearModel()
        end)
    end

    local eA=makePanel(6,"left","Visuals Extras")
    eA:Toggle("Crosshair",false,function(v) SHF.crosshair=v end,nil,{get=function() return SHF.crosshair end, legacy="SHF_crosshair"})
    eA:Slider("Crosshair Gap",4,0,20,function(v) SHF.crosshair_gap=v end,{get=function() return SHF.crosshair_gap end,legacy="SHF_crosshair_gap"})
    eA:Slider("Crosshair Length",8,2,30,function(v) SHF.crosshair_len=v end,{get=function() return SHF.crosshair_len end,legacy="SHF_crosshair_len"})
    eA:Slider("Crosshair Thickness",2,1,6,function(v) SHF.crosshair_thick=v end,{get=function() return SHF.crosshair_thick end,legacy="SHF_crosshair_thick"})
    eA:Slider("Crosshair Rotation",0,0,10,function(v) SHF.crosshair_rot=v end,{get=function() return SHF.crosshair_rot end,legacy="SHF_crosshair_rot"})
    eA:Toggle("China Hat",false,function(v) SHF.china_hat=v end,nil,{get=function() return SHF.china_hat end, legacy="SHF_china_hat"})
    eA:Toggle("Self Chams",false,function(v) SHF.self_chams=v end,nil,{get=function() return SHF.self_chams end, legacy="SHF_self_chams"})
    eA:Dropdown("Self Chams Color",visualColors.names,"Default",function(v)
        SHF.self_chams_col=visualColors.values[v] or Color3.fromRGB(0,200,255)
    end,{get=function() return uiConfig.colorName(SHF.self_chams_col,visualColors.values,"Default") end,legacy="SHF_self_chams_col",fromLegacy=function(value) assert(typeof(value)=="Color3","expected Color3"); return uiConfig.colorName(value,visualColors.values,"Default") end})
    eA:Toggle("Tool Chams",false,function(v) SHF.tool_chams=v end,nil,{get=function() return SHF.tool_chams end, legacy="SHF_tool_chams"})
    eA:Dropdown("Tool Chams Color",visualColors.names,"Default",function(v)
        SHF.tool_chams_col=visualColors.values[v] or Color3.fromRGB(255,200,0)
    end,{get=function() return uiConfig.colorName(SHF.tool_chams_col,visualColors.values,"Default") end,legacy="SHF_tool_chams_col",fromLegacy=function(value) assert(typeof(value)=="Color3","expected Color3"); return uiConfig.colorName(value,visualColors.values,"Default") end})
    eA:Toggle("Landing Circle",false,function(v)
        SHF.landing_circle=v
        if v and SHF.lc_bind then SHF.lc_bind() end
    end,nil,{get=function() return SHF.landing_circle end, legacy="SHF_landing_circle"})
    eA:Toggle("Gun ESP",false,function(v) SHF.gun_esp=v; SHF.gun_esp_text=v; SHF.gun_esp_hl=v end,nil,{get=function() return SHF.gun_esp end, legacy="SHF_gun_esp"})
    -- Avatar cosmetics are client-side only; they do not change the Roblox account avatar.
    local avatarPanel=makePanel(6,"right","Avatar")
    local avatarHighlightEnabled=false
    local avatarHighlightColor=Color3.fromRGB(190,90,255)
    local avatarHighlight
    local function updateAvatarHighlight()
        if avatarHighlight then pcall(function() avatarHighlight:Destroy() end); avatarHighlight=nil end
        local char=LP.Character
        if not avatarHighlightEnabled or not char then return end
        avatarHighlight=Instance.new("Highlight")
        avatarHighlight.Name="DaDaAvatarHighlight"
        avatarHighlight.FillColor=avatarHighlightColor
        avatarHighlight.OutlineColor=avatarHighlightColor
        avatarHighlight.FillTransparency=0.72
        avatarHighlight.OutlineTransparency=0.08
        avatarHighlight.DepthMode=Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
        avatarHighlight.Parent=char
    end
    avatarPanel:Toggle("Avatar Glow",false,function(v)
        avatarHighlightEnabled=v
        updateAvatarHighlight()
        if v then notify("Avatar","Local avatar glow enabled") else notify("Avatar","Avatar glow disabled") end
    end,"DaDaAvatarGlow",{get=function() return avatarHighlightEnabled end})
    avatarPanel:Dropdown("Glow Color",{"Purple","Red","Blue","Green","White","Gold"},"Purple",function(v)
        local colors={Purple=Color3.fromRGB(190,90,255),Red=Color3.fromRGB(255,65,80),Blue=Color3.fromRGB(70,155,255),Green=Color3.fromRGB(80,255,140),White=Color3.fromRGB(245,245,255),Gold=Color3.fromRGB(255,190,65)}
        avatarHighlightColor=colors[v] or colors.Purple
        if avatarHighlightEnabled then updateAvatarHighlight() end
    end,{get=function() return uiConfig.colorName(avatarHighlightColor,uiConfig.overlayColors,"Purple") end})
    LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function() task.wait(0.5); if avatarHighlightEnabled then updateAvatarHighlight() end end)

    -- Trade Helper: alert when a trade-related panel/prompt appears locally.
    local tradePanel=makePanel(6,"right","Trade Helper")
    local tradeHelperEnabled=false
    local lastTradeNotice=0
    local function scanTradeUI()
        if not tradeHelperEnabled then return end
        local pg=LP:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
        if not pg then return end
        for _,obj in ipairs(pg:GetDescendants()) do
            if (obj:IsA("GuiObject") and obj.Visible) or obj:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
                local nm=(tostring(obj.Name).." "..tostring(obj.Parent and obj.Parent.Name or "")):lower()
                if nm:find("trade",1,true) or nm:find("exchange",1,true) then
                    if os.clock()-lastTradeNotice>8 then
                        lastTradeNotice=os.clock()
                        notify("Trade Helper","Trade interface detected")
                    end
                    return
                end
            end
        end
    end
    tradePanel:Toggle("Trade Alerts",false,function(v)
        tradeHelperEnabled=v
        if v then scanTradeUI(); notify("Trade Helper","Alerts enabled") else notify("Trade Helper","Alerts disabled") end
    end,"DaDaTradeHelper",{get=function() return tradeHelperEnabled end})
    tradePanel:Button("Check Trade UI",function()
        local old=tradeHelperEnabled
        tradeHelperEnabled=true
        scanTradeUI()
        tradeHelperEnabled=old
        if os.clock()-lastTradeNotice>1 then notify("Trade Helper","No trade interface detected") end
    end)
    task.spawn(function()
        while gui and gui.Parent do
            if tradeHelperEnabled then pcall(scanTradeUI) end
            task.wait(2)
        end
    end)

    local eB=makePanel(6,"right","Sounds")
    eB:Toggle("Gunshot Sound",false,function(v) SHF.gunshot_sound=v; if v then SHF.snd_preload(SHF.gunshot_sound_name); SHF.silenceOriginalGunshot() end end,nil,{get=function() return SHF.gunshot_sound end, legacy="SHF_gunshot_sound"})
    eB:Dropdown("Gunshot Style",SHF.snd_list or {"mc bow"},SHF.gunshot_sound_name,function(v) SHF.gunshot_sound_name=v; SHF.snd_preload(v) end,{get=function() return SHF.gunshot_sound_name end, legacy="SHF_gunshot_sound_name"})
    eB:Slider("Gunshot Vol",1,0,10,function(v) SHF.gunshot_sound_vol=v end,{get=function() return SHF.gunshot_sound_vol end, legacy="SHF_gunshot_sound_vol"})
    eB:Toggle("Sheriff Kill Sound",false,function(v) SHF.sheriff_sound=v end,nil,{get=function() return SHF.sheriff_sound end, legacy="SHF_sheriff_sound"})
    eB:Dropdown("Sheriff Sound",SHF.snd_list or {"mc bow"},SHF.sheriff_sound_name,function(v) SHF.sheriff_sound_name=v; SHF.snd_preload(v) end,{get=function() return SHF.sheriff_sound_name end, legacy="SHF_sheriff_sound_name"})
    eB:Slider("Sheriff Vol",1,0,10,function(v) SHF.sheriff_sound_vol=v end,{get=function() return SHF.sheriff_sound_vol end, legacy="SHF_sheriff_sound_vol"})
    eB:Toggle("Murder Kill Sound",false,function(v) SHF.kill_sound=v end,nil,{get=function() return SHF.kill_sound end, legacy="SHF_kill_sound"})
    eB:Dropdown("Murder Sound",SHF.snd_list or {"skeet"},SHF.kill_sound_name,function(v) SHF.kill_sound_name=v; SHF.snd_preload(v) end,{get=function() return SHF.kill_sound_name end, legacy="SHF_kill_sound_name"})
    eB:Slider("Murder Vol",1,0,10,function(v) SHF.kill_sound_vol=v end,{get=function() return SHF.kill_sound_vol end, legacy="SHF_kill_sound_vol"})
    do
    -- Double Gun / Double Knife (local visual only).
    -- The equipped tool is mirrored across the torso onto the left hand; every
    -- part of the tool is copied, including CustomGunModel (AWP etc.), and the
    -- copy rebuilds whenever the tool's contents change. The left arm mirrors
    -- the right arm's pose so it is raised and actually holds the copy.
    local dualPanel=makePanel(6,"left","Dual Wield")
    local dualOn={Gun=ST.double_gun,Knife=ST.double_knife}
    local dualState={tool=nil,char=nil,model=nil,pairs={},dirty=true,conns={},motors={}}
    local function mirrorCF(cf,flip)
        -- Reflect across the reference frame's YZ plane, then flip one local
        -- axis back so the result is a proper rotation (Roblox can't reflect meshes).
        local x,y,z,r00,r01,r02,r10,r11,r12,r20,r21,r22=cf:GetComponents()
        r00,r01,r02=-r00,-r01,-r02
        if flip==2 then r01,r11,r21=-r01,-r11,-r21
        elseif flip==3 then r02,r12,r22=-r02,-r12,-r22
        else r00,r10,r20=-r00,-r10,-r20 end
        return CFrame.new(-x,y,z,r00,r01,r02,r10,r11,r12,r20,r21,r22)
    end
    local function lateralAxis(cf)
        local _,_,_,r00,r01,r02=cf:GetComponents()
        local a,b,c=math.abs(r00),math.abs(r01),math.abs(r02)
        if a>=b and a>=c then return 1 elseif b>=c then return 2 end
        return 3
    end
    local function dualRig(char)
        local torso=char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
        if torso then
            return torso,char:FindFirstChild("RightHand"),{
                {char:FindFirstChild("RightUpperArm"),"RightShoulder",char:FindFirstChild("LeftUpperArm"),"LeftShoulder"},
                {char:FindFirstChild("RightLowerArm"),"RightElbow",char:FindFirstChild("LeftLowerArm"),"LeftElbow"},
                {char:FindFirstChild("RightHand"),"RightWrist",char:FindFirstChild("LeftHand"),"LeftWrist"},
            }
        end
        torso=char:FindFirstChild("Torso")
        if torso then
            return torso,char:FindFirstChild("Right Arm"),{{torso,"Right Shoulder",torso,"Left Shoulder"}}
        end
    end
    local function dualClear()
        for _,c in ipairs(dualState.conns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
        dualState.conns={}
        for motor,pose in pairs(dualState.motors) do
            if motor.Parent then pcall(function()
                motor.C0=pose.c0
                motor.Transform=pose.transform
            end) end
        end
        table.clear(dualState.motors)
        if dualState.model then pcall(function() dualState.model:Destroy() end) end
        dualState.model=nil; dualState.pairs={}; dualState.tool=nil; dualState.char=nil
    end
    local DUAL_STRIP={"LuaSourceContainer","JointInstance","Constraint","Sound","TouchTransmitter",
        "ClickDetector","ProximityPrompt","BodyMover","Tool","BasePart","WeldConstraint"}
    local function dualCopy(part)
        local old=part.Archivable
        local ok,copy=pcall(function() part.Archivable=true; return part:Clone() end)
        pcall(function() part.Archivable=old end)
        if not ok or not copy then return nil end
        for _,d in ipairs(copy:GetDescendants()) do
            for _,cls in ipairs(DUAL_STRIP) do
                if d:IsA(cls) then pcall(function() d:Destroy() end); break end
            end
        end
        copy.Anchored=true; copy.CanCollide=false; copy.CanTouch=false
        copy.CanQuery=false; copy.Massless=true
        return copy
    end
    local function dualBuild(char,tool)
        dualClear()
        local torso,hand=dualRig(char)
        if not torso or not hand then return end
        local model=Instance.new("Model"); model.Name="DaDaDualWield"
        for _,part in ipairs(tool:GetDescendants()) do
            if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                local copy=dualCopy(part)
                if copy then
                    copy.Parent=model
                    dualState.pairs[#dualState.pairs+1]={src=part,copy=copy,
                        axis=lateralAxis(hand.CFrame:ToObjectSpace(part.CFrame))}
                end
            end
        end
        model.Parent=char
        dualState.model=model; dualState.tool=tool; dualState.char=char; dualState.dirty=false
        local function mark() dualState.dirty=true end
        dualState.conns[#dualState.conns+1]=tool.DescendantAdded:Connect(mark)
        dualState.conns[#dualState.conns+1]=tool.DescendantRemoving:Connect(mark)
    end
    local function dualTool(char)
        for name,on in pairs(dualOn) do
            if on then
                local t=char:FindFirstChild(name)
                if t and t:IsA("Tool") then return t end
            end
        end
    end
    -- Resolve motors by Part1, not by their storage location. Custom rigs can
    -- keep shoulder motors in the torso instead of inside the upper arm.
    local function findArmMotor(char,part)
        if not part then return nil end
        for _,joint in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
            if joint:IsA("Motor6D") and joint.Part1==part and joint.Part0 then return joint end
        end
    end
    local function rememberMotor(motor)
        if not dualState.motors[motor] then
            dualState.motors[motor]={c0=motor.C0,transform=motor.Transform}
        end
        return dualState.motors[motor]
    end
    local function dualPose(render)
        local char=dualState.char
        if not char or char~=LP.Character or not dualState.tool or dualState.tool.Parent~=char then return end
        local torso=char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or char:FindFirstChild("Torso")
        if not torso then return end
        local r15=char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")~=nil
        local names=r15 and {{"RightUpperArm","LeftUpperArm"},{"RightLowerArm","LeftLowerArm"},{"RightHand","LeftHand"}}
            or {{"Right Arm","Left Arm"}}
        local rightUpper=char:FindFirstChild(names[1][1])
        if not rightUpper then return end
        -- If the equipped animation does not lift even the right arm, enforce
        -- the standard forward tool-hold pose rather than mirroring a hanging arm.
        local extension=-rightUpper.CFrame.UpVector
        local holding=extension:Dot(torso.CFrame.LookVector)>0.25
        local targets={}
        for _,pair in ipairs(names) do
            local right=char:FindFirstChild(pair[1])
            local left=char:FindFirstChild(pair[2])
            local motor=findArmMotor(char,left)
            if right and left and motor then
                local original=rememberMotor(motor)
                local parentCF=targets[motor.Part0] or motor.Part0.CFrame
                local target
                if holding then
                    target=torso.CFrame*mirrorCF(torso.CFrame:ToObjectSpace(right.CFrame),1)
                else
                    local shoulder=parentCF:PointToWorldSpace(original.c0.Position)
                    local rotation=torso.CFrame.Rotation*CFrame.Angles(math.pi/2,0,0)
                    target=CFrame.new(shoulder)*rotation*CFrame.new(-motor.C1.Position)
                end
                targets[left]=target
                motor.C0=parentCF:ToObjectSpace(target)*motor.C1
                motor.Transform=CFrame.identity
                -- A final visual pass after Animator removes the one-frame lag
                -- and prevents cached Transform writes from leaving the arm down.
                if render then left.CFrame=target end
            end
        end
    end
    local function dualUpdate(render)
        local char=LP.Character
        local tool=char and dualTool(char)
        if not tool then
            if dualState.model or next(dualState.motors) then dualClear() end
            return
        end
        if tool~=dualState.tool or char~=dualState.char or dualState.dirty
            or not dualState.model or not dualState.model.Parent then dualBuild(char,tool) end
        dualPose(render)
        local _,rightHand=dualRig(char)
        local leftHand=char:FindFirstChild("LeftHand") or char:FindFirstChild("Left Arm")
        if not rightHand or not leftHand then return end
        for _,pair in ipairs(dualState.pairs) do
            local source,copy=pair.src,pair.copy
            if source.Parent then
                copy.CFrame=leftHand.CFrame*mirrorCF(rightHand.CFrame:ToObjectSpace(source.CFrame),pair.axis)
                copy.Transparency=source.Transparency
                copy.LocalTransparencyModifier=source.LocalTransparencyModifier
                if copy.Size~=source.Size then copy.Size=source.Size end
            else dualState.dirty=true end
        end
    end
    local dualArmConn=RunService.PreSimulation:Connect(function() dualUpdate(false) end)
    local dualRenderName="DaDaDualWieldPose"
    RunService:BindToRenderStep(dualRenderName,Enum.RenderPriority.Last.Value-1,function() dualUpdate(true) end)
    table.insert(espLifecycle.connections,dualArmConn)
    table.insert(espLifecycle.cleanups,function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(dualRenderName) end)
    table.insert(espLifecycle.cleanups,dualClear)
    dualPanel:Toggle("Double Gun",false,function(v)
        dualOn.Gun=v; ST.double_gun=v; dualState.dirty=true
        if not v and dualState.tool and dualState.tool.Name=="Gun" then dualClear() end
    end,"DaDaDoubleGun",{get=function() return dualOn.Gun end,legacy="ST_double_gun"})
    dualPanel:Toggle("Double Knife",false,function(v)
        dualOn.Knife=v; ST.double_knife=v; dualState.dirty=true
        if not v and dualState.tool and dualState.tool.Name=="Knife" then dualClear() end
    end,"DaDaDoubleKnife",{get=function() return dualOn.Knife end,legacy="ST_double_knife"})
    SHF.setDualWield=function(gun,knife)
        dualOn.Gun=gun==true; dualOn.Knife=knife==true
        ST.double_gun=dualOn.Gun; ST.double_knife=dualOn.Knife
        dualState.dirty=true
        local current=dualState.tool
        if current and not dualOn[current.Name] then dualClear() end
    end
    dualPanel:Label("Local visual only. Copies custom gun models too")
    end
    local eD=makePanel(6,"right","Skins / Anims")
    eD:Dropdown("Gun Skin",{"None","Default","Golden","Rainbow","Neon","Dark","Blood"},"None",function(v) SHF.gun_skin=v end,{get=function() return SHF.gun_skin end, legacy="SHF_gun_skin"})
    eD:Dropdown("Knife Skin",{"None","Default","Golden","Neon","Blood","Shadow"},"None",function(v) SHF.knife_skin=v end,{get=function() return SHF.knife_skin end, legacy="SHF_knife_skin"})
    eD:Dropdown("Anim Pack",{"Default","Ninja","Zombie","Pirate","Robot"},"Default",function(v)
        SHF.anim_pack=v
        if SHF.applyAnim then SHF.applyAnim(v) end
    end,{get=function() return SHF.anim_pack end, legacy="SHF_anim_pack"})
    eD:Toggle("Round Notify",false,function(v) SHF.round_notify=v end,nil,{get=function() return SHF.round_notify end, legacy="SHF_round_notify"})
    eD:Toggle("Killer Notify",false,function(v) SHF.killer_notify=v end,nil,{get=function() return SHF.killer_notify end, legacy="SHF_killer_notify"})

    -- Bundle / asset thumbnail types and catalogues.
    -- The gallery is native to DaDa's existing panels, with explicit pagination.
    local function makeAnimationGallery(panel,thumb,callback,isSelected)
        local state={items={},filtered={},page=1,pageSize=9,selected=nil,status="loading..."}
        local root=Instance.new("Frame")
        root.Name="PagedLibrary"; root.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,360)
        root.BackgroundTransparency=1; panel:Mount(root,"Catalogue "..thumb)
        local search=Instance.new("TextBox")
        search.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,28); search.PlaceholderText="Search..."
        search.Text=""; search.ClearTextOnFocus=false; search.Font=FONT_MEDIUM
        search.TextSize=12; search.TextColor3=pal.text; search.PlaceholderColor3=pal.muted
        search.BackgroundColor3=pal.control; search.BorderSizePixel=0; search.Parent=root
        Instance.new("UICorner",search).CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,8)
        local grid=Instance.new("Frame")
        grid.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(0,34); grid.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,282)
        grid.BackgroundTransparency=1; grid.Parent=root
        local layout=Instance.new("UIGridLayout")
        layout.CellSize=UDim2.new(1/3,-4,0,90); layout.CellPadding=UDim2.fromOffset(6,6)
        layout.SortOrder=Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder; layout.Parent=grid
        local message=Instance.new("TextLabel")
        message.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(0,34); message.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,282)
        message.BackgroundTransparency=1; message.TextColor3=pal.muted; message.TextWrapped=true
        message.Font=FONT_MEDIUM; message.TextSize=12; message.Parent=root
        local footer=Instance.new("TextLabel")
        footer.Position=UDim2.new(0,42,0,324); footer.Size=UDim2.new(1,-84,0,28)
        footer.BackgroundTransparency=1; footer.TextColor3=pal.text
        footer.Font=FONT_MEDIUM; footer.TextSize=11; footer.Parent=root
        local function nav(text,x)
            local b=Instance.new("TextButton")
            b.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(36,28); b.Position=x; b.Text=text
            b.Font=FONT_BOLD; b.TextSize=16; b.TextColor3=pal.text
            b.BackgroundColor3=pal.control; b.BorderSizePixel=0; b.Parent=root
            Instance.new("UICorner",b).CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,7)
            return b
        end
        local prev=nav("‹",UDim2.new(0,0,0,324))
        local nextButton=nav("›",UDim2.new(1,-36,0,324))
        local render
        render=function()
            if espLifecycle.stopped or not root.Parent then return end
            for _,child in ipairs(grid:GetChildren()) do
                if child:IsA("GuiObject") then child:Destroy() end
            end
            local pages=math.max(1,math.ceil(#state.filtered/state.pageSize))
            state.page=math.clamp(state.page,1,pages)
            footer.Text=string.format("%d / %d · %d",state.page,pages,#state.filtered)
            prev.Active=state.page>1; nextButton.Active=state.page<pages
            prev.TextTransparency=state.page>1 and 0 or 0.65
            nextButton.TextTransparency=state.page<pages and 0 or 0.65
            message.Visible=#state.filtered==0
            message.Text=#state.items==0 and state.status or "No matches"
            local first=(state.page-1)*state.pageSize+1
            for i=first,math.min(first+state.pageSize-1,#state.filtered) do
                local item=state.filtered[i]
                local button=Instance.new("TextButton")
                button.Name="Asset_"..tostring(item.id); button.Text=""
                button.BackgroundColor3=pal.control; button.BorderSizePixel=0
                button.LayoutOrder=i; button.Parent=grid
                Instance.new("UICorner",button).CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,8)
                local chosen
                if isSelected then chosen=isSelected(item.name) else chosen=state.selected==item.name end
                if chosen then
                    local stroke=Instance.new("UIStroke",button)
                    stroke.Color=pal.blue; stroke.Thickness=2
                end
                local image=Instance.new("ImageLabel")
                image.Size=UDim2.new(1,-8,0,58); image.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(4,2)
                image.BackgroundTransparency=1; image.ScaleType=Enum.ScaleType.Fit
                image.Image="rbxthumb://type="..thumb.."&id="..tostring(item.id).."&w=150&h=150"
                image.Parent=button
                local label=Instance.new("TextLabel")
                label.Position=UDim2.new(0,3,0,60); label.Size=UDim2.new(1,-6,0,28)
                label.BackgroundTransparency=1; label.Text=item.name; label.TextWrapped=true
                label.Font=FONT_MEDIUM; label.TextSize=10; label.TextColor3=pal.text
                label.TextTruncate=Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd; label.Parent=button
                button.Activated:Connect(function()
                    if espLifecycle.stopped then return end
                    local ok,err=pcall(callback,item.name)
                    if ok then state.selected=item.name; render()
                    elseif notify then pcall(notify,"Library",tostring(err)) end
                end)
            end
        end
        local function filter()
            state.filtered={}
            local query=string.lower(search.Text)
            for _,item in ipairs(state.items) do
                if query=="" or string.find(string.lower(item.name),query,1,true) then
                    state.filtered[#state.filtered+1]=item
                end
            end
            state.page=1; render()
        end
        search:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(filter)
        prev.Activated:Connect(function() state.page=state.page-1; render() end)
        nextButton.Activated:Connect(function() state.page=state.page+1; render() end)
        local api={}
        function api:SetData(items,status)
            state.items={}
            for _,item in ipairs(items or {}) do state.items[#state.items+1]=item end
            table.sort(state.items,function(a,b) return string.lower(a.name)<string.lower(b.name) end)
            state.status=status or "Catalogue unavailable. Press Reload Catalogue."
            filter()
        end
        function api:Refresh() render() end
        function api:SetSelection(name) state.selected=name; render() end
        function api:ClearSelection() state.selected=nil; render() end
        render()
        return api
    end
    local eE=makePanel(6,"right","Animations")
    eE:Dropdown("Category",{"all","idle","walk","run","jump","fall","climb","swim","swimidle"},"all",function(v)
        getgenv().DADA_ANIM_CAT=v
        if SHF.animGallery then SHF.animGallery:Refresh() end
    end,{get=function() return getgenv().DADA_ANIM_CAT or "all" end})
    SHF.animGallery=makeAnimationGallery(eE,"BundleThumbnail",function(name)
        local fn=getgenv().DADA_ANIM_APPLY
        if not fn then error("Animation engine not ready") end
        fn(name)
    end,function(name)
        local sel=SHF.animSelections or {}
        local category=getgenv().DADA_ANIM_CAT or "all"
        if category~="all" then return sel[category]==name end
        for _,key in ipairs({"idle","walk","run","jump","fall","climb","swim","swimidle"}) do
            if sel[key]~=name then return false end
        end
        return true
    end)
    eE:Button("Reset Animations",function()
        if SHF.animReset then SHF.animReset() end
        SHF.animGallery:ClearSelection()
    end)
    eE:Button("Reload Catalogue",function() if SHF.reloadAnimations then SHF.reloadAnimations() end end)
    local eF=makePanel(6,"left","Emotes")
    SHF.emoteGallery=makeAnimationGallery(eF,"Asset",function(name)
        local fn=getgenv().DADA_EMOTE_PLAY
        if not fn then error("Emote engine not ready") end
        if fn(name)==false then error("Emote asset is not in the catalogue") end
    end,function(name)
        return SHF.emoteSelection and SHF.emoteSelection.name==name
    end)
    eF:Button("Stop Emote",function()
        if SHF.emoteStop then SHF.emoteStop() end
        SHF.emoteGallery:ClearSelection()
    end)
    eF:Button("Reload Catalogue",function() if SHF.reloadEmotes then SHF.reloadEmotes() end end)

    -- CONFIG
    local cfgA=makePanel(7,"left","Config")
    cfgA:Dropdown("Theme",{"Halloween","Default","White","Blue","Pink","Purple","Green"},"Halloween",function(v)
        applyTheme(v)
        notify("Theme","Changed to "..v)
    end,{get=function() for name,theme in pairs(Themes) do if theme==currentTheme then return name end end end})
    cfgA:Toggle("Hot Keys",false,function(v) ST.quick=v end,nil,{id="7/left/Config/Toggle/Quick Menu",get=function() return ST.quick end, legacy="ST_quick"})
    cfgA:Toggle("Lock Hot Keys",false,function(v) ST.hotkeys_locked=v end,nil,
        {id="custom/hotkeys/locked",get=function() return ST.hotkeys_locked end,legacy="ST_hotkeys_locked"})
    cfgA:Slider("Hot Keys Size",100,60,180,function(v) ST.hotkeys_scale=v/100 end,
        {id="custom/hotkeys/size",get=function() return ST.hotkeys_scale*100 end,step=1})
    cfgA:Button("Reset Hot Keys Position",function() ST.hotkeys_x=18; ST.quick_offset_y=180 end)
    cfgA:Button("Unload",function() if getgenv().DADA_UNLOAD then getgenv().DADA_UNLOAD() end end)
    local cfgB=makePanel(7,"right","World / Misc")
    cfgB:Toggle("Fake Position",false,function(v) SHF.fake_pos=v end,nil,{get=function() return SHF.fake_pos end, legacy="SHF_fake_pos"})
    cfgB:Toggle("Movement Graph",false,function(v) SHF.mov_graph=v end,nil,{get=function() return SHF.mov_graph end, legacy="SHF_mov_graph"})
    cfgB:Button("Version: v16.3")
    cfgB:Button("Notify Test",function() notify("DaDa v16.3","Test") end)

    -- MAP PRIORITY
    local cfgC=makePanel(7,"left","Map Priority")
    local mapList={}
    local mapDropdownBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
    mapDropdownBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,20)
    mapDropdownBtn.BackgroundColor3 = pal.control
    mapDropdownBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
    mapDropdownBtn.Text = "Priority: ALL (random)"
    mapDropdownBtn.TextColor3 = pal.text
    mapDropdownBtn.TextSize = 10
    mapDropdownBtn.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    cfgC:Mount(mapDropdownBtn,"Map priority")
    Instance.new("UICorner", mapDropdownBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,4)
    local function refreshMapList()
        mapList = {}
        for _,e in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
            if e.Name == "MapName" and e:IsA("TextLabel") and e.Text ~= "" and e.Text ~= "MAP NAME" then
                local dup = false
                for _,m in ipairs(mapList) do if m == e.Text then dup = true; break end end
                if not dup then mapList[#mapList+1] = e.Text end
            end
        end
        table.sort(mapList)
        if #mapList == 0 then
            mapDropdownBtn.Text = "Priority: ALL (no maps yet)"
        end
    end
    local mapIdx = 0
    mapDropdownBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        refreshMapList()
        if #mapList == 0 then
            mapDropdownBtn.Text = "Priority: ALL (no maps yet)"
            return
        end
        mapIdx = mapIdx % (#mapList + 1)
        if mapIdx == 0 then
            mapDropdownBtn.Text = "Priority: ALL (random)"
            getgenv().DaDa_MapPriority = nil
            getgenv().DaDa_SelectedMapName = nil
            getgenv().DaDa_SelectedVoteModel = nil
        else
            local currentMap = mapList[mapIdx]
            mapDropdownBtn.Text = "Priority: "..currentMap
            getgenv().DaDa_MapPriority = currentMap
            getgenv().DaDa_SelectedMapName = currentMap
            getgenv().DaDa_SelectedVoteModel = nil
        end
        mapIdx = (mapIdx + 1) % (#mapList + 1)
    end)
    cfgC:Button("Refresh Map List", function()
        refreshMapList()
