        if espLifecycle.stopped then return false end
        local ok,errors,applied=restore(defaults)
        if not ok and (applied or 0)==0 then reportErrors(errors); return false end
        if notify then notify("Config","Настройки сброшены") end
        return true
    end
    getgenv().DaDa_Config=CFG
    table.insert(espLifecycle.cleanups,function()
        if getgenv().DaDa_Config==CFG then getgenv().DaDa_Config=nil end
    end)
    task.delay(3.5,function()
        -- An old instance must not auto-load into a re-executed script.
        if espLifecycle.stopped or getgenv().DaDa_Config~=CFG or manuallyUsed then return end
        local last=CFG.last()
        if last then CFG.load(last) end
    end)
end
initConfigs()

-- ============================================================
-- INIT 23: DOUBLE JUMP (по твоему коду)
-- ============================================================
do
    local UIS = game:GetService("UserInputService")
    local Players = game:GetService("Players")

    local player = Players.LocalPlayer
    local character
    local humanoid

    local enabled = false
    local jumps = 0
    local maxJumps = 2
    local jumpTick = tick()

    local function onCharacterAdded(newCharacter)
        character = newCharacter
        humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")

        jumps = 0
        jumpTick = tick()

        humanoid.StateChanged:Connect(function(_, new)
            if new == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Landed
            or new == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running
            or new == Enum.HumanoidStateType.RunningNoPhysics then
                jumps = 0
            end
        end)
    end

    UIS.JumpRequest:Connect(function()
        if not enabled then return end
        if not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 then return end
        if jumps < maxJumps and tick() - jumpTick > 0.2 then
            jumpTick = tick()
            jumps += 1
            humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
        end
    end)

    player.CharacterAdded:Connect(onCharacterAdded)
    if player.Character then
        onCharacterAdded(player.Character)
    end

    getgenv().DADA_DOUBLEJUMP_TOGGLE = function(v)
        enabled = v and true or false
        ST.double_jump = enabled
        if not enabled then jumps = 0 end
    end
end

-- ============================================================
-- INIT 24: ANIMATIONS + EMOTES
-- ============================================================
do
    local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")

    -- ==========================================================
    -- ANIMATIONS ENGINE
    -- ==========================================================
    do
        local cats = {
            {"Idle","idle"},{"Walk","walk"},{"Run","run"},{"Jump","jump"},
            {"Fall","fall"},{"Climb","climb"},{"Swim","swim"},{"Swim Idle","swimidle"}
        }
        local animItems, byName, mapCache, sel, origMap = {}, {}, {}, {}, {}
        SHF.animSelections=sel
        local enabled, animConn = false, nil
        local fetched, pendingApply, applyToken = false, false, 0

        local function getParts()
            local char = LP.Character
            if not char then return end
            return char, char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid"), char:FindFirstChild("Animate")
        end

        local function stopTracks(hum)
            if not hum then return end
            for _,track in pairs(hum:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
                -- Downloading a movement bundle may finish AFTER the config's
                -- Action-priority emote starts. Never stop that restored emote.
                local priority=track.Priority
                if priority==Enum.AnimationPriority.Core or priority==Enum.AnimationPriority.Idle
                    or priority==Enum.AnimationPriority.Movement then
                    pcall(function() track:Stop() end)
                end
            end
        end

        local function refreshAnimate(animate, hum)
            if animate and hum and not animate.Disabled then
                animate.Disabled = true
                animate.Disabled = false
                hum:Move(hum.MoveDirection, false)
            end
        end

        local function resolveMappings(data)
            local key = tostring(data.id)
            if mapCache[key] then return mapCache[key] end
            local bundled = data.bundledItems
            if type(bundled) ~= "table" then return nil end
            local mappings = {}
            for _, ids in pairs(bundled) do
                if type(ids) == "table" then
                    for _, assetId in pairs(ids) do
                        local ok, objs = pcall(game.GetObjects, game, "rbxassetid://"..assetId)
                        if ok and objs then
                            local known={idle=true,walk=true,run=true,jump=true,fall=true,climb=true,swim=true,swimidle=true}
                            local function scan(object, category)
                                local name=string.lower(object.Name)
                                if known[name] then category=name end
                                if object:IsA("Animation") and object.AnimationId~="" then
                                    mappings[#mappings+1]={category=category,name=object.Name,id=object.AnimationId}
                                end
                                for _,child in ipairs(object:GetChildren()) do scan(child,category) end
                            end
                            for _, o in ipairs(objs) do
                                scan(o,nil)
                                pcall(function() o:Destroy() end)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            mapCache[key] = mappings
            return mappings
        end

        local function catItems(data, folderName)
            local mappings = resolveMappings(data)
            if not mappings then return nil end
            local items = {}
            for _, m in ipairs(mappings) do
                if m.category and m.category:lower() == folderName then
                    items[m.name:lower()] = m.id
                end
            end
            return items
        end

        local function applyCat(animate, folderName, data)
            if not (animate and data) then return end
            local items = catItems(data, folderName)
            if not (items and next(items)) then return end
            local folder = animate:FindFirstChild(folderName)
            if not folder then
                for _,candidate in ipairs(animate:GetChildren()) do
                    if string.lower(candidate.Name)==folderName then folder=candidate; break end
                end
            end
            if not folder then return end
            local _, first = next(items)
            for _, a in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
                if a:IsA("Animation") then
                    local id = items[a.Name:lower()] or first
                    if id then
                        if origMap[a] == nil then origMap[a] = a.AnimationId end
                        a.AnimationId = id
                        a.Parent = nil
                        a.Parent = folder
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        local function applyAll(animate)
            for _, c in ipairs(cats) do
                local name = sel[c[2]]
                if name and name ~= "none" and byName[name] then
                    applyCat(animate, c[2], byName[name])
                end
            end
        end

        local function restore()
            for a, id in pairs(origMap) do
                if a and a.Parent then
                    pcall(function()
                        local folder = a.Parent
                        a.AnimationId = id
                        a.Parent = nil
                        a.Parent = folder
                    end)
                end
            end
            origMap = {}
        end

        local function doApply()
            applyToken = applyToken + 1
            local token = applyToken
            task.spawn(function()
                local char,hum,animate=getParts()
                local deadline=os.clock()+15
                -- A config may load while the CURRENT character is still being
                -- assembled, without another CharacterAdded event to retry it.
                while (not char or not hum or not animate) and os.clock()<deadline do
                    if token~=applyToken or espLifecycle.stopped or not enabled then return end
                    task.wait(0.2)
                    char,hum,animate=getParts()
                end
                if not char or not hum or not animate or token~=applyToken
                    or espLifecycle.stopped or not enabled then return end
                -- Resolve bundled assets before mutating this character; GetObjects may yield.
                for _, category in ipairs(cats) do
                    local selected = sel[category[2]]
                    if selected and byName[selected] then resolveMappings(byName[selected]) end
                end
                if LP.Character ~= char or token ~= applyToken or espLifecycle.stopped
                    or char:FindFirstChild("Animate") ~= animate or not enabled then return end
                stopTracks(hum)
                restore()
                applyAll(animate)
                if token == applyToken then refreshAnimate(animate, hum) end
            end)
        end

        local function requestApply()
            if not enabled then return end
            if not fetched then
                pendingApply = true
                return
            end
            doApply()
        end

        local function fetchAll()
            local urls = {
                "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/7yd7/sniper-Emote/refs/heads/test/AnimationSniper.json",
                "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/7yd7/sniper-Emote/refs/heads/test/AnimationSniperoffsale.json"
            }
            local seen = {}
            for _, url in ipairs(urls) do
                local ok, res = pcall(function()
                    local c = game:HttpGet(url)
                    return c ~= "" and HttpService:JSONDecode(c) or nil
                end)
                if ok and type(res) == "table" then
                    local list = res.data or res
                    for _, item in pairs(list) do
                        local id = tonumber(item.id)
                        if id and id > 0 and item.bundledItems and not seen[id] then
                            seen[id] = true
                            local nm = tostring(item.name or ("Animation_"..id))
                            if byName[nm] then nm = nm.." ["..id.."]" end
                            byName[nm] = {id = id, bundledItems = item.bundledItems}
                            animItems[#animItems+1] = {name = nm, id = id}
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        local function bind_respawn()
            if animConn then return end
            animConn = LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(c)
                applyToken = applyToken + 1
                origMap = {}
                -- Bounded retries also catch animation folders populated after Animate itself.
                task.spawn(function()
                    local deadline = os.clock() + 15
                    local lastAnimate, lastCount = nil, -1
                    while enabled and not espLifecycle.stopped and LP.Character == c
                        and c.Parent and os.clock() < deadline do
                        local hum = c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                        local animate = c:FindFirstChild("Animate")
                        if hum and animate then
                            local count = 0
                            for _, item in ipairs(animate:GetDescendants()) do
                                if item:IsA("Animation") then count = count + 1 end
                            end
                            if animate ~= lastAnimate or count ~= lastCount then
                                lastAnimate, lastCount = animate, count
                                requestApply()
                            end
                        end
                        task.wait(0.4)
                    end
                end)
            end)
        end

        local function drop_respawn()
            if animConn then pcall(function() animConn:Disconnect() end) animConn = nil end
        end

        local function wipe_anims()
            local _, hum, animate = getParts()
            stopTracks(hum)
            restore()
            refreshAnimate(animate, hum)
        end

        SHF.animApply = function(name, catKey)
            catKey=catKey or "all"
            if name and not byName[name] then return end
            if name and SHF.anim_pack ~= "Default" and SHF.applyAnim then
                SHF.applyAnim("Default")
            end
            if catKey == "all" then
                for _, c in ipairs(cats) do
                    if name then sel[c[2]] = name else sel[c[2]] = nil end
                end
            else
                if name then sel[catKey] = name else sel[catKey] = nil end
            end
            if SHF.animGallery then SHF.animGallery:Refresh() end
            enabled = false
            for _, v in pairs(sel) do
                if v then enabled = true; break end
            end
            if enabled then
                bind_respawn()
                requestApply()
            else
                pendingApply = false
                applyToken = applyToken + 1
                drop_respawn()
                wipe_anims()
            end
        end
        SHF.animReset = function()
            for k in pairs(sel) do sel[k] = nil end
            SHF.animApply(nil, "all")
        end

        table.insert(espLifecycle.cleanups, function()
            enabled = false
            pendingApply = false
            applyToken = applyToken + 1
            drop_respawn()
            restore()
        end)

        getgenv().DADA_ANIM_APPLY = function(name)
            local cat = getgenv().DADA_ANIM_CAT or "all"
            SHF.animApply(name, cat)
        end
        getgenv().DADA_ANIM_RESET = function()
            SHF.animReset()
        end

        -- Configs contain stable asset data, not only a gallery highlight. Saved
        -- bundles remain usable when catalogue HTTP requests finish late/fail.
        local restoredBundles={}
        SHF.animExportConfig=function()
            local result={}
            for _,category in ipairs(cats) do
                local key=category[2]
                local name=sel[key]
                if name then
                    local data=byName[name] or restoredBundles[name]
                    result[key]={name=name,id=data and data.id or nil,
                        bundledItems=data and data.bundledItems or nil}
                end
            end
            return result
        end
        SHF.animRestoreConfig=function(records)
            table.clear(sel)
            table.clear(restoredBundles)
            for _,category in ipairs(cats) do
                local key=category[2]
                local record=records[key]
                if record then
                    sel[key]=record.name
                    if record.id and record.bundledItems then
                        local bundle={id=record.id,bundledItems=record.bundledItems}
                        restoredBundles[record.name]=bundle
                        if not byName[record.name] then byName[record.name]=bundle end
                    end
                end
            end
            enabled=next(sel)~=nil
            if enabled then
                if SHF.anim_pack~="Default" and SHF.applyAnim then SHF.applyAnim("Default") end
                bind_respawn()
                requestApply()
            else
                pendingApply=false; applyToken=applyToken+1
                drop_respawn(); wipe_anims()
            end
            if SHF.animGallery then SHF.animGallery:Refresh() end
        end

        local fetching=false
        SHF.reloadAnimations=function()
            if fetching or espLifecycle.stopped then return end
            fetching=true
            fetched=false
            if SHF.animGallery then SHF.animGallery:SetData({},"Loading animation bundles...") end
            task.spawn(function()
                local oldItems,oldByName=animItems,byName
                animItems,byName={},{}
                local ok,err=pcall(fetchAll)
                if #animItems==0 then animItems,byName=oldItems,oldByName end
                -- HTTP completion must not erase bundles imported by a config.
                for name,bundle in pairs(restoredBundles) do
                    if not byName[name] then byName[name]=bundle end
                end
                fetching=false
                fetched=true
                if espLifecycle.stopped then return end
                if SHF.animGallery then SHF.animGallery:SetData(animItems) end
                if enabled then pendingApply=false; requestApply() end
                if not ok then warn("[DaDa Animations] "..tostring(err)) end
                if notify then pcall(notify,"Animations","Загружено "..#animItems.." паков") end
            end)
        end
        SHF.reloadAnimations()
    end

    -- ==========================================================
    -- EMOTES ENGINE
    -- ==========================================================
    do
        local emoteFile = "emotes.json"
        local statEmotes = {
            {"Griddy", "129149402922241"}
        }
        local custEmotes, emoteMap, emoteList, animCache = {}, {}, {}, {}
        local allList, allMap = {}, {}
        local curTrack, selId, custRaw, alive = nil, nil, nil, true
        SHF.emoteSelection={name="",id=""}
        local playToken=0

        local function parseCustom()
            if not (isfile and isfile(emoteFile)) then
                if custRaw ~= nil then custRaw, custEmotes = nil, {} return true end
                return false
            end
            local ok, raw = pcall(readfile, emoteFile)
            if not ok or raw == custRaw then return false end
            custRaw, custEmotes = raw, {}
            local dok, data = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(raw) end)
            if dok and type(data) == "table" then
                for k, v in pairs(data) do
                    local name, id
                    if type(v) == "table" then
                        name, id = tostring(v.name or v[1] or k), tostring(v.id or v[2] or "")
                    else
                        name, id = tostring(k), tostring(v)
                    end
                    if name ~= "" and id ~= "" then
                        custEmotes[#custEmotes+1] = {name, id}
                    end
                end
            end
            return true
        end

        local function buildList()
            emoteMap, emoteList = {}, {}
            local function add(name, id)
                name = tostring(name)
                if emoteMap[name] then name = name.." ["..tostring(id).."]" end
                emoteMap[name] = tostring(id)
                emoteList[#emoteList+1] = {name = name, id = tonumber((tostring(id):gsub("%D", ""))) or id}
            end
            for _, e in ipairs(statEmotes) do add(e[1], e[2]) end
            for _, e in ipairs(custEmotes) do add(e[1], e[2]) end
            for _, e in ipairs(allList) do add(e.name, e.id) end
            if not espLifecycle.stopped and SHF.emoteGallery then SHF.emoteGallery:SetData(emoteList) end
        end

        parseCustom()
        buildList()

        local function getHum()
            local char = LP.Character
            return char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        end

        local function stopEmote()
            playToken=playToken+1
            if curTrack then
                pcall(function() curTrack:Stop(0.1); curTrack:Destroy() end)
                curTrack = nil
            end
        end

        local function resolveId(id)
            if animCache[id] then return animCache[id] end
            if id:find("://") then animCache[id] = id return id end
            local raw = id:gsub("%D", "")
            local ok, objs = pcall(game.GetObjects, game, "rbxassetid://"..raw)
            if ok and type(objs) == "table" then
                local found
                local function scan(inst)
                    if found then return end
                    if inst:IsA("Animation") and inst.AnimationId ~= "" then
                        found = inst.AnimationId
                        return
                    end
                    for _, c in ipairs(inst:GetChildren()) do scan(c) end
                end
                for _, o in ipairs(objs) do
                    scan(o)
                    pcall(function() o:Destroy() end)
                end
                if found then animCache[id] = found return found end
            end
            local url = "rbxassetid://"..raw
            animCache[id] = url
            return url
        end

        local function playEmote()
            local hum=getHum()
            if not hum or hum.Health<=0 or not selId or not alive then return end
            stopEmote()
            local token=playToken
            local requested=selId
            task.spawn(function()
                local resolved=resolveId(requested)
                if not alive or espLifecycle.stopped or token~=playToken
                    or getHum()~=hum or selId~=requested or hum.Health<=0 then return end
                local animator=hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
                if not animator then
                    if notify then pcall(notify,"Emotes","Animator not found on this character") end
                    return
                end
                local anim=Instance.new("Animation")
                anim.AnimationId=resolved
                local ok,track=pcall(function() return animator:LoadAnimation(anim) end)
                anim:Destroy()
                if ok and track then
                    if token~=playToken or not alive or getHum()~=hum then track:Destroy(); return end
                    track.Priority=Enum.AnimationPriority.Action
                    track.Looped=true
                    track:Play(0.1)
                    curTrack=track
                else
                    warn("[DaDa Emotes] "..tostring(track))
                    if notify then pcall(notify,"Emotes","Animation could not be loaded; check asset access and rig") end
                end
            end)
        end

        local function fetchEmotes()
            local ok, res = pcall(function()
                local c = game:HttpGet("https://raw.githubusercontent.com/7yd7/sniper-Emote/refs/heads/test/EmoteSniper.json")
                return c ~= "" and HttpService:JSONDecode(c) or nil
            end)
            if ok and type(res) == "table" then
                local list = res.data or res
                local seen = {}
                for _, item in pairs(list) do
                    local id = tonumber(item.id)
                    if id and id > 0 and not seen[id] then
                        seen[id] = true
                        local nm = tostring(item.name or ("Emote_"..id))
                        if allMap[nm] then nm = nm.." ["..id.."]" end
                        allMap[nm] = tostring(id)
                        allList[#allList+1] = {name = nm, id = id}
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        SHF.emotePlay = function(name)
            if type(name)~="string" or name=="" then
                selId=nil; SHF.emoteSelection={name="",id=""}; stopEmote()
                if SHF.emoteGallery then SHF.emoteGallery:ClearSelection() end
                return true
            end
            local id=emoteMap[name]
            if not id then return false end
            selId=tostring(id)
            SHF.emoteSelection={name=name,id=selId}
            if SHF.emoteGallery then SHF.emoteGallery:SetSelection(name) end
            playEmote()
            return true
        end
        SHF.emoteStop = function()
            selId=nil; SHF.emoteSelection={name="",id=""}
            stopEmote()
            if SHF.emoteGallery then SHF.emoteGallery:ClearSelection() end
        end
        SHF.emoteExportConfig=function()
            return {name=SHF.emoteSelection.name,id=SHF.emoteSelection.id}
        end
        SHF.emoteRestoreConfig=function(record)
            if record.name=="" or record.id=="" then SHF.emoteStop(); return end
            SHF.emoteSelection={name=record.name,id=tostring(record.id)}
            selId=tostring(record.id)
            if SHF.emoteGallery then SHF.emoteGallery:SetSelection(record.name) end
            playEmote()
        end


        getgenv().DADA_EMOTE_PLAY = SHF.emotePlay
        getgenv().DADA_EMOTE_STOP = SHF.emoteStop

        local char_conn = LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
            stopEmote()
            task.spawn(function()
                local hum=char:WaitForChild("Humanoid",10)
                if hum then hum:WaitForChild("Animator",10) end
                if alive and not espLifecycle.stopped and LP.Character==char and selId then playEmote() end
            end)
        end)

        local fetching=false
        SHF.reloadEmotes=function()
            if fetching or not alive or espLifecycle.stopped then return end
            fetching=true
            task.spawn(function()
                local oldList,oldMap=allList,allMap
                allList,allMap={},{}
                local ok,err=pcall(fetchEmotes)
                if #allList==0 then allList,allMap=oldList,oldMap end
                fetching=false
                if not alive or espLifecycle.stopped then return end
                buildList()
                if not ok then warn("[DaDa Emotes] "..tostring(err)) end
                if notify then pcall(notify,"Emotes","Загружено "..#emoteList.." эмоций") end
            end)
        end
        SHF.reloadEmotes()

        task.spawn(function()
            while alive and task.wait(3) do
                if parseCustom() then
                    buildList()
                end
            end
        end)

        SHF.emoteUnload = function()
            alive = false
            selId = nil
            stopEmote()
            if char_conn then pcall(function() char_conn:Disconnect() end) char_conn = nil end
        end
        table.insert(espLifecycle.cleanups,SHF.emoteUnload)
    end

    -- A manual/automatic config load can precede engine initialization.
    local queued=SHF._queuedMediaConfig
    SHF._queuedMediaConfig=nil
    if queued then
        if queued.animations then SHF.animRestoreConfig(queued.animations) end
        if queued.emote then SHF.emoteRestoreConfig(queued.emote) end
    end
    task.wait(1)
    if notify then pcall(notify, "DaDa", "Animations + Emotes загружены") end
end

notify("DaDa v16.3","здраствуй в царство воин")
print("[DaDa v16.3] кароч ку это значит то что скрипт загружен и тд кароч приятной игры")
